Notably, with our training pipeline, RILA lifts the compact Qwen3.5-9B backbone from 40.40% to 57.52%, surpassing far larger one-shot generators, including the 1T-parameter Kimi-K2.6 (55.61%) and the proprietary GPT-5.5 (55.74%).

</details>


### [35] [Evidence-Guided Detection, Localization and Explanation for Text-Centric Image Forensics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02097)
*Peifeng Liu,Bin Li,Qingsong Zhang,Yangxin Yu,Leqing Chen,Xiaoye Qiu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出一个证据引导的检测-定位-推理系统，用于文本中心图像篡改取证，在官方隐藏测试集排名第二。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AIGC 的快速发展降低了基于文本的图像编辑门槛，使图像取证不仅需要判断真伪，还需要定位篡改区域并给出基于证据的解释报告。

Method: 系统由三部分级联组成：图像级检测器提供全局真实性先验；专用定位器提取篡改区域并生成空间 grounding 证据；基于多模态大语言模型的推理器结合检测结果和局部证据生成结构化取证报告。方法还引入迭代式难度感知挖掘提升定位质量，并使用报告-掩码一致性后处理，使报告中的空间依据与预测掩码对齐。

Result: 在 ACM Multimedia 2026 GenText-Forensics Challenge 官方隐藏测试集上，系统最终得分为 0.638，排名第二。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，证据引导的模块化级联系统能够有效结合真实性检测、篡改定位和解释生成，提升文本中心图像篡改取证的综合表现。

Abstract: The rapid progress of AIGC has made text-centric image manipulation increasingly accessible, creating new forensic challenges that require not only authenticity detection but also spatial grounding and evidence-based explanation. This paper presents our solution to the GenText-Forensics Challenge at ACM Multimedia 2026. We propose an evidence-guided detector-localizer-reasoner system, where an image-level detector provides a global authenticity prior, a dedicated localizer extracts tampered regions as spatial grounding evidence, and an MLLM-based reasoner generates structured forensic reports grounded in this expert forensic evidence. These modules are connected through a cascaded evidence flow: the detector gates the subsequent localization and prompting process, the localizer converts tamper responses into grounding boxes, and the reasoner is trained to synthesize the detector decision and localized evidence into the final report. As a key part of our method, we introduce iterative difficulty-aware mining to improve localization quality and apply report-mask consistency post-processing to align report grounding with predicted masks. On the official hidden test set, our system achieves a final score of 0.638 and ranks second in the challenge, validating the effectiveness of the proposed evidence-guided system. The code is available at https://github.com/peifengLiu42/ACMMM26-evidence-guided-detector-localizer-reasoner-system.

</details>


### [36] [Disease Burden over Skin Tone: Decomposing the Dermatology-AI Generalization Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02111)
*Nirajan Kunwor,Sanjaya Poudel,Quoc-Huy Trinh,Jahidul Arafat,Sunil Kumar Gaire*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文分析皮肤科AI跨数据集泛化问题，指出疾病分布偏移比肤色代表性不足对性能下降的影响更大；皮肤科预训练基础模型比癌症专用模型具有更好的可迁移表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有皮肤科AI模型多基于浅肤色、癌症相关图像训练，但实际部署场景中患者可能同时存在肤色差异与疾病谱差异。研究旨在区分模型泛化失败主要源于肤色不足，还是疾病分布变化。

Method: 评估癌症训练基线模型、两个皮肤科基础模型以及一个通用视觉模型作为冻结特征提取器；在疾病匹配且按肤色分层的数据集DDI，以及疾病分布偏移且肤色多样的数据集SCIN上测试；结合平衡准确率、肤色组间差距、无标签表征聚类分析和少样本轻量适配实验。

Result: 癌症基线在迁移到不熟悉临床疾病时平衡准确率从0.62降至0.21；同一疾病内肤色差距较小且不稳定，约为0.10–0.18。癌症专用特征对未知疾病聚类能力弱，kNN纯度仅比随机高0.06；皮肤科预训练特征结构更强，提升约0.23。约每类10个标注样本即可使皮肤科基础模型恢复大部分可达到性能。

Conclusion: 在所评估场景中，疾病分布偏移是皮肤科AI泛化失败的主要因素，而非单纯肤色不足。提升皮肤科AI可靠性应重视更广泛疾病谱的预训练、可迁移表征质量与可复现审计协议。

Abstract: Dermatology artificial intelligence (AI) models are predominantly trained on light-skinned, cancer-focused image collections, yet they are increasingly proposed for deployment in resource-constrained settings where patients differ from training populations along two confounded axes: skin tone and disease distribution. We investigate whether poor generalization is primarily caused by skin-tone underrepresentation or disease-distribution shift. We evaluate a cancer-trained baseline (ResNet-50 fine-tuned on HAM10000 and ISIC 2019), two dermatology foundation models (DermLIP and MONET), and a general-purpose vision model (DINOv3) as frozen feature extractors. Models are evaluated on a tone-stratified disease-matched dataset (Diverse Dermatology Images, DDI) and a disease-shifted tone-diverse dataset (Skin Condition Image Network, SCIN). Our results show that disease-distribution shift contributes more than skin tone in the evaluated settings. The cancer baseline decreases from 0.62 to 0.21 balanced accuracy when transferred to unfamiliar clinical conditions, while the within-disease skin-tone gap is smaller (0.10-0.18) and inconsistent. Label-free representation analysis shows that this failure reflects a representational limitation rather than only missing output labels: cancer-specialized features poorly cluster unfamiliar conditions (kNN purity lift +0.06 over chance), whereas dermatology-pretrained features retain stronger transferable structure (+0.23). Finally, we show that representation quality predicts recoverable performance under lightweight adaptation. Starting from dermatology foundation models, approximately ten labeled examples per clinical category recover most attainable performance. We release the evaluation protocol and code to support reproducible auditing of dermatology AI generalization.

</details>


### [37] [Synergistic Information Disentanglement for Omni-modal Slide Representation Learning in Computational Pathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02118)
*Mingxin Liu,Chengfei Cai,Anwen Lu,Pengbo Xu,Jun Li,Jinze Li,Depin Chen,Jun Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Φ-Omni：一种基于部分信息分解（PID）的全模态自监督病理表征学习框架，通过强调跨模态不可约协同信息而非简单对齐冗余信息，在多个外部数据集和任务上取得更好的少样本性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算病理中需要整合组织学、基因组学和临床报告以学习可迁移的全切片图像表示。现有多模态 SSL 方法通常通过对比学习把异质模态压到统一潜空间，容易导致“模态坍塌”，即保留模态间显性冗余而丢弃各模态独特且具有诊断价值的协同信号。作者认为，最强的任务无关训练信号应来自跨模态协同交互的蒸馏，而不只是共享冗余的对齐。

Method: 提出 Φ-Omni 框架，理论基础为部分信息分解（PID）。核心是 Synergistic Information Bottleneck（SIB）与 ΦID 目标函数：显式抑制边际冗余信息，同时最大化不可约协同信息，从而提取高阶跨模态交互，并用于 WSI/slide 表征预训练。

Result: 在乳腺队列（n=1031）和肺部队列（n=919）上预训练后，模型在五个独立外部数据集、八个任务的少样本设置中优于监督学习和其他自监督学习基线。

Conclusion: 论文表明，在全模态计算病理 SSL 中，建模并蒸馏跨模态协同信息比单纯对齐共享冗余更有效；Φ-Omni 能学习更具泛化性和任务迁移能力的病理切片表示。

Abstract: In computational pathology (CPath), developing omni-modal self-supervised learning (SSL) models that integrate histology, genomics, and clinical reports enables transferable representation learning for whole slide images (WSIs). Existing approaches implicitly force heterogeneous modalities into a uniform latent space by contrastive alignment, causing modality collapse where unique, synergistic diagnostic signals (termed as $\mathrmΦ$) are discarded in favor of trivial redundancy. We hypothesize that the strongest task-agnostic SSL training signal stems from distilling the synergistic interactions over merely aligning shared redundancy. To this end, we introduce \textsc{$\mathrmΦ$-Omni}, a synergistic information disentanglement framework grounded in Partial Information Decomposition (PID) theory for slide representation learning. Unlike standard contrastive approaches, \textsc{$\mathrmΦ$-Omni} employs a Synergistic Information Bottleneck (SIB) regulated by the proposed $\mathrmΦ\text{ID}$ objective, which explicitly suppresses marginal redundancy while maximizing irreducible synergy, thereby distilling high-order cross-modal interactions. Following pretraining on breast ($n$=1031) and lung ($n$=919) cohorts, \textsc{$\mathrmΦ$-Omni} demonstrates superior few-shot performance across five independent external datasets spanning eight tasks compared to supervised and SSL baselines. Source code is available here.

</details>


### [38] [World-Coherent Decoding: Self-Verifying Test-Time Planning for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02159)
*Chuhan Zhang,Seiji Ito,Kenta Hoshino,Satoshi Ikehata,Ikuro Sato*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 World-Coherent-Decoding（WCD），一种无需更新骨干 WAM 的测试时规划方法，通过对多个想象未来进行可靠性排序来提升机器人控制成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 World Action Models 通过随机生成视觉未来并解码动作来控制机器人，但实际效果高度依赖所选未来；仅增加采样数量并不一定可靠，因此需要在执行前判断哪些未来更可信。

Method: WCD 将 WAM 的 rollout 视为可证伪的“未来—动作”假设。在每个决策步，从冻结的 WAM 中采样多个候选，并使用内部生成信号进行排序：基于 flow 的视频 surprisal 衡量视觉合理性，action path effort 衡量动作生成稳定性。执行后利用真实观测审计所选想象，计算想象—现实不匹配，并训练轻量在线预测器，用于之后候选选择。

Result: 在 RoboTwin 2.0 上，WCD 在有限随机场景监督下将 Hard 成功率从 55.80% 提升到 60.90%；在 Horizon-3 任务上提升 16.43 个点，并在真实 Franka 视觉偏移测试中表现出定性鲁棒性。

Conclusion: WCD 表明 WAM 的测试时扩展关键不在于生成更多未来，而在于选择更可靠的未来；利用自验证信号可在不更新骨干模型的情况下提升机器人规划稳定性。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) aim to control robots by stochastically generating visual futures and then decoding actions, but empirical observations indicate that the results can strongly depend on which future is selected. We propose World-Coherent-Decoding (WCD), a self-verifying test-time planning framework that treats WAM rollouts as falsifiable future--action hypotheses. At each decision step, WCD samples multiple candidates from a frozen WAM and ranks them using internal generative signals: flow-based video surprisal for visual plausibility and action path effort for action-generation stability. After execution, the realized observation audits the selected imagination, yielding an imagination--reality mismatch that trains a lightweight online predictor for future candidate selection. Thus, WCD converts delayed self-verification into pre-execution reliability estimation without updating the backbone model. On RoboTwin 2.0, WCD improves Hard success under limited randomized-scene supervision from $55.80\%$ to $60.90\%$, with a $+16.43$ gains on Horizon-3 tasks, and shows qualitative robustness on real Franka visual-shift tests. These results highlight a simple principle: test-time scaling for WAMs depends less on sampling more futures than on selecting reliable ones.

</details>


### [39] [Progressive Pseudo-Label Optimization for Point-Supervised Change Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02171)
*Hailong Ning,Hao Wang,Yimeng Wang,Tao Lei,Renwei Dian,Asoke K. Nandi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向点监督变化检测的两阶段框架，引入 SAM2 先验并通过自训练逐步适配变化检测任务，在多个基准数据集上优于多数弱监督方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 点监督变化检测仅依赖稀疏点标注，虽然显著降低标注成本，但由于空间覆盖不足，生成的伪标签往往不完整且含噪，尤其在目标边界与局部结构一致性方面表现较差。因此需要一种能够利用通用分割先验并逐步提升伪标签质量的方法。

Method: 方法包含两个阶段：第一阶段利用 SAM2 根据双时相图像中的点标注生成目标感知候选掩码，并设计双时相掩码选择策略，将通用分割结果转化为更可靠的变化伪标签；随后使用轻量级 CNN 精炼模块和不确定性感知损失改善边界质量与局部结构一致性。第二阶段构建教师-学生自训练框架，教师模型通过指数滑动平均更新，并周期性刷新伪标签，从而形成伪标签精炼与模型再优化交替进行的闭环优化。

Result: 在 WHU-CD、LEVIR-CD 和 SYSU-CD 三个基准数据集上的实验表明，该方法在多数基准上超过已有弱监督变化检测方法，并且与若干全监督方法相比仍具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，将 SAM2 的通用分割先验引入点监督变化检测，并结合不确定性精炼与教师-学生自训练机制，可以有效缓解稀疏点标注带来的伪标签噪声与不完整问题，提升弱监督变化检测性能。

Abstract: Point-supervised change detection (PS-CD) aims to identify pixel-level changes between bi-temporal images using only sparsely annotated points. Although point annotations substantially reduce labeling costs, their limited spatial coverage often results in incomplete and noisy pseudo-labels. To address this issue, we propose a two-stage framework that introduces SAM2 priors into PS-CD and progressively adapts them to the target task. In Stage I, SAM2 generates object-aware candidate masks from point annotations on the bi-temporal images, and a bi-temporal mask selection strategy is designed to convert generic segmentation responses into more reliable change pseudo-labels. Subsequently, a lightweight CNN refinement module with an uncertainty-aware loss is employed to improve boundary quality and local structural consistency. In Stage II, we construct a teacher-student self-training framework in which the teacher is updated by exponential moving average and periodically refreshes the pseudo-labels. This design establishes a closed-loop optimization process that alternates between pseudo-label refinement and model re-optimization. Experiments on three benchmark datasets, including WHU-CD, LEVIR-CD, and SYSU-CD, demonstrate that the proposed method outperforms previous weakly supervised approaches on most benchmarks and remains competitive with several fully supervised methods.

</details>


### [40] [CC-4DGS: Computational Deformation and Point-Cloud Compression for Storage-Efficient Dynamic Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02184)
*Kyungdae Park,Chae Eun Rhee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CC-4DGS通过计算式变形场和规范点云属性压缩，将动态4D Gaussian Splatting的场景存储降至约20–30 MB，同时保持接近现有先进方法的重建质量与实时渲染能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态4D Gaussian Splatting方法虽然能实现高质量视角合成，但通常依赖大型多分辨率哈希表和高维高斯属性，导致每个场景需要数十到数百MB存储，限制了可扩展部署与实际应用。

Method: 论文提出两个核心模块：其一是计算式变形场CDF，用确定性稠密哈希编码和紧凑神经解码器替代大规模可学习哈希表，按需生成变形特征，将变形相关存储压缩到1–3 MB；其二是规范点云属性压缩CCA，通过条件自编码、选择性量化和残差码本压缩球谐外观项及辅助高斯属性，实现点云属性3–5倍压缩。

Result: 在N3DV和Technicolor Light Field数据集上的实验表明，CC-4DGS在重建精度上接近Swift4D等先进方法，同时总存储量降低到20–30 MB，并在运行时显存与渲染性能之间取得较好折中。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，动态4DGS中的变形建模和规范属性存储均存在显著冗余；通过计算式特征生成与属性压缩，可以在几乎不损失质量的前提下大幅提升存储效率，增强动态场景表示的实用性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Dynamic four-dimensional (4D) Gaussian Splatting has emerged as a powerful explicit representation for high-quality view synthesis, yet existing methods still require tens to hundreds of megabytes per scene due to their heavy reliance on large multi-resolution hash tables and high-dimensional Gaussian attributes. This paper presents CC-4DGS, a storage-efficient and scalable framework that rethinks both deformation modeling and canonical attribute storage. First, we introduce a computational deformation field (CDF) that replaces large multi-resolution learnable hash tables with deterministic dense hash encoding and compact neural decoders, enabling on-the-fly synthesis of deformation features while reducing deformation storage to only 1--3 MB per scene. Second, we propose a compression of canonical point-cloud attributes (CCA) pipeline that compresses high-dimensional spherical harmonic appearance terms and auxiliary Gaussian attributes via conditional autoencoding, selective quantization, and residual codebooks, achieving 3--5$\times$ point-cloud reduction with negligible quality loss. Together, these components yield a unified representation that preserves real-time rendering performance while reducing total storage to 20--30 MB. Extensive experiments across the N3DV and Technicolor Light Field datasets demonstrate that CC-4DGS achieves reconstruction accuracy comparable to state-of-the-art methods such as Swift4D, while offering significantly improved storage efficiency and favorable runtime-memory trade-offs.

</details>


### [41] [Lightweight Adaptation of General-Purpose VLMs for Multispectral and SAR Image Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02187)
*Shanji Liu,Kelu Yao,Junxiao Xue,Chenghui Lv,Xiangyang Miao,Yekai Huang,Yaying Chen,Chao Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需新建遥感基础模型的轻量适配方案：将多光谱与SAR观测渲染为多视图图像输入通用VLM，并通过LoRA微调，使其在土地覆盖识别、洪水验证和遥感图文生成任务上取得改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用视觉语言模型多基于三通道自然图像预训练，难以直接处理多光谱测量和合成孔径雷达（SAR）等遥感传感器数据；传统适配通常需要专用编码器和领域预训练，成本高且限制了强大通用模型检查点的复用。

Method: 利用通用VLM的多图像输入接口，将每个遥感观测渲染为五个光学视图和一个SAR视图，并在提示词中显式命名这些视图；通过LoRA微调语言网络和部分视觉Transformer模块。方法将波段组合、光谱指数与雷达后向散射信息暴露给已有视觉接口。针对土地覆盖识别，引入结构化监督，将预测类别与传感器证据关联；同时构造偏好对，隐藏真实标签但保留支持证据，以鼓励模型给出完整且与观测一致的预测。

Result: 在基于BigEarthNet-v2构建的平衡六类土地覆盖基准上，适配后的Qwen3-VL达到0.8275 micro F1。相同输入与适配协议提升了四种测试VLM架构的表现，并可迁移到Sen1Floods11洪水验证和BigEarthNet.txt图像描述任务。图像移除与错配控制实验表明模型确实利用了输入的传感器观测。

Conclusion: 通用VLM可通过渲染多传感器输入和紧凑LoRA适配，被有效复用于多光谱与SAR遥感任务，而无需训练新的遥感基础模型；该方案具有轻量、可迁移、可复用通用模型能力的优势。

Abstract: General-purpose vision-language models (VLMs) now support strong visual recognition, instruction following, and generation. However, most pretrained visual encoders are built around three-channel natural images and do not directly accommodate observations such as native multispectral measurements or synthetic aperture radar (SAR). Adapting VLMs to these sensors typically requires dedicated encoders and domain pretraining, slowing the reuse of stronger general-purpose checkpoints. We show that the multi-image interface of general-purpose VLMs offers a lightweight alternative. Our protocol renders each observation as five optical views and one SAR view, names them in the prompt, and adapts the language network and selected visual transformer blocks with LoRA. This exposes band composites, spectral indices, and radar backscatter through an existing visual interface. For land-cover recognition, structured supervision couples predicted classes with sensor evidence. We further construct preference pairs in which a true label is omitted while its supporting evidence is retained, encouraging complete predictions that remain consistent with the observations. On a balanced six-class land-cover benchmark derived from BigEarthNet-v2, the adapted Qwen3-VL reaches 0.8275 micro F1. The same input and adaptation protocol improves all four tested VLM architectures and transfers to Sen1Floods11 flood verification and BigEarthNet.txt captioning. Image removal and mismatch controls show that the adapted models use the supplied sensor observations. Together, these results demonstrate that VLMs can be repurposed for multispectral and SAR tasks through rendered inputs and compact LoRA adaptation, without training a new foundation model.

</details>


### [42] [TAME: Temporal-Aware Mixture-of-Experts for Text-Video Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02204)
*Uicheol Jung,Juyoung Hong,Hojung Kwon,Yukyung Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TAME 是一个面向文本-视频检索的 CLIP 扩展框架，通过稀疏 MoE、帧时序 token 和跨时序交互聚合模块增强视频的帧级结构与时序关系建模，在多个基准上优于 CLIP 类基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有将 CLIP 等图文模型直接扩展到视频检索的方法缺乏充分的时序建模能力。视频帧之间存在外观、运动和语义状态的异质性，若将所有帧压缩为单一表示，容易丢失 temporal structure 与 semantic transitions，从而限制文本-视频匹配性能。

Method: 论文提出 Temporal-Aware Mixture-of-Experts for Text-Video Retrieval（TAME）。其一，在 CLIP 的视觉和文本编码器中加入稀疏 Mixture-of-Experts 层，并在视觉分支采用 frame-consistent routing，使专家按帧级视觉模式特化，同时尽量保持原有视觉-语言对齐。其二，引入 Frame-Temporal tokens，用于聚合跨帧全局信息并反馈到各帧，以捕获长程时序依赖且保留局部细节。其三，设计 Cross-Temporal Interaction and Aggregation（CTIA）模块，通过分阶段的时序过滤与融合细化帧级句子-视频相似度。

Result: 在多个标准文本-视频检索基准上，TAME 相比 CLIP-based baselines 获得稳定提升。在 MSR-VTT 上，相比 CLIP4Clip 的 R@1 提升 4.0；在 DiDeMo、MSVD、LSMDC 和 ActivityNet 上也取得一致增益。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，在 CLIP 框架中显式引入帧级专家建模、跨帧全局交互和时序相似度聚合，可以有效弥补图文预训练模型在视频检索中的时序建模不足，提升文本-视频检索效果。

Abstract: Text-Video Retrieval (TVR) retrieves videos that match a natural-language query, but extending image-text models such as CLIP to videos is fundamentally limited by the lack of temporal modeling. Videos exhibit frame-wise heterogeneity in appearance and motion, and compressing all frames into a single representation often obscures temporal structure and semantic transitions. To address this, we propose Temporal-Aware Mixture-of-Experts for Text-Video Retrieval (TAME), a CLIP-based framework that jointly models frame-level structure and temporal relations. First, we integrate sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) layers into both CLIP encoders and apply frame-consistent routing on the vision branch so that experts specialize according to frame-level visual patterns while preserving the original vision-language alignment. Second, we introduce Frame-Temporal (FT) tokens that aggregate global cross-frame information and feed it back to each frame, enabling the visual encoder to capture long-range temporal dependencies without harming local details. Third, we design a Cross-Temporal Interaction and Aggregation (CTIA) module that refines frame-wise sentence-video similarities through staged temporal filtering and fusion. Experiments on standard TVR benchmarks show that TAME consistently improves over CLIP-based baselines. On MSR-VTT, it improves R@1 by 4.0 over CLIP4Clip, and also achieves consistent gains on DiDeMo, MSVD, LSMDC, and ActivityNet. The code is available at https://github.com/sejong-rcv/TAME.

</details>


### [43] [LeakageBench: Document-Level Leakage Risk for Redacting Personally Identifiable Information in Document Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02207)
*Vishnu Prasad Vijaya Kumar,Santhosh Venkatesh,Ivan P. Yamshchikov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 LeakageBench，用于评估文档图像中 PII 脱敏/遮盖的真实泄漏风险；结果显示现有方法即使定位能力提升，页面级泄漏仍然很高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的 PII 脱敏常发生在扫描件、截图和 PDF 渲染图等文档图像上，OCR 错误、版面结构和视觉噪声会直接影响敏感信息是否被真正移除。现有基准多以文本为中心，难以衡量“只要漏掉一个标识符整页就不安全”的文档级风险。

Method: 作者构建了 LeakageBench：包含 500 张文档图像和 11,954 个符合 GDPR 范畴的 PII 标注，覆盖直接标识符、关联键和上下文再识别信息。评测对象包括通用 OCR 流水线、商业及任务适配的 OCR 依赖检测器，以及无需 OCR 的视觉语言模型；指标包括实体级 F1、分组泄漏和文档级泄漏。

Result: Code Interpreter 将 GPT-5.5 的定位 F1 从 0.090 提升到 0.249，但关键的页面级泄漏率仍高达 0.968，说明定位性能改善并未使多数页面达到安全发布标准。

Conclusion: LeakageBench 揭示了文档图像 PII 脱敏的高召回、空间定位需求与现实泄漏风险之间的差距，可作为诊断 OCR 依赖和 OCR-free 方法在图像级脱敏任务中可靠性的基准。

Abstract: Real-world personally identifiable information (PII) redaction often operates on document images---scans, screenshots, and PDF renderings---where OCR errors, layout structure, and visual noise determine whether sensitive information is actually removed. Existing PII benchmarks are mostly text-centric and do not measure document-level redaction risk: a page remains unsafe if even one identifier is missed. We introduce LeakageBench, a challenge set of 500 document images with 11,954 GDPR-aligned PII annotations spanning direct identifiers, linkage keys, and contextual re-identification surfaces. We evaluate generic OCR pipelines, commercial and task-adapted OCR-dependent detectors, and OCR-free vision-language models using entity-level F1, group-wise leakage, and document-level leakage metrics. Code Interpreter raises GPT-5.5 localization F1 from 0.090 to 0.249, but critical page-level leakage remains 0.968. These results show that stronger detection and tool assistance improve localization without making most pages safe for release. LeakageBench provides a diagnostic benchmark for high-recall, spatially grounded PII redaction in document images.

</details>


### [44] [Asymmetric Paired-Annotation Learning for Multi-Structure ULF Pediatric Brain MRI Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02210)
*Ha-Hieu Pham,Dang P. M. Cao,Minh Hoang Pham,Khanh Nguyen Vo Ngoc,Thanh-Huy Nguyen,Ulas Bagci,Huy-Hieu Pham*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出AURA：一种基于nnU-Net的非对称监督策略，用于便携式超低场MRI中的儿童脑结构分割；其将高场来源标注与低场编辑标注视为不同证据来源，并通过可靠性门控融合低场信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 0.064 T超低场MRI有助于扩大儿童神经影像可及性，但图像解剖边界弱、小结构可见性差，且高场参考标注可能存在局部配准误差；同时挑战赛提供的HF与LF两类标注并不等价，需要更合理的监督方式。

Method: 以nnU-Net为基础，训练主要锚定高场来源HF mask作为评分目标，同时引入低场编辑LF mask；LF信息通过有界可靠性门控调节，门控依据包括标签分歧、边界区域、预测不确定性、类别可靠性和训练阶段。

Result: 在16例开发集上，HF监督基线、AURA及其集成模型的Dice分别为0.7984、0.7950和0.7988；集成模型HD95为1.8892，ASSD为0.7855。

Conclusion: AURA为处理非等价多源标注提供了初步可行方案，但当前开发集结果显示相较HF基线提升有限，仍需在隐藏测试集和外部超低场MRI队列上进一步验证泛化能力与实际价值。

Abstract: Portable ultra-low-field (ULF) MRI can expand access to pediatric neuroimaging, but segmentation at 0.064 T remains challenging because anatomical boundaries are weakly delineated, small structures may be only partially visible, and high-field references can be locally misregistered. The LISA 2026 Challenge provides two non-equivalent annotations reflecting different sources of anatomical evidence: a highfield-derived (HF) mask defining the scored target and a low-field-edited (LF) mask aligned with visible ULF anatomy. In this challenge report, we describe AURA, an nnU-Net-based asymmetric supervision strategy that treats these annotations as distinct observations rather than interchangeable ground truths. AURA anchors training to the HF mask and incorporates the LF mask through a bounded reliability gate based on label disagreement, boundaries, predictive uncertainty, class reliability, and training stage. On a 16-case development split, the HF-supervised baseline, AURA, and their ensemble achieved Dice scores of 0.7984, 0.7950, and 0.7988, respectively, while the ensemble achieved an HD95 of 1.8892 and an ASSD of 0.7855. These results provide a preliminary evaluation of AURA within the LISA 2026 Challenge and motivate further assessment on the hidden test set and external ULF cohorts. Our code and pretrained models are available at https://github.com/minhdang050806/ A-nnU-Net-based-asymmetric-supervision-strategy.

</details>


### [45] [FuDU: A Fuzzy Dual-dimensional Uncertainty Framework for Streaming Active Learning in Industrial Defect Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02212)
*Zhaoyang Wang,Haiyong Chen,Binyi Su,Xinwei Lyu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 FuDU，一种面向连续工业媒体流的流式主动学习方法，通过融合图像级与框级不确定性来选择高价值样本，从而提升实时工业缺陷检测系统的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时工业缺陷检测属于高风险质量检测场景，深度学习模型需要在连续数据流中保持可靠性。由于人工标注成本高且不确定样本对模型改进更有价值，论文希望从工业媒体流中主动挖掘不确定样本，以提高检测系统稳定性与泛化能力。

Method: 方法包含三个核心部分：1）在骨干网络上设计基于原型的全局不确定性量化模块 PGUQ，通过正常/缺陷特征原型评估图像级不确定性；2）在检测头中集成双熵缺陷不确定性评估器 DeUE，用于量化检测框级不确定性；3）将不确定性建模为系统误差，并利用模糊推理融合图像级和框级双维不确定性，实现专家知识驱动的自适应采样决策。

Result: 综合实验表明，FuDU 具有较好的效率与灵活性，适用于具有挑战性的工业检测任务，并能支持对高不确定样本的有效筛选。

Conclusion: 该工作说明，将原型级全局不确定性、检测框级局部不确定性与模糊推理机制结合，可以增强流式主动学习在工业缺陷检测中的实用性与可靠性。

Abstract: Ensuring the reliability of deep learning models in real-time industrial defect detection is critical for high-stakes quality inspection. To mine uncertain samples within continuous industrial media streams, thereby enhancing the reliability of the detection system, this paper proposes a streaming active learning method based on the Fuzzy Dual-dimensional Uncertainty (FuDU) framework. Specifically, we first design a Prototype-based Global Uncertainty Quantification (PGUQ) module on the backbone to evaluate image-level uncertainty via normal/defective feature prototypes. A Dual-entropy defect Uncertainty Evaluator (DeUE) is then integrated into the detection head to quantify box-level uncertainty. Finally, by modeling uncertainty as systematic error, we propose a fuzzy dual-dimensional uncertainty-aware strategy that leverages fuzzy inference to fuse dual-dimensional uncertainties, enabling expert knowledge-driven adaptive sampling decisions. Comprehensive experiments demonstrate that FuDU is efficient and flexible, making it well-suited for challenging industrial inspection tasks such as the detection of nuclear fuel rod defects. Our code is publicly available at: https://github.com/wangzhaoyang-508/FuDU.

</details>


### [46] [Signal or Noise? Auditing Rotation-Induced Saliency Drift in Medical and Aerial Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02224)
*Khawaja Murad ul Hassan,Mehran Ebrahimi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 EquiGrad-CAM：一种无需训练的多旋转视图对齐平均方法，用于显著提升 Grad-CAM 等事后显著图在旋转下的一致性，同时保持解释忠实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事后显著图常用于审计视觉模型决策，但在输入旋转且预测不变时热图会漂移；在无标准方向的图像领域，这会削弱显著图作为证据的可信度。论文旨在判断这种漂移是模型真实信号还是 CAM 操作引入的噪声。

Method: 作者逐阶段测量 CAM 算子的旋转等变性，分析通道权重、空间激活张量和分类头池化的作用；通过遮挡实验检验漂移区域的因果重要性。提出 EquiGrad-CAM：对输入生成 T 个旋转视图，分别计算显著图，再逆旋转回统一坐标系并平均；同时提出 PEUM 用于按解释可复现性排序。

Result: 实验显示，通道权重较稳定，ResNet-50 中因 GAP+线性头导致类别梯度场空间常数而完全稳定；主要漂移来自空间激活张量，而分类器池化会丢弃该运动信息。遮挡漂移像素对模型影响小于随机遮挡，说明漂移多为非忠实自由度。EquiGrad-CAM 在 ImageNet-1K 上相较单视图 Grad-CAM 将等变性提升：ResNet-50 +36.0%，VGG-16 +87.5%，ViT-B/16 +247%；还提升 CLIP 零样本解释一致性，并在 PatchCamelyon、RESISC45 上产生更一致解释。

Conclusion: 显著图的旋转漂移主要不是分类决策所依赖的忠实信号，而是 CAM 空间激活中的不稳定成分。通过旋转视图对齐后平均，可在无需重训练的情况下显著提高解释的旋转一致性，并保留或增强解释忠实性。

Abstract: Post-hoc saliency maps such as Grad-CAM are increasingly used to audit why a deployed vision model made a decision, yet the heatmap drifts when the input is rotated, even when the prediction is unchanged. In domains with no canonical orientation, such as histopathology and aerial imagery, this undermines using saliency as evidence. We ask whether that drift is faithful signal or noise introduced by the CAM operator, and answer it by measuring equivariance at every stage of the operator rather than inferring it from the network's output. The instability is not where one would guess: the channel weights are the most rotation-stable stage, and on ResNet-50 exactly stable, because a GAP+linear head makes the class gradient field spatially constant. What moves is the spatial activation tensor, and the classifier's own pooling discards that movement. A causal test confirms the consequence: occluding the pixels whose saliency drifts costs the model less than occluding random pixels, at either orientation. The drift is carried by degrees of freedom the classifier throws away, which is what makes removing it faithful rather than destructive. EquiGrad-CAM is a training-free wrapper that takes T rotated views, inverse-rotates each view's saliency into a common canonical frame, and averages. On the full ImageNet-1K validation set it raises equivariance over single-view Grad-CAM by +36.0% (ResNet-50), +87.5% (VGG-16) and +247% (ViT-B/16); a scale-matched ablation isolates alignment before averaging, not the locus of aggregation, as the driver. It beats rotation-augmented training without retraining, lifts zero-shot CLIP by +145%, and yields rotation-consistent explanations on PatchCamelyon and RESISC45. Its by-product PEUM ranks explanations by how reproducible they are, at no cost beyond the views already taken. Code: https://github.com/Khawaja-Murad/EquiGrad-CAM

</details>


### [47] [InfraPatch: Cross-Task Targeted Grayscale Patch Attacks on Infrared-Adapted Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02233)
*Chengyin Hu,Dingyi Lu,Jiaju Han,Xiang Chen,Weiwen Shi,Jiahuan Long,Yiwei Wei,Jiujiang Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 InfraPatch，一种面向红外视觉语言模型的白盒、逐样本、目标式数字灰度补丁攻击框架；在约 5% 局部面积约束下，可在分类、描述生成和二元问答任务中诱导指定语义，十种红外适配模型上的目标攻击成功率达 86%–100%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 红外视觉语言模型用于低可见度场景的多模态感知，但其面对目标式对抗补丁攻击的鲁棒性尚缺乏系统研究。既有方法多集中于 RGB 模型或单一下游任务，未充分刻画局部扰动是否能在 IR-VLM 中注入预期语义目标。

Method: 作者提出 InfraPatch：在白盒设置下，为每个样本优化单通道灰度小补丁；限制补丁约占局部区域 5%；结合代理模型引导的放置位置搜索与任务自适应语义目标函数，分别针对图像分类、图像描述和二元视觉问答设计目标式攻击。实验使用 DiffV2IR 将固定的 30 类 COCO 子集转换为 300 张合成红外风格图像，并评估十种红外适配模型变体。

Result: InfraPatch 在十种模型变体上取得 86.00% 到 100% 的目标攻击成功率。相较优化随机放置，代理位置搜索在 CLIP 和 BLIP-2 上分别提升 6.67 和 10.33 个百分点；LLaVA-1.5 在两种放置策略下均接近 100% 饱和成功率。补丁面积与目标函数消融显示不同架构和任务形式之间存在明显脆弱性差异。

Conclusion: 小面积灰度补丁能够在受控数字威胁模型下跨多类 IR-VLM 注入指定目标语义，说明红外多模态系统存在显著安全风险，并需要更系统、更强的鲁棒性评估与防御机制。

Abstract: Infrared vision-language models (IR-VLMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for multimodal perception under low-visibility conditions, yet their robustness to targeted adversarial attacks remains poorly understood. Existing adversarial patch methods mainly study RGB-based models or a single downstream task and do not characterize whether localized perturbations can induce an intended semantic target in IR-VLMs. We propose InfraPatch, a white-box, per-instance framework for targeted digital grayscale patch attacks against IR-VLMs. InfraPatch optimizes a compact single-channel patch within an approximately 5% local-area budget, combines proxy-guided placement with task-adaptive semantic objectives, and induces target behaviors in image classification, image captioning, and binary visual question answering. We evaluate ten infrared-adapted model variants on 300 synthetic infrared-style images generated by applying DiffV2IR to a fixed 30-category COCO subset, using clean-conditioned targeted success criteria. InfraPatch achieves targeted attack success rates from 86.00% to 100% across the ten variants. On CLIP and BLIP-2, proxy location search improves success by 6.67 and 10.33 percentage points over optimized random placement, respectively; LLaVA-1.5 remains saturated near 100% under both settings. Patch-area and objective ablations further expose substantial differences in vulnerability across architectures and task formats. These results show that small grayscale patches can inject chosen target semantics across IR-VLM families under a controlled digital threat model, motivating stronger robustness evaluation for infrared multimodal systems.

</details>


### [48] [SAUF-Net: Structure--Appearance Representation Learning with Uncertainty Feedback for Semi-Supervised Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02247)
*Qin Lu,Zheyang Jing,Yujie Yang,Jianwang Li,Chen Yi,Shaofeng Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SAUF-Net 通过结构-外观解耦、外观交换一致性和可靠性图引导的不确定性反馈，提升半监督医学图像分割在少标注场景下的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有半监督医学图像分割方法多依赖预测级一致性来利用未标注数据，但忽视了内部特征表示的可靠性；医学图像中目标结构线索容易与不稳定外观变化纠缠，导致伪标签不可靠和训练误差累积。

Method: 提出 SAUF-Net：使用结构-外观分解模块将瓶颈特征拆分为结构表示与外观表示；通过解耦引导模块将两类表示注入解码过程以增强结构感知分割；辅助解码器生成分支特定预测用于可靠性估计，并生成融合预测用于外观交换一致性；引入外观交换一致性分支约束结构表示在外观变化下保持稳定；采用可靠性图引导的双头判别器，包括有效性头与不确定性头，提供特征级不确定性反馈。

Result: 在 ISIC-2016 和 Kvasir-SEG 数据集上的实验表明，SAUF-Net 优于现有先进半监督方法，尤其在低标注比例设置下表现更突出。

Conclusion: 该方法说明，在半监督医学图像分割中显式建模结构与外观的解耦关系，并结合不确定性反馈，可提升特征可靠性、减少伪标签误差传播，从而改善分割性能。

Abstract: Semi-supervised learning has shown great potential for reducing annotation costs in medical image segmentation. However, most existing methods mainly exploit unlabeled data through prediction-level consistency, while the reliability of internal feature representations is often overlooked. In medical images, target-related structural cues are easily entangled with unstable appearance variations, which may lead to unreliable pseudo labels and error accumulation during training. To address these issues, we propose SAUF-Net, a Structure--Appearance Representation Learning with Uncertainty Feedback Network for semi-supervised medical image segmentation. SAUF-Net uses the Structure--Appearance Decomposition Module (SADM) to separate bottleneck features into structural and appearance representations. The Disentangled Guidance Module (DGM) injects these representations into the decoding process to enhance structure-aware segmentation. Meanwhile, the Auxiliary Decoder produces branch-specific predictions for reliability estimation and a fused prediction for appearance-swapped consistency. Furthermore, we introduce an Appearance-Swapped Consistency branch to encourage structural representations to remain stable under appearance variations. We also introduce a reliability-map-guided dual-head discriminator with a Validity Head and an Uncertainty Head to provide feature-level uncertainty feedback. Extensive experiments on ISIC-2016 and Kvasir-SEG demonstrate that SAUF-Net outperforms state-of-the-art semi-supervised methods, especially under low-label settings.

</details>


### [49] [Handwriting Trajectory Recovery via Autoregressive Ordered Stroke Instance Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02251)
*En-Guang Wang,Yan-Ming Zhang,Fei Yin,Cheng-Lin Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种两阶段手写轨迹恢复框架：先自回归预测有序笔画实例，再重建笔画内连续运动，在中文手写实验中优于对比方法，并具备跨类别与跨语言泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线手写图像只保留最终墨迹形状，丢失笔顺、方向和笔尖运动等时间信息，导致轨迹恢复高度歧义；现有方法多直接预测完整轨迹，未充分利用手写的笔画级组织结构。

Method: 提出遵循真实书写过程的两阶段框架：第一阶段将笔画提取与笔顺恢复结合，通过自回归方式预测有序笔画实例；第二阶段利用方向相关结构线索生成笔画内部连续轨迹。

Result: 在中文手写数据上的实验表明，有序预测比事后笔画排序更有效；完整点模型即使不做轨迹简化，也在数值上优于所有对比基线；控制实验显示轨迹采样密度会显著影响恢复性能评估；此外方法可泛化到未见中文字符类别，并可扩展到英文与泰米尔文手写。

Conclusion: 显式建模笔画级顺序与笔画内运动有助于缓解离线手写轨迹恢复的时序歧义，两阶段“先笔画、后运动”的设计更符合书写生成机制，具有较好的性能与泛化潜力。

Abstract: Handwriting trajectory recovery aims to infer the dynamic writing process hidden behind a static handwritten image. Since offline handwriting preserves only the final spatial ink pattern, temporal information such as stroke order, writing direction, and pen-tip motion is lost, making recovery inherently ambiguous. Existing learning-based methods often directly predict the complete character trajectory without explicitly exploiting the stroke-level organization of handwriting. We argue that recovering the writing process should follow the writing process itself. Accordingly, we propose a two-stage framework that first recovers ordered stroke instances and then reconstructs continuous within-stroke motion. The first stage integrates stroke extraction and stroke-order recovery through autoregressive ordered stroke prediction, while direction-related structural cues further support within-stroke trajectory generation. Experiments on Chinese handwriting show that the proposed ordered prediction is more effective than post-hoc stroke ordering. Even without trajectory simplification, our full-point model achieves numerically better results than those reported by all compared baselines, while a controlled analysis shows that trajectory sampling density substantially affects measured recovery performance. Additional experiments demonstrate generalization to unseen Chinese character categories and cross-language extensibility to English and Tamil handwriting.

</details>


### [50] [T2LSC-Bench: Benchmarking Localized Semantic Control in Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02255)
*Yan Wang,Xinyi Hou,Weiguo Lin,Junjun Si,Siwei Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 T2LSC-Bench，用于诊断文生图模型在局部文字生成中是否发生“目标文本关联语义泄漏”，即文本语义出现在指定文字区域之外的非文本视觉内容中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文生图文字评测主要关注可读性、拼写和布局，但在产品标签、标识、界面设计等场景中，仅正确生成文字不够，还需要保证文字语义被限制在指定区域内，并且不改变主体身份或周围场景语义。

Method: 构建包含 50 个种子主体、每个模型 1,200 个提示案例的 T2LSC-Bench，在六个模型上评估 7,160 张图像；通过因子化设计控制语义关系、场景开放性、提示模式和语言；采用 OCR-VLM 文本验证与结构化 VLM 语义判断的双分支协议，度量 TAA、SSP、SLR 和 cSLR。

Result: 在压力测试条件下，SLR 从 1.2% 上升到 18.1%，cSLR 从 1.3% 上升到 18.2%，而 TAA 仅从 91.4% 降至 90.9%；反泄漏提示可将 SLR 从 16.6% 降至 8.4%，且不降低文字渲染准确性；420 张图像的人类验证显示自动标注与人工裁决高度一致。

Conclusion: 准确渲染目标文字并不意味着模型能将目标文本语义局部约束在指定区域内；目标文本关联语义泄漏是文生图局部文字控制中一个重要但此前被低估的评测维度。

Abstract: Recent text-to-image models have become increasingly capable of rendering explicit text, but reliable localized text control requires more than generating the correct string. In applications such as product labeling, signage, and interface design, target text should be rendered within a designated text-bearing region without altering the predefined subject identity or surrounding scene semantics. We refer to violations of this requirement as target-text-associated semantic leakage, in which target-text semantics are expressed through non-textual visual content beyond the designated anchor. Existing visual-text benchmarks primarily evaluate readability, spelling accuracy, and layout, leaving this form of semantic leakage largely unexamined. We introduce T2LSC-Bench, a controlled diagnostic benchmark comprising 50 seed subjects and 1,200 prompt cases per model, yielding 7,160 evaluated images across six models. Its factorized design varies semantic relation, scene openness, prompt mode, and language. A dual-branch protocol combines OCR-VLM text verification with structured VLM semantic judgments to measure Text-at-Anchor Accuracy (TAA), Semantic Subject Preservation (SSP), Semantic Leakage Rate (SLR), and Conditional Semantic Leakage Rate (cSLR). Under stress-test conditions, SLR increases from 1.2% to 18.1% and cSLR from 1.3% to 18.2%, whereas TAA decreases only from 91.4% to 90.9%. Anti-leakage prompting reduces SLR from 16.6% to 8.4% without degrading rendering accuracy. Human validation on 420 images shows strong agreement between automatic and adjudicated annotations. These results show that accurate text rendering does not guarantee local containment of target-text semantics.

</details>


### [51] [MAOL: Morphology-Aware Ordinal Learning for Fine-Grained Industrial Defect Severity Grading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02266)
*Zhaoyang Wang,Haiyong Chen,Binyi Su,Kun Liu,Kun Wang,Xianen Zhou,Atik Shahariar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MAOL 是一个面向细粒度工业缺陷严重程度评级的形态感知序数学习框架，通过显式形态特征、自适应序数阈值和预测感知训练提升在干净 ROI 与噪声预测实例下的分级性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业检测中的缺陷严重程度标签具有天然序数关系，且分级高度依赖缺陷形态特征；同时，两阶段检测/分级流程中训练时使用干净标注实例、测试时使用带定位误差的预测实例，导致训练-测试分布不一致。

Method: 论文将严重程度分级建模为实例级序数学习任务；引入显式形态学特征增强表示；设计类别条件的自适应序数阈值以刻画不同缺陷类别的分级边界；通过定位扰动进行预测感知训练，提升模型对不完美预测实例的鲁棒性。

Result: 在 clean-ROI 和 predicted-instance 两种实验设置下，MAOL 均优于规则方法、普通名义分类模型和已有序数学习基线，尤其在 predicted-instance 场景中优势更明显；该方法在 IDA 2026 高精密制造细粒度严重程度分级挑战中排名第三。

Conclusion: MAOL 表明，将形态先验、序数结构和预测实例噪声建模结合起来，是提升工业缺陷细粒度严重程度评级精度与鲁棒性的有效策略。

Abstract: Fine-grained defect severity grading is essential for industrial inspection, yet remains challenging due to the ordinal nature of severity labels, the strong dependence on morphology-related cues, and the train-test discrepancy between clean annotated instances and noisy predicted instances in two-stage pipelines. We propose MAOL, a Morphology-Aware Ordinal Learning framework for fine-grained industrial defect severity grading. MAOL formulates severity grading as an instance-level ordinal learning task, incorporates explicit morphological features to enhance representation learning, introduces class-conditional adaptive ordinal thresholds to model defect-specific grading boundaries, and employs prediction-aware training via localization perturbation to improve robustness to imperfect predicted instances. Extensive experiments under both clean-ROI and predicted-instance settings demonstrate that MAOL consistently outperforms rule-based methods, nominal classification models, and existing ordinal baselines, especially in the predicted-instance setting. The proposed approach ranked third in the IDA 2026 Challenge on Fine-Grained Severity Grading for High-Precision Manufacturing.

</details>


### [52] [RouteGraph-Mona: Confusion-Aware Routing Fine-Tuning for Mineral Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02282)
*Jierui Li,Zhiyuan Qi,Hao Zhu,Yufan Liu,Jixian Liu,Shaojie Jiang,Jianda Wang,Yaqi Liu,Xiaotong Li,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 RouteGraph-Mona：在 Mona 适配器中用样本自适应路由替代静态多尺度聚合，并在路由空间引入类别锚点与混淆加权间隔，以提升矿物图像分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矿物图像分类存在类内外观差异大、类间视觉相似度高的问题；现有 Mona 虽能参数高效适配预训练视觉模型，但其静态多尺度聚合难以处理不同样本的尺度偏好，也难以缓解相似矿物类别间的混淆。

Method: 基于 Mona 构建轻量级 RouteGraph-Mona。首先将静态多尺度聚合替换为样本自适应分支路由，使每张图像形成表示尺度偏好的紧凑路由签名；随后在路由空间中使用类别级 route anchors 约束同类样本形成一致路由模式，并使用 confusion-weighted margins 增大易混类别之间的路由空间间隔。

Result: 在三个公开矿物图像数据集和两个视觉骨干网络上的实验表明，RouteGraph-Mona 在平均准确率上稳定优于 Mona，并且与代表性微调方法和矿物图像分类基线相比具有竞争力。

Conclusion: RouteGraph-Mona 通过样本自适应路由与路由空间正则化增强了 Mona 对尺度差异和类别混淆的建模能力，是一种有效且轻量的参数高效矿物图像分类方法。

Abstract: Mineral image classification is important for geological exploration and resource development, but it remains challenging due to substantial intra-class variations in appearance and high inter-class visual similarity. Multi-cognitive Visual Adapter (Mona) is a vision-oriented parameter-efficient adapter that adapts pre-trained visual models by tuning only a few parameters. However, Mona statically aggregates responses from multiple scales, limiting its ability to accommodate sample-specific scale preferences and model confusion among visually similar mineral categories. To address this issue, we propose \textbf{RouteGraph-Mona}, a lightweight route-space regularization method built on Mona. Specifically, we replace Mona's static multi-scale aggregation with sample-adaptive routing. The resulting branch-selection behavior defines a compact routing space that captures each image's scale preferences. We then regularize the resulting routing signatures with class-wise route anchors and confusion-weighted margins. The route anchors encourage class-consistent routing patterns, while the margins promote greater separation between visually similar categories in the routing space. Experiments on three public mineral image datasets with two visual backbones show that RouteGraph-Mona consistently outperforms Mona in mean accuracy and remains competitive with representative fine-tuning methods and mineral image classification baselines.

</details>


### [53] [Diffusion-Encoding Gaussian Field for Joint k-q dMRI Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02288)
*Zhibo Chen,Yajuan Huang,Yu Guan,Qiuyun Fan,Dong Liang,Qiegen Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种面向扩散MRI的受试者特异性空间-角度高斯场，在自监督条件下从欠采样k-space联合重建空间与q空间/方向信息，提高缺失方向DWI及扩散指标估计质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散MRI采集时间受空间采样和扩散方向采样共同限制。现有joint k-q方法往往将方向参数绑定到固定体素，或把空间重建与角度补全分开处理，未充分利用不同扩散方向图像共享解剖结构但信号强度随方向变化这一互补性，可能把残余空间误差误当成角向变化并传播到未观测方向。

Method: 构建受试者特异性的spatial-angular Gaussian field：用共享的3D Gaussian primitives表示局部空间支撑，每个primitive携带连续的q-conditioned tensor-residual响应；每个位置的信号由多个重叠primitive响应合成，从而耦合邻近空间区域与扩散方向。模型从已观测方向的欠采样k-space测量中渐进优化，无需全采样监督目标或留出方向监督。

Result: 在三个HCP扩散shell、多个加速设置下实验显示，该方法在缺失方向DWI重建、张量衍生指标以及主扩散方向估计方面均取得一致改进。

Conclusion: 该方法通过共享空间解剖表示与方向条件残差响应的联合建模，更有效地区分空间结构误差和真实角向信号变化，为加速扩散MRI的联合k-q重建提供了有效的自监督框架。

Abstract: Diffusion MRI requires repeated k-space acquisitions over multiple diffusion-encoding directions, making acquisition time dependent on both spatial and angular sampling. Existing joint k-q methods either associate directional parameters with fixed voxels or separate spatial reconstruction from angular completion. However, diffusion-weighted images acquired under different directions share the same anatomical organization, while their local signal intensities vary with diffusion encoding. Existing formulations do not fully exploit the complementarity between shared anatomy and direction-dependent signal variation. Consequently, residual spatial errors may be misinterpreted as genuine angular variation and propagated to unobserved directions.
  We propose a subject-specific spatial-angular Gaussian field for self-supervised joint k-q dMRI reconstruction. Shared 3D Gaussian primitives provide local spatial support, with each primitive carrying a continuous q-conditioned tensor-residual response. The signal at each location is synthesized from multiple overlapping primitive responses, coupling neighboring spatial regions and diffusion directions. The field is progressively optimized from undersampled k-space measurements of observed directions, without fully sampled targets or held-out-direction supervision.
  Experiments on three HCP diffusion shells under multiple acceleration settings demonstrated consistent improvements in missing-direction DWI reconstruction, tensor-derived metrics, and principal diffusion orientation estimation.

</details>


### [54] [If It Moves, Radar Knows: A Physics-Aware Radar Transformer for Class-Agnostic Moving-Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02289)
*Yinghao Sun,Shuguang Li,Jinliang Shao,Tieshan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种轻量级雷达-only稀疏检测器PART，用于类无关移动目标检测，能预测目标存在性、代表性表面点和地平面速度，在稀有/未标注类别及恶劣环境下表现较好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭集标注训练的检测器容易漏检训练类别之外的稀有移动目标；车载雷达虽具备类别无关的多普勒运动线索且对光照、天气更鲁棒，但其回波稀疏且噪声大，使类别感知的3D框检测困难。因此，转向预测表面位置与速度，以服务运动推理和碰撞避免。

Method: 论文提出Physics-Aware Radar Transformer（PART），一种完全稀疏的雷达-only检测框架。它通过Doppler-Aware Query Initialization（DAQI）按位置和速度聚类雷达点，生成输入相关查询；通过Physics-Guided Cross-Attention（PGCA）将径向多普勒一致性和RCS信息纳入查询-点关联；并使用不确定性感知监督，随机遮蔽真值目标并为雷达支持但标注不确定的查询分配软存在性标签。

Result: PART仅有110万参数，在nuScenes上取得CA-AP 0.8827、mASTE 0.3188 m、mAVE 0.8084 m；对标准评估外的稀有且安全相关类别召回率达到0.9203，并在夜间、雨天和严重遮挡条件下保持有效。部分表观误检经检查对应于nuScenes标注缺失的移动目标。

Conclusion: PART表明，将雷达物理约束、多普勒速度信息和不确定性感知监督结合，可在不依赖类别标签和完整3D框恢复的情况下，实现高效、鲁棒的移动目标感知，尤其适合开放集和安全关键自动驾驶场景。

Abstract: Detectors trained on closed-set annotations can miss rare moving objects outside the training taxonomy. Automotive radar provides category-independent Doppler motion cues and is less affected by adverse illumination and weather, but sparse, noisy returns hinder class-aware 3D box detection. Surface location and velocity remain useful for motion reasoning and collision avoidance when full box geometry is difficult to recover. We present the Physics-Aware Radar Transformer (PART), a fully sparse radar-only detector that predicts existence confidence, a representative surface point, and 2D ground-plane velocity for each moving-object hypothesis. Doppler-Aware Query Initialization (DAQI) replaces scene-independent learned queries with input-dependent proposals by clustering radar returns in position and velocity, easing query-object assignment in sparse scenes. Physics-Guided Cross-Attention (PGCA) incorporates radial-Doppler consistency and radar cross section (RCS) into query-point association. Uncertainty-aware supervision randomly masks ground-truth objects and assigns soft existence targets to ambiguous radar-supported queries, reducing reliance on exhaustive annotations. With only 1.1 million parameters, PART achieves a class-agnostic average precision (CA-AP) of 0.8827, a mean average surface translation error (mASTE) of 0.3188 m, and a mean average velocity error (mAVE) of 0.8084 m/s on nuScenes. It attains 0.9203 recall on rare and safety-relevant categories excluded from the standard evaluation and remains effective at night, in rain, and under severe occlusion. Inspection of apparent false positives shows that some predictions correspond to moving objects absent from the nuScenes annotations. Code and pretrained model weights will be publicly available at https://github.com/sunyinghao-uestc/PART.

</details>


### [55] [VoRTeC: Taming Foundation Flow for One-step Real time Video Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02291)
*Yichong Xia,Qinhong Wu,Bin Chen,Jinpeng Wang,Zeyuan Chen,Haoqian Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VoRTeC 是一种基于基础流模型 Wan2.1 的超低码率视频压缩框架，旨在兼顾高感知质量、低码率、快速解码和时序一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有超低码率视频压缩存在两类核心问题：传统神经视频压缩容易产生模糊伪影；扩散式生成视频压缩虽然感知质量较好，但解码延迟高且时间一致性较差。因此需要一种能利用生成式先验、同时实现快速解码和稳定时序的视频压缩方法。

Method: 论文提出 VoRTeC：基于基础流匹配视频模型 Wan2.1，通过紧凑编码潜在视频表示、预测压缩表示在流轨迹中的位置，并融合多尺度先验，使压缩器能够有效利用生成式视频流先验。该方法不访问流匹配网络的参数或梯度，实现一步解码；同时通过尾帧复用和先验缓存维持帧组间一致性。

Result: 实验显示，相比已有扩散式方法，VoRTeC 的比特消耗降低 58%，解码速度提升 3 到 197 倍；在 720p 分辨率下达到 13 FPS，在 480p 下达到 32 FPS。

Conclusion: VoRTeC 表明，基础流模型可以作为高效的视频压缩生成先验，在超低码率场景下实现更低码率、更快解码和更高感知保真的重建，同时改善生成式压缩中的时序一致性问题。

Abstract: Ultra-low bitrate video compression still faces critical challenges: traditional neural video compression inevitably introduces blurring artifacts, while diffusion-based generative video compression suffers from excessive decoding latency and poor temporal consistency. To address these issues, we propose $\mathtt{VoRTeC}$, a Video Compression framework built upon a foundational flow model (Wan2.1). By compactly encoding latent video representations, predicting the positions of compressed representations along flow trajectories, and integrating multi-scale priors, $\mathtt{VoRTeC}$ enables the compressor to harness generative video flow priors effectively. Without accessing the parameters or gradients of flow matching networks, our framework achieves one-step decoding and reconstructions with high perceptual fidelity. Meanwhile, we maintain consistency across frame groups via tail-frame reuse and prior caching. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method reduces bit consumption by 58\% compared to prior diffusion-based approaches, with decoding speed boosted by 3 to 197 times: $\mathtt{VoRTeC}$ achieves a decoding speed of 13 FPS at 720p and 32 FPS at 480p.

</details>


### [56] [Domain shift-robust object detection with GenAI image editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02299)
*Isabel D. Stein,Thijs A. Eker,Sebastiaan P. Snel,Ella P. Fokkinga,Klamer Schutte,Luca Ambrogioni,Friso G. Heslinga*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Method: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Result: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Conclusion: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Abstract: Object detectors often degrade under domain shifts such as changes in lighting, weather, or occlusion. These shifts alter object appearance and expose a reliance on visual shortcuts learned from the training distribution that do not generalize across domains. Acquiring sufficient real-world samples to capture such domain variation is particularly difficult in specialized, low-data settings. Recent advances in diffusion-based generative image editing have shown promise for improving the in-domain performance of object detectors through synthetic data augmentation. However, their potential to improve out-of-domain robustness remains largely unexplored. We hypothesize that generative image editing can simulate a controlled domain shift in training data, effectively bridging the gap between source and target domains. To test this, we studied camouflaged military vehicle detection as a challenging domain shift scenario. Detectors trained on uncamouflaged data demonstrate substantial degradation on real test imagery containing foliage, netting, and multi-spectral camouflage across 15 vehicle classes in close-up, ground-level imagery. We used two diffusion-based editing models, Qwen Image Edit 2509 and Flux.2 Dev, to synthetically add camouflage to the training data, alongside a LoRA fine-tuned version of Qwen. A non-generative black-bar occlusion baseline served as a lower bound on augmentation quality. Using a GroundingDINO detector trained on real and synthetic data, generative camouflage augmentation yielded substantial mAP improvements for foliage (+20.1) and netting (+14.4) camouflage. Generating multi-spectral camouflage proved more challenging, but LoRA fine-tuning improved performance by 4.4 mAP over the uncamouflaged baseline.

</details>


### [57] [YesTrack: Referring Multi-Object Tracking via MLLM-based Yes/No Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02318)
*Quansheng Hu,Qin Sun,Qiansen Dai,Jin Ding,Wan Zhang,Xue Zhou,Jianxiao Zou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 YesTrack，将指代表达多目标跟踪（RMOT）从生成式描述范式转为判别式 Yes/No 验证范式，直接利用多模态大语言模型的视觉-语言对齐能力，并在 Refer-KITTI 系列数据集上取得高效且领先的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RMOT 方法虽引入多模态大语言模型以提升泛化能力，但通常只把 MLLM 当作图像/目标描述生成器，最终匹配与决策仍依赖外部模块。这会带来额外延迟，并且未充分利用 MLLM 本身的视觉-语言对齐与判别能力。

Method: 作者提出两阶段方法 YesTrack：将 referring 过程重构为判别式任务，让 MLLM 对候选目标与语言表达是否匹配进行 Yes/No 验证，而非生成文本描述。为提升验证的可靠性与效率，设计了两个轻量级时序一致性约束：Temporal Confidence Prior（TCP）和 Temporal Reference Propagation（TRP）。此外，作者将该判别范式扩展到通用多目标跟踪，形成 YesTrack-MOT。

Result: 在 Refer-KITTI 和 Refer-KITTI-V2 上，YesTrack 显著优于现有最先进方法，并保持较高推理效率；即便使用最小规模的 Qwen3-VL 变体，也能取得强性能。YesTrack-MOT 也验证了该判别式范式在通用 MOT 场景中的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，将 MLLM 用作直接判别器而非文本生成器，可以更充分发挥其视觉-语言对齐能力，降低系统复杂度与延迟，并提升 RMOT 乃至通用 MOT 的性能与泛化能力。

Abstract: Referring multi-object tracking (RMOT) aims to track every instance in a video that matches a given language expression. Despite the recent integration of multimodal large language models (MLLMs) to enhance generalization, existing methods predominantly relegate them to the role of caption generators, necessitating external modules for final decision-making. This paradigm not only introduces extra latency but also severely underutilizes the inherent vision-language alignment capabilities of MLLMs. To address these limitations, we propose YesTrack, a novel two-stage RMOT method that reformulates referring as a discriminative task, directly leveraging MLLMs for Yes/No verification without explicit text generation. To further enhance the reliability and efficiency of this MLLM-based verification, we introduce two lightweight temporal consistency constraints: Temporal Confidence Prior (TCP) and Temporal Reference Propagation (TRP). We further validate the generality of this discriminative paradigm by proposing YesTrack-MOT, a straightforward yet highly effective instantiation for generic multi-object tracking (MOT). Experiments on Refer-KITTI and Refer-KITTI-V2 show that YesTrack significantly outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods while maintaining high efficiency, even when implemented with the smallest variant of Qwen3-VL. Code is released at https://github.com/ggbondrighthere24/YesTrack.

</details>


### [58] [ORB-SVM : An Innovative Hybrid Framework for Efficient Brain Tumor Detection from MRI Scans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02333)
*Amirhosein Azarpour*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向脑部MRI图像肿瘤识别的轻量级混合框架，结合ORB特征提取与SVM分类，在显著降低数据量的同时实现较高分类准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑部MRI诊断对早期发现至关重要，但人工判读存在主观差异，医学图像噪声和高维特征增加分析难度；深度学习方法通常参数量大、计算成本高且依赖大量训练数据，因此需要更高效、低资源消耗且可靠的替代方案。

Method: 研究采用ORB算法提取局部关键点与描述子，以保留与肿瘤识别相关的诊断模式，并去除大量非信息背景；随后使用基于核方法的SVM对提取后的稀疏特征进行分类。

Result: 在Br35H数据集上的实验显示，该方法实现约99.5%的数据压缩/削减，并取得97.5%的分类准确率。

Conclusion: ORB与SVM结合能够在降低计算开销和数据冗余的同时保持较高诊断性能，为医学图像分析提供一种资源高效、结构清晰的轻量化方案。

Abstract: Brain cancer remains one of the most significant challenges in modern medicine, where the accuracy of early stage diagnosis is a decisive factor in patient survival and treatment efficacy. Although Magnetic Resonance Imaging (MRI) is the established gold standard for visualizing neurological structures, the interpretation of these high dimensional scans is often complicated by subjective variability among practitioners and the inherent noise present in complex medical images. While contemporary approaches frequently rely on high parameter deep learning architectures, such models often involve significant computational costs and require extensive data for effective training. This study introduces a hybrid framework that utilizes the Oriented FAST and Rotated BRIEF (ORB) algorithm for precise feature extraction and a Support Vector Machine (SVM) for classification [1], [2]. The proposed approach achieves a sub- stantial data reduction of approximately 99.5%, which effectively minimizes the influence of non informative background data while preserving critical diagnostic patterns essential for tumor identification. By balancing feature sparsity with a robust kernel based classifier, this methodology addresses the limitations of over parameterized systems while maintaining high diagnostic integrity. Experimental evaluations conducted on the Br35H dataset demonstrate that the framework attains a classification accuracy of 97.5%. The findings suggest that the integration of localized feature representation and optimized classification provides a reliable and resource efficient alternative for medical image analysis, offering a structured solution that maintains per- formance without the need for extensive computational overhead.

</details>


### [59] [Towards Zero-Shot Transfer Across Embodiments For Driving VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02341)
*Caio Azevedo,Stefano Sabatini,Sascha Hornauer,Fabien Moutarde*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究自动驾驶VLA模型的多数据集训练与零样本跨数据集/跨相机配置泛化，并提出BEV-Forcing辅助目标以提升空间表征能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶VLA通常在单一数据集上训练，较少评估对未见数据集和相机rig的零样本迁移；同时，简单增加训练数据集并不一定提升已见配置性能。作者希望理解多数据集、多具身/相机配置训练下，辅助任务是否仍能带来稳定收益。

Method: 作者进行驾驶任务的多数据集训练，并引入BEV-Forcing：利用专门的鸟瞰图模型提供地面目标布局信息，将其作为辅助监督注入VLA骨干网络，促使模型通过共享BEV空间接口学习目标位置与场景布局表征。

Result: 在训练相机配置数量较少时，BEV-Forcing可同时提升分布内与分布外表现；但随着训练配置/embodiment数量增加，该辅助任务的增益逐渐减弱。

Conclusion: BEV空间辅助监督有助于低多样性训练场景下的泛化，但其收益会随训练数据与配置多样性的扩展而下降；因此评估新技术时应考虑数据规模和训练多样性，而不能只在小规模设置下报告效果。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action models (VLAs) have shown strong potential in autonomous driving by leveraging multimodal pretraining for instruction following, visual reasoning, and scene-level generalization. In robotic manipulation, scaling VLA fine-tuning across multiple robot setups--especially when unifying representations across embodiments--has been shown to improve in-dataset performance and cross-embodiment generalization; in autonomous driving, however, VLAs remain largely trained on individual datasets and are rarely evaluated for zero-shot transfer to unseen datasets and camera rigs; furthermore naively adding more datasets to the training data does not necessarily lead to better performance within seen embodiments. To address these problems, we study multi-dataset training for the driving task and BEV-Forcing, an auxiliary objective that transfers ground-plane object-layout information from a specialized Bird's-Eye-View model into the VLA backbone. By encouraging the model to represent object position through a shared BEV spatial interface, we show that an auxiliary task such as BEV-Forcing can improve both in-distribution and out-of-distribution performance when training on a small number of camera rigs. As the number of training embodiments increases, however, the benefits of the auxiliary task are reduced; we present this as evidence that new techniques in the literature may see their benefits diminish when simply scaling up training diversity, which motivates presenting results taking into account data scaling.

</details>


### [60] [Structured-Prior-Guided Diffusion Inpainting with Physical Consistency for Traffic Sign Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02348)
*Luo Li,Chongchong Huang,Jun Jia,Qiang Gao,Xinlong Liu,Gui Yang,Liang Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结构化先验引导的扩散修复框架，用于生成物理一致的交通标志样本，以缓解长尾类别数据不足问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通标志检测存在显著长尾分布，稀有标志样本少但监管意义同样重要；通用图像修复模型直接用于标志区域时容易造成数字畸变、几何/透视变形和颜色偏移，原因在于条件信号过于抽象，无法准确约束标志的物理组成。

Method: 方法基于约14亿参数的Stable Diffusion 1.5，设计三条正交先验注入路径：JSON格式文本提示提供语义先验，带实测主色的正视矢量模板经IP-Adapter提供外观先验，仿射对齐矢量模板经ControlNet提供几何先验；同时引入CIELAB色度L1损失和Sobel梯度损失，分别约束颜色一致性与边缘结构一致性，并在AMAP内部采集的大规模图像上进行自监督重建训练。

Result: 在跨来源的TT100K-2021上零样本评估，该方法在重建保真度、物理一致性和语义可控性指标上全面优于七类代表性竞品；OCR精确匹配率达到91.1%，显著高于12B参数FLUX.1 Fill [dev]的44.2%，推理时间仅为其1/14；消融实验证明三类先验路径和两个损失项均有独立贡献；用于下游检测时，稀有类别group-pooled AP50相对仅真实数据基线提升1.23倍至7.40倍。

Conclusion: 结构化语义、外观与几何先验结合物理一致性约束，能有效提升交通标志修复/合成质量，并显著改善长尾稀有类别检测性能，是面向交通标志生成式数据增强的有效方案。

Abstract: Traffic sign detection faces a long-tailed data distribution. Many rare signs matter as much as common ones from a regulatory standpoint, yet they have very few samples. Generative data augmentation is one way out. General-purpose inpainting models, however, distort digits, deform geometry and perspective, and shift colours when applied directly to sign regions. We trace this to a single gap: the conditioning signal is too abstract for the physical composition of a sign. We propose a structured-prior-guided diffusion inpainting framework with physical consistency. It injects the semantic, appearance and geometric priors of a sign through three orthogonal pathways: a JSON-formatted text prompt, a front-view vector template rendered with measured dominant colours (via IP-Adapter), and an affine-aligned vector template (via ControlNet). Two physical consistency losses constrain colour with a CIELAB chromaticity $L_1$ term and edge structure with a Sobel gradient term. We train by self-supervised reconstruction on a large set of images collected in-house at AMAP, then evaluate zero-shot on the public TT100K-2021 dataset, a different source. Our method uses a Stable Diffusion 1.5 backbone of about 1.4B parameters. It beats seven representative competitors on every metric of reconstruction fidelity, physical consistency and semantic controllability. Its OCR exact-match rate reaches 91.1\%, against 44.2\% for the 12B industrial model FLUX.1 Fill [dev], and it needs only $1/14$ of that model's inference time. Leave-one-out ablations confirm that each of the three prior pathways and both loss terms contribute on their own. In downstream detection, the synthetic data raises the group-pooled AP50 of rare classes by $1.23\times$ to $7.40\times$ over a real-data-only baseline. Code and pre-trained models are available at https://github.com/52hz-whale/TrafficSignInpaint.

</details>


### [61] [GlyphAnchor: Enhancing Visual Text Rendering via Position-Anchored Glyph Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02349)
*Qiang Xiang,Shuang Sun,Binglei Li,Yibo Chen,Xu Tang,Yao Hu,Junping Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GlyphAnchor 通过将轻量级字形补丁条件锚定到扩散 Transformer 的原生位置编码中，显著提升文本到图像生成与图像编辑中的文字渲染准确性，尤其适用于长文本、密集排版、复杂文本和罕见字符场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像生成与编辑模型在准确渲染文字方面仍存在明显不足，特别是在目标文本较长、结构复杂、排列密集或包含罕见字符时。已有方法要么依赖更强骨干网络和数据训练但缺少显式字形先验，要么引入字形先验但在复杂场景下准确性和鲁棒性不足，因此需要一种更稳定、通用且轻量的文本渲染增强方案。

Method: 论文提出 GlyphAnchor，用于增强文本到图像和图像编辑类扩散 Transformer 模型。核心方法是在骨干模型中加入轻量级 glyph patch 条件，并利用模型原生位置编码将这些字形补丁的位置锚定到目标图像空间。训练上采用分阶段监督微调，并进一步通过文本感知后训练提升复杂场景下的鲁棒性。同时提出 InfoTextBench，用于评估文本密集视觉内容在生成和编辑任务中的文字渲染能力。

Result: 在多个骨干模型和多个基准测试上，GlyphAnchor 在长文本、复杂文本、密集排版和罕见字符等挑战性场景中均持续提升文字保真度，同时保持整体图像质量。实验表明该方法具有较好的跨模型适用性和稳定增益。

Conclusion: GlyphAnchor 证明了将显式字形先验与模型原生空间位置机制结合，可以有效提升扩散 Transformer 在图像生成和编辑中的文本渲染能力。其轻量设计、分阶段训练策略和新基准 InfoTextBench 共同推动了文本丰富图像生成与编辑的评估和性能提升。

Abstract: Rendering accurate text remains difficult for image generation and editing models, especially when the target contains long, complex, and densely arranged text or rare characters. Existing approaches either improve native text rendering through stronger backbones and data-centric training without explicit glyph priors, or incorporate glyph priors through specialized designs that remain insufficiently accurate and robust under challenging scenarios. We introduce GlyphAnchor, a novel text-rendering enhancement method for both text-to-image and image-editing diffusion transformer models. GlyphAnchor enhances the backbone with lightweight glyph patch conditions whose positions are anchored to the target image through the model's native positional encoding. We train this capability with staged supervised finetuning and further refine it with text-aware post-training to improve robustness. We also introduce InfoTextBench, a benchmark for evaluating text-rich visual text rendering in both generation and editing settings. Experiments across multiple backbones and benchmarks, including long, complex, and densely arranged text and rare character scenarios, show that GlyphAnchor consistently improves text fidelity while preserving overall image quality.

</details>


### [62] [LookStep: Efficient Vision-Language Navigation with Linguistic Foresight and Event Driven Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02350)
*Kun-Yang Yu,Yingzhe Li,Hongyu Xu,Shi-Yu Tian,Zhi Zhou,Yang Chen,Ming Yang,Sheng Wang,Qing Yu,Lan-Zhe Guo,Yu-Feng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LookStep 是一个面向资源高效 VLN 的端到端框架，通过语言中心的未来状态建模与事件驱动滚动记忆，在较低数据、计算和存储开销下提升导航成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLN 方法多采用下一步动作预测，仅监督专家动作，训练数据需求较高；同时依赖认知地图、历史帧累积或外部 3D 工具维护状态，带来较大的计算与内存开销。因此需要一种更资源高效且性能稳定的 VLN 方法。

Method: 提出 LookStep：结合 Language Centric Future State Modeling，为每个候选动作生成粗粒度导航进度与未来状态的语言标签；结合 Event Driven Rolling Memory，由模型自主判断是否将当前观测以特定语义角色写入有界滚动记忆，从而控制记忆规模并保留关键状态信息。

Result: 在 VLN-CE 任务上，LookStep 在相同训练设置下优于已有方法；在 R2R-CE Val-Unseen 上达到 49.7% 的成功率，同时具备更好的内存效率并减少数据使用。

Conclusion: LookStep 表明，利用语言化的未来状态预测与事件驱动的有限记忆机制，可以在无需高成本地图、长历史帧或外部 3D 工具的情况下实现更高效的视觉语言导航。

Abstract: Vision-Language Navigation (VLN) requires an embodied agent to follow natural-language instructions in unseen environments. Recent progress has been largely driven by Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs). Existing methods follow a next-step action prediction paradigm, supervising only the expert action, which requires a high quantity of data for training. They also rely on cognitive maps, accumulated historical frames, or external 3D tools to maintain states, leading to high computational and memory overhead. To realize resource efficiency VLN, we propose LookStep, a unified end-to-end framework that combines Language Centric Future State Modeling and Event Driven Rolling Memory that uses language labels to generate coarse-grained navigation progress and future states for each candidate action, while autonomously deciding whether to write each observation into a bounded rolling memory with a semantic role. We validate LookStep empirically. On VLN-CE tasks, LookStep outperforms existing methods under the same training settings, achieving a 49.7\% success rate on R2R-CE Val-Unseen with better memory efficiency and less data usage. Code and model is available at https://github.com/kunyang-YU/LookStep.

</details>


### [63] [TempoGround: State-Aware Streaming Visual Grounding with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02359)
*Leqian Ding,Junning Qiu,Manwen Yang,Yu Guo,Fei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TempoGround 是一个面向流式输入的视觉语言模型原生视觉定位框架，通过跨帧对象对应与显式对象状态建模，提升流式视觉 grounding 的定位准确性与时序一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单帧和视频视觉定位方法在流式场景下容易出现身份漂移、跨帧预测不一致，以及部分遮挡时定位不稳定等问题，难以满足实时、因果输入条件下的开放词汇感知需求。

Method: 论文提出 TempoGround：首先基于状态感知的跨帧对应进行课程式预测，依次完成二维实例关联、对象状态预测（新进入、持续存在、离开视野）、二维框解码，并进一步提升到相机坐标系下的三维框。同时提出 Streaming Grounding Reinforcement（SGR），利用可验证的 Grounding、Identity 和 Consistency 奖励，强化持续定位和时序一致预测；训练上采用三阶段策略并使用大规模数据。

Result: 在多个具有挑战性的因果流式视觉定位基准上，TempoGround 平均提升 F1_2D@0.5 4.4 个点、F1_2D@0.95 0.5 个点，并将 F1_3D@0.25 和 AP_3D 分别提升 6.2 和 7.5 个点。

Conclusion: TempoGround 有效缓解了流式视觉 grounding 中的身份漂移和跨帧不一致问题，在二维和三维定位指标上均取得提升，表明其可作为流式输入下视觉定位任务的实用基础框架。

Abstract: Visual grounding maps language referents to spatial targets and is central to open-vocabulary perception with vision-language models. Existing methods have made substantial progress on single-frame and video-based visual grounding, yet under streaming inputs they still suffer from identity drift, cross-frame inconsistency, and fragile localization under partial occlusion. To address these issues, we present TempoGround, a VLM-native framework that detects cross-frame object correspondence and explicitly models object presence states, thereby enabling accurate and consistent visual grounding under streaming inputs. The key is a curriculum prediction mechanism guided by state-aware cross-frame correspondence: TempoGround resolves 2D instance association, predicts whether each object newly enters, continues in, or leaves the view, decodes the 2D box, and then lifts it to a camera-frame 3D box. As token-level supervision alone cannot capture the geometric objectives of streaming grounding, we further introduce Streaming Grounding Reinforcement (SGR), which optimizes TempoGround with verifiable Grounding, Identity, and Consistency rewards, jointly reinforcing persistent localization and temporally consistent predictions. We carefully design a three-stage training strategy and train TempoGround on large-scale data. We evaluate visual grounding under causally streaming inputs on multiple challenging benchmarks: TempoGround improves F1_2D@0.5 and F1_2D@0.95 by 4.4 and 0.5 on average, and F1_3D@0.25 and AP_3D by 6.2 and 7.5, respectively. These results demonstrate that TempoGround provides a practical foundation for visual grounding under streaming inputs.

</details>


### [64] [Information Density Imbalance in Visual Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02369)
*Ziwei Zhao,Yanxi Lu,Yuwei Hu,Shiyang Su,Mingxuan Wang,Chenyue Zhou,Jiayi Chen,Hehan Li,Xiaoshuai Hao,Andi Zhang,Yanbiao Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出“信息密度”用于解释目标检测中的类别偏差，发现类别信息密度与检测准确率显著负相关，并基于该概念改进损失函数，在多个数据集上降低偏差并提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上常用类别实例数量判断长尾分布，并默认少样本类别性能较差；但即使实例数相对均衡的数据集中，目标检测模型仍存在类别偏差，说明仅用实例数量无法充分解释偏差来源。

Method: 作者首先定义并度量类别的信息密度，分析其与类别检测准确率之间的相关性，并研究训练过程对该关系的影响；随后将信息密度引入三种先进目标检测损失函数，进行简单改进与实验验证。

Result: 实验表明，类别信息密度与准确率存在显著负相关；在 Pascal VOC、COCO-LT 和 LVIS 数据集上，基于信息密度改进的损失函数能够显著减少模型类别偏差，同时提升现有损失函数的整体检测性能。

Conclusion: 信息密度不平衡可能是目标检测类别偏差的重要来源之一。该研究为理解检测模型中的广义偏差现象提供了新视角，并为设计更公平的损失函数和训练策略提供了可行工具。

Abstract: In object detection, the number of instances is typically used to determine whether a dataset exhibits a long-tailed distribution, implicitly assuming that the model will perform poorly on categories with fewer instances. This assumption has led to extensive research on category bias in datasets with imbalanced instance numbers. However, even in datasets where instance numbers are relatively balanced, models still exhibit category bias, indicating that instance count alone cannot explain this phenomenon. In this work, we first introduce the concept and measurement of information density. We then observe a significant negative correlation between a category's information density and its accuracy, and we investigate how the training process impacts this relationship. Empirical studies suggest that information density imbalance may be a potential source of category bias. To preliminarily validate the potential of information density, we made simple improvements to three advanced object detection loss functions using this concept. Experiments on the Pascal VOC, COCO-LT, and LVIS datasets demonstrate that information density can significantly reduce model bias while effectively enhancing the overall performance of existing loss functions. This study provides a new perspective for understanding the generalized bias phenomenon in object detection models and offers new tools for designing fairer loss functions and training strategies.

</details>


### [65] [ProSR: Semantic-Prototype-Guided Discrete Modeling for Physically Consistent SAR Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02377)
*Byoungwoo Kim,Munchurl Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 ProSR，将 SAR 图像超分辨率从连续平滑生成转化为量化潜空间中的语义引导离散 token 预测，以更好保持 SAR 相干散射特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率 SAR 图像对自动目标识别等精细分析很重要，但获取成本高；现有基于扩散的生成式超分方法容易过平滑，破坏相干散射统计并产生不符合 SAR 物理特性的随机结构失真，同时 SAR 标注数据稀缺。

Method: 方法包括：使用量化潜空间将信号特征映射为离散散射原语；引入自监督学习骨干网络提取无标签语义先验；提出语义对齐细节编码，将高频信号解耦为离散散射原语；构建语义原型图生成器，并通过原型图引导注意力在同类语义区域内路由信息、减少类间干扰。

Result: 作者基于 Umbra Open Dataset 构建大规模 0.25m 分辨率基准，实验显示 ProSR 在视觉质量上优于对比方法，并能更好保持 SAR 应用所需的关键散射特征。

Conclusion: ProSR 通过语义原型与离散散射原语建模，缓解了传统平滑近似生成方法在 SAR 超分中的物理一致性不足问题，为高保真 SAR 图像超分提供了有效方案。

Abstract: High-resolution Synthetic Aperture Radar (SAR) imagery is critical for precision analysis such as automatic target recognition, yet its acquisition is costly. Although generative image super-resolution (ISR) models offer a promising alternative, current smooth-approximation based diffusion frameworks often struggle to preserve the coherent scattering statistics, causing stochastic structural distortions that are less consistent with real SAR physics. To address this, we propose Semantic Prototype-Guided Super-Resolution (ProSR), reformulating SAR ISR as a semantically-guided discrete token prediction task within a quantized latent space. By mapping signal features to discrete scattering primitives, ProSR preserves the impulsive nature of SAR without over-smoothing. Furthermore, we integrate a Self-Supervised Learning backbone into SAR ISR to extract label-free semantic priors, overcoming label scarcity. Guided by these priors, we introduce Semantic-Aligned Detail Encoding to decouple high-frequency signals into discrete scattering primitives. In parallel, the Semantic Prototype Map Generator explicitly constructs semantic prototype maps, allowing Prototype-Map-Guided Attention to route the information flows within identical categories and mitigate inter-class interference. To validate our approach, we present a large-scale 0.25m resolution benchmark from the Umbra Open Dataset. Experimental results show ProSR achieves superior visual quality while preserving essential scattering characteristics required for practical SAR applications.

</details>


### [66] [CA-OPD: Confidence-Aware On-Policy Distillation for Structured Visual Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02401)
*Menghao Li,Linjie Mu,Yin Wang,Haotian Hu,Yannian Gu,Lujiayi Xue,Fanyi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出置信度感知的在策略蒸馏 CA-OPD，用教师置信度选择性纠正学生 rollout，并将干预决策与 token 级监督方式对齐，从而提升视觉语言模型在 GUI grounding 与 OCR 上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视觉语言模型在推理时容易产生累积误差；传统离线训练与推理分布不匹配。在策略蒸馏虽能缓解该问题，但训练早期学生预测不可靠，会破坏轨迹并降低教师监督质量。现有交错蒸馏依赖刚性排序指标，未充分利用教师置信度，也未将干预决策用于指导监督设计。

Method: 提出 Confidence-Aware On-Policy Distillation（CA-OPD）。方法使用教师置信度判断学生转移是否可靠：不可靠位置由教师纠正，可靠位置保留学生 token；同时采用从严格到宽松的调度逐步把 rollout 控制权转移给学生。监督上，教师纠正的位置使用教师预测的直接交叉熵监督，学生保留的位置使用教师完整预测分布进行蒸馏。

Result: 在 GUI grounding 和 OCR 的多教师设置中，CA-OPD 在六个目标基准上均显著提升 Qwen3.5-0.8B 基线；ScreenSpot-Pro 提升 9.50 分，OCRBench-v2 English 提升 6.72 分。消融实验显示性能提升来自干预位置、渐进式 rollout 控制和干预对齐监督，而不仅是干预频率。

Conclusion: CA-OPD 通过置信度驱动的可靠轨迹构造与自适应 token 级监督，有效缓解自回归视觉语言模型的训练-推理不匹配和累积误差问题，是一种适用于多任务视觉语言模型蒸馏的有效框架。

Abstract: Autoregressive vision language models unify heterogeneous perception tasks but are highly susceptible to compounding errors. On-policy distillation (OPD) bridges the training-inference mismatch by training students on their own rollouts. However, unreliable student predictions, especially early in training, can derail the trajectory and degrade the quality of teacher supervision. While recent interleaved distillation methods allow the teacher to verify and replace student tokens, they primarily rely on rigid ranking metrics rather than exact teacher confidence, and they overlook how intervention decisions can inform token-level supervision. To address this, we introduce Confidence-Aware On-Policy Distillation (CA-OPD), a framework that couples reliable rollout construction with adaptive supervision. CA-OPD utilizes teacher confidence to selectively correct unreliable student transitions, gradually transferring rollout control to the student via a strict-to-relaxed schedule. Crucially, CA-OPD aligns knowledge transfer with these intervention decisions: corrected positions receive direct cross-entropy supervision from the teacher's prediction, while retained positions benefit from the teacher's full predictive distribution. Evaluated in a multi-teacher setting for GUI grounding and optical character recognition, CA-OPD substantially improves the Qwen3.5-0.8B baseline across all six target benchmarks, including gains of $9.50$ points on ScreenSpot-Pro and $6.72$ points on OCRBench-v2 English. Controlled studies further show that the gains depend on intervention placement, progressive rollout control, and intervention-aligned supervision, rather than intervention frequency alone.

</details>


### [67] [The Diagnosis a Reporter Leaves Unspoken: Surfacing Frozen Tumor Features for Brain-Tumor MRI Reporting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02411)
*Khawaja Murad ul Hassan,Ruqiyya Adil,Adil Qayyum,Rida Hassan,Asad Mansoor Khan,Muhammad Usman Akram,Mehran Ebrahimi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 NeuroFusion：通过显式利用脑 MRI 报告模型内部潜在的肿瘤类别判别信号，显著改善诊断正确性、报告质量、可验证性与推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于医学 Mistral-7B 的多链 CoT 脑 MRI 报告生成器虽然能生成报告文本，但在关键诊断上表现“沉默”或错误：常将脑膜瘤和转移瘤误称为胶质瘤；同时模型冻结的分割特征中又能被线性探针恢复出较强的肿瘤分类信息，说明问题不在于模型完全没有诊断信号，而在于生成过程未能有效使用这些潜在信号。

Method: 作者提出 NeuroFusion：在相同 Mistral 主干上，针对每个病灶特征加入判别式 field-classifier heads，并将其确定输出作为条件，驱动快速的 single-pass draft-then-review 解码器生成报告；同时采用语法约束解码以提升结构合法性与可蕴含性。论文还设置负向对照：用 learned diagnosis pin 直接覆盖解码器诊断，以检验机制差异。

Result: NeuroFusion 将诊断召回显著提升：脑膜瘤 0.92、转移瘤 0.75；在三组 held-out cohorts 上的 9 项 prose-content 比较中赢得 8 项，剩余 1 项无显著损失；延迟约 80 s/case，相比基线约 457 s/case 降低 5–6 倍。语法约束使 92.3% 记录 schema-valid，矛盾句比例 7.5%，低于直接基线的 36.8%。负向对照中，直接覆盖式 diagnosis pin 导致 OOD 转移瘤召回降至 0.03。九例盲评中，两名神经科医生均将 NeuroFusion 评为各肿瘤类型最佳，且无 critical errors。

Conclusion: 论文表明，医学报告生成模型的失败可能源于潜在判别信息未被生成器正确利用，而非信息不存在。将冻结表征中的诊断信号以“辅助条件”方式注入生成过程，比强行覆盖诊断更稳健，可同时提升诊断召回、报告内容质量、可检查性与效率。

Abstract: A capable brain-MRI report generator can still be, in effect, diagnostically silent. When a multi-chain chain-of-thought (CoT) reporter built on a medical Mistral-7B backbone is evaluated on held-out cohorts, it names most meningiomas and almost all metastases "glioma" (diagnosis recall 0.44/0.07). Yet the answer is not absent from the model: a supervised linear probe applied to its frozen segmentation features recovers the three tumour cohorts at 0.82 macro-F$_1$ (5-fold cross-validation; chance $\approx$0.33). We introduce NeuroFusion, an assistive reporter that surfaces this latent signal rather than overriding it: discriminative field-classifier heads over per-lesion features condition a fast, single-pass draft-then-review decoder on their committed outputs. Built on the identical Mistral backbone, this restores the diagnosis (meningioma 0.92, metastasis 0.75) and wins 8 of 9 prose-content comparisons across three held-out cohorts (RaTEScore, RadGraph-F$_1$, GREEN; Holm-corrected paired BCa), with no significant loss on the ninth, at 5-6x lower latency ($\approx$80 vs. 457 s/case). A controlled negative result sharpens the mechanism: a learned diagnosis pin that overrides the decoder instead of merely informing it collapses out-of-distribution metastasis recall to 0.03. Grammar-constrained decoding keeps 92.3% of records schema-valid, making every sentence entailment-checkable (7.5% contradicted vs. 36.8% for the direct baseline).
