In a blinded nine-case pilot, two board-certified neurologists independently rated NeuroFusion highest in every tumour type, the only system with zero critical errors, and gave it the top-rated sign-off in eight of nine cases (six outright, two ties).

</details>


### [68] [Uncertainty-Guided Adverse Weather Restoration via Gated Transformer Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02434)
*Zheke Jin,Yuning Cui,Tianle Jin,Alois Knoll,Hu Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 UAR-Net，用于一体化恶劣天气图像复原，通过不确定性引导、门控双尺度 Transformer、平衡多尺度跳连和亮度感知能量损失，在多个恶劣天气基准上达到先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 恶劣天气造成的图像退化具有空间异质性。现有天气特定复原方法常依赖天气无关的全局聚合、简单跨尺度融合和确定性优化目标，难以在一体化恶劣天气场景中有效处理不同区域、不同尺度的复杂退化。

Method: 提出 Uncertainty-guided Adverse-weather Restoration Network（UAR-Net）。核心包括：Gated Dual-scale Transformer Blocks（GDTB）用于选择性全局交互与多尺度局部结构建模；progressive Balanced Multi-scale Skip Connection（BMSC）用于更均衡的多尺度特征融合；Uncertainty-Aware Refinement Head（URH）用于伪影去除、细节增强与预测不确定性估计；同时采用 Brightness-Aware Energy Loss（BAE-Loss）监督重建并校准不确定性。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法在多个恶劣天气图像复原基准数据集上取得 state-of-the-art 性能，优于现有方法。

Conclusion: UAR-Net 通过显式建模空间异质性退化和预测不确定性，提升了一体化恶劣天气图像复原的鲁棒性和重建质量，是一种有效的天气特定 AiO 图像复原框架。

Abstract: Restoring images degraded by adverse weather remains challenging due to spatially heterogeneous degradations. Many existing weather-specific restoration models rely on weather-agnostic global aggregation, naive cross-scale fusion, and deterministic objectives, which struggle to handle heterogeneous degradations in all-in-one adverse-weather settings. To address these limitations, we propose an Uncertainty-guided Adverse-weather Restoration Network (UAR-Net), a weather-specific AiO framework that integrates a gated transformer with balanced multi-scale skip connections. Specifically, we employ Gated Dual-scale Transformer Blocks (GDTB) to jointly model selective global interactions and multi-scale local structures, a progressive Balanced Multi-scale Skip Connection (BMSC) for balanced multi-scale feature integration, and an Uncertainty-Aware Refinement Head (URH) that performs artifact removal, detail enhancement, and predictive uncertainty estimation. The model is supervised by a Brightness-Aware Energy Loss (BAE-Loss) to encourage accurate reconstruction with well-calibrated uncertainty. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple adverse-weather benchmarks. The codes will open source upon acceptance.

</details>


### [69] [Adapting a Foundation Model for Lunar Surface Height Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02448)
*Patrick Bauer,Marius Schwinning,Melanie Siegel,Andreas Weinmann,Hichem Snoussi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文将通用零样本相对深度估计模型 Depth Anything V2 迁移到月表高度估计任务，通过使用公开的立体摄影测量生成 DEM 数据进行微调，显著提升了月表相对深度/高度估计性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来月球着陆任务需要精确的高度信息以识别岩石、陨坑等危险地形。传统 DEM 生成方法如 SfS 和 SPG 有效但存在任务与数据约束；通用单目深度模型虽强，但直接零样本用于月表领域可能因域差异而性能不足。

Method: 以 Depth Anything V2 作为基础模型，利用公开可获得的由 SPG 生成的月表 DEM 数据构建监督信号，对模型进行目标域微调，使其从月球单目图像中估计相对表面高度/深度。

Result: 相较未经适配的零样本 DAV2，微调后的模型在月表相对深度估计上取得显著性能提升，说明领域适配能有效改善通用视觉模型在月球 DEM 估计任务中的表现。

Conclusion: 通过少量/公开月表 DEM 数据对通用深度估计模型进行微调，可将 DAV2 转化为更可靠的月表相对高度估计器，为月面危险地形定位和着陆安全分析提供补充信息。

Abstract: Digital elevation models (DEMs) can provide accurate height information, making it invaluable for analyzing the lunar surface. As the European Space Agency (ESA) prepares for future lunar missions that aim to land on the Moon, a precise method for height estimation will be essential for hazardous terrain that could endanger the landing approach. Traditional approaches to generate DEMs from imagery, such as shape from shading (SfS) and stereophotogrammetry (SPG) have been proven highly valuable for this task. However, due to advancements in machine learning, especially computer vision, the focus has shifted towards monocular depth estimation via deep learning. The lunar surface is covered by rocks and craters, and classic hazard detection methods rely solely on 2D image data. Our goal is to address this issue by developing a relative lunar surface height estimator that can provide additional information for hazard localization. In this letter, we present a methodology that builds on the well-known zero-shot relative depth estimation model Depth Anything V2 (DAV2). Other works have been using it as a state-of-the-art comparison for their proposed lunar DEM estimation method, but without adaptations to the target domain. Thus, it may underperform. Therefore, we propose a fine-tuning strategy with publicly available SPG-derived DEM data of the lunar surface. Our results demonstrate a significant improvement in performance compared to the zero-shot model, effectively transforming DAV2 into a reliable relative depth estimator of the lunar surface.

</details>


### [70] [WiFlow: Estimating Optical Flow using WiFi Channel State Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02452)
*Thomas Weigel,Simon Kiefhaber,Fabian Portner,Matthias Hollick,Simone Schaub-Meyer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 WiFlow：利用 WiFi 信道状态信息（CSI）替代摄像头帧进行光流估计，并构建首个相关数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统光流估计依赖摄像头，但摄像头存在隐私风险，且图像质量易受光照影响。作者希望探索一种更隐私友好、对光照不敏感的运动感知方式，即使用 WiFi CSI 推断场景中物体的位置变化与运动速度。

Method: 作者提出 CSI-based 光流估计框架 WiFlow，包括 CSI 预处理评估方法，以及三种在精度与复杂度之间具有不同权衡的模型架构。同时构建了首个用于训练和评估 CSI 光流估计器的数据集，并通过实验分析关键设计因素。

Result: 实验表明，WiFi CSI 可以用于光流估计任务，所提出的数据集和模型能够支持该方向的训练与评估，并揭示了影响 CSI-based 光流估计性能的若干重要设计选择。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了以 WiFi CSI 替代摄像头进行运动场估计的可行性，为隐私保护、弱光或无光条件下的场景运动感知提供了新方向，并为后续研究提供了基准数据与代码。

Abstract: Knowing where and how fast objects are moving within a scene is important across various domains. Usually, cameras are used to capture the data necessary for this task, but adding cameras often raises privacy concerns, and the quality of captured frames is heavily influenced by lighting conditions. In this work, we explore using WiFi channel state information (CSI) instead of camera frames for optical flow estimation. We propose WiFlow, a CSI based flow estimator, a preprocessor evaluation for CSI, and three model architectures that offer different trade-offs between accuracy and complexity. Further, we create the first dataset for training and evaluating CSI-based optical flow estimators, and our experiments provide insights into key design elements for this task. Code and data are available at https://visinf.github.io/wiflow.

</details>


### [71] [ShallowStream: Index Shallow then Answer Deep for Streaming Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02780)
*Jitai Hao,Ke Yang,Qiang Huang,Jun Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ShallowStream 利用多模态大语言模型的浅层 KV cache 为视频流构建轻量级检索索引，在保持性能的同时显著降低流式视频理解的计算延迟与缓存增长开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续视频流输入下，现有 MLLM 方法通常需要对新帧反复执行全深度 prefill，导致计算成本高、延迟大，并且 KV cache 随 prefill 深度快速增长。既有优化多集中在视觉 token 剪枝、合并、量化、按需检索或上下文卸载，较少从模型深度维度降低开销。

Method: 提出 ShallowStream：在流处理阶段仅使用 MLLM 的浅层同时完成帧编码与检索索引构建，维护一个常驻的轻量级浅层 KV cache 索引；在查询回答阶段，利用浅层产生的注意力分数评估上下文帧相关性，并结合多样性感知选择策略检索准确且覆盖全面的证据帧。

Result: 在性能上可与最强的现有流式方法相当，同时将单帧 prefill 延迟最高降低 52.1 倍，将 10 秒端到端延迟最高降低 11.9 倍。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，MLLM 浅层表示可有效承担流式视频场景中的索引与检索功能，从模型深度维度显著降低在线视频理解成本，为低延迟、长时视频流理解提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Streaming video understanding is a critical capability for real-world applications, including embodied intelligence, autonomous driving, industrial monitoring, surveillance and early warning, and wearable assistants. However, processing continuous video streams with multimodal large language models (MLLMs) is computationally expensive. Existing efforts have explored reducing streaming overhead through visual token pruning, token merging, quantization, on-demand frame retrieval, and context offloading. However, most existing methods overlook the dimension of model depth. Repeatedly executing full-depth MLLM prefill over incoming frames is prohibitively expensive, incurring substantial computational overhead and causing the KV cache to grow at a rate directly proportional to the prefill depth. To address these challenges, we propose ShallowStream, a novel framework that leverages the shallow layers of an MLLM to simultaneously perform frame encoding and retrieval index building. During stream processing, ShallowStream maintains an always-on lightweight index using the KV cache of shallow layers. During query-time answering, we leverage the attention scores generated by the shallow layers to score context frames and employ a diversity-aware selection strategy to retrieve precise and comprehensive evidence. ShallowStream achieves performance on par with the strongest existing streaming methods, while reducing per-frame prefill latency and 10-second end-to-end latency by up to 52.1x and 11.9x, respectively. Our code is available at https://github.com/CURRENTF/ShallowStream.

</details>


### [72] [VIPS: Vehicle-Infrastructure Cooperative Planning Benchmark via Pseudo-Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02462)
*Hoonhee Cho,Jae-Young Kang,Giwon Lee,Hyemin Yang,Heejun Park,Kuk-Jin Yoon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VIPS，一个面向车路协同（V2I）自动驾驶的伪仿真评测基准，并提出基于稀疏表示的协同规划框架CoS-V2X，用于在更可扩展且更贴近真实的条件下评估鲁棒性与误差传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市端到端自动驾驶面临部分可观测、遮挡严重、交叉口密集交通和多智能体交互复杂等问题。单车感知能力受限，V2I协作可改善感知与规划；但现有开环评测无法反映误差累积与偏离恢复，闭环评测成本高、扩展性差且仿真域差距明显，因此需要一种折中且可靠的评测方式。

Method: 作者提出VIPS基准，将伪仿真扩展到车端与路侧基础设施观测融合的V2I场景，以避免完全仿真的高成本，同时保留对鲁棒性和误差传播的评估能力。此外，提出CoS-V2X框架，利用稀疏/紧凑特征建模车路交互，实现高效通信与异构观测下的协同决策。

Result: 摘要声称VIPS能够实现可扩展且较真实的协同自动驾驶评测，并可评估鲁棒性和误差传播；CoS-V2X则在通信效率和复杂观测条件下的决策鲁棒性方面具有优势。但摘要未给出具体定量指标或与基线方法的数值比较。

Conclusion: 该工作主要贡献在于提出一种介于开环与闭环之间的V2I伪仿真评测范式，并配套提出稀疏表示驱动的协同规划方法，为车路协同自动驾驶的可扩展评估与规划研究提供数据、代码和基准支持。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving in urban environments requires robust decision-making under partial observability and complex multi-agent interactions. Severe occlusions and dense traffic at intersections limit the perception capability of single-agent systems, motivating recent efforts on Vehicle-to-Infrastructure (V2I) cooperation for perception and planning. However, existing evaluation protocols face a fundamental trade-off: open-loop evaluation fails to capture error accumulation and recovery from deviations, while closed-loop evaluation is costly, difficult to scale, and often relies on simulated environments that may suffer from domain gaps. To bridge this gap, we propose VIPS, a benchmark for cooperative autonomous driving in V2I settings based on pseudo-simulation. VIPS extends pseudo-simulation by integrating vehicle and infrastructure observations. This enables scalable yet realistic evaluation of robustness and error propagation without full simulation. We further present CoS-V2X, a cooperative planning framework based on sparse representations. CoS-V2X models vehicle-infrastructure interactions using compact features for efficient communication and robust decision-making under heterogeneous observations. Code and dataset are available at https://vips2026.github.io.

</details>


### [73] [Learning to Track from Privileged Target Appearances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02471)
*Xin Chen,Jiao Xu,Dong Wang,Huchuan Lu,Kede Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PATT利用训练阶段可用的真实目标外观作为特权信息，通过教师-学生框架提升视觉跟踪器在部署时的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉跟踪中的目标模板存在核心矛盾：初始真实模板定位准确但随时间失效，近期预测模板外观更新但来源于不确定预测，可能引入误差。作者通过一个不可部署的oracle验证，若能获得当前帧精确目标裁剪，LaSOT AUC可提升15.2个百分点，说明外观信息瓶颈显著。

Method: 提出Privileged Appearance Transfer for Tracking（PATT）。训练时，教师模型可观察过去、当前和未来帧的真实目标裁剪；学生模型只能使用部署时可获得的过去帧模板。学生通过多层级表征预测学习教师的搜索区域表示。为避免传播低质量教师信号，方法根据教师相对学生的定位优势以及教师自身绝对定位精度，对迁移损失进行可靠性加权。推理时移除教师、潜变量预测器、可靠性权重和特权裁剪，仅保留标准学生跟踪器。

Result: 在七个基准数据集、两个模型规模以及长短期跟踪协议下，PATT均带来稳定性能提升。oracle实验显示当前帧精确目标裁剪可在LaSOT上带来15.2个百分点AUC提升，支撑了方法设计的必要性。

Conclusion: 该工作证明训练阶段的真实目标外观可作为有效特权信息，通过表示蒸馏方式注入可部署跟踪器，从而缓解模板新鲜度与定位可靠性之间的矛盾，并在不增加推理成本的情况下提升视觉跟踪性能。

Abstract: Target templates define what a visual tracker searches for, yet the templates available at inference trade off localization certainty with appearance freshness: the initial ground-truth template is exact but becomes stale, whereas recent templates better reflect the current appearance but are cropped from uncertain predictions. We quantify this bottleneck with a non-deployable oracle that supplies an exact current-frame target crop, improving AUC on LaSOT by 15.2 percentage points. This gap reveals a training-only opportunity: frame-level ground truths provide exact current- and future-frame target crops, although such crops are unavailable at deployment. We introduce Privileged Appearance Transfer for Tracking (PATT), a teacher-student training framework that transfers these privileged appearances to a deployable tracker through multi-level representation prediction. The privileged teacher observes exact target crops from past, current, and future frames, whereas the student receives only past-frame templates and learns to predict the teacher's search representations. To avoid transferring unreliable teacher signals, PATT weights this transfer by the teacher's relative localization advantage over the student and its absolute localization accuracy. After training, the teacher, latent predictor, reliability weights, and privileged crops are removed, leaving standard student-only inference. Across seven benchmarks at two model scales, PATT achieves consistent gains under both long- and short-term tracking protocols.

</details>


### [74] [UnCapsTSR: An Unsupervised Transformer-based Image Super-Resolution Approach for Capsule Endoscopy Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02476)
*Anjali Sarvaiya,Shubh Kawa,Lalit Agrawal,Jagrit Joshi,Kishor Upla,Kiran Raja*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 UnCapsTSR：一种面向无线胶囊内镜低分辨率图像的无监督 Transformer-GAN 超分辨率框架，无需真实 LR-HR 配对或显式退化估计，并在多个数据集和无参考指标上优于现有无监督 SR 方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线胶囊内镜受胶囊尺寸与无线传输限制，采集图像分辨率较低，影响胃肠道异常观察；真实场景中难以获得严格配对的低/高分辨率训练样本，因此需要适合真实 WCE 数据的无监督超分辨率方法。

Method: 提出基于 Transformer 的生成对抗网络 UnCapsTSR，在无监督条件下进行 WCE 图像空间超分辨率；引入 Bilateral Total Variation 损失以增强超分辨率图像的空间连续性；构建来自 Kvasir Capsule 的新训练数据集；提出面向内镜图像的无参考质量指标 Endoscopy Quality Metric（EndoQM）。

Result: 在未参与训练的 KID 与 GIANA 数据集上验证泛化能力；实验显示该方法在 NIQE、BRISQUE、PIQE 和 EndoQM 等无参考质量评价指标上持续优于先进无监督超分辨率方法；统计结果显示从 LR 到 SR，EndoQM 提升约 40% 至 80%。

Conclusion: UnCapsTSR 能在缺乏真实 LR-HR 配对数据的情况下有效提升 WCE 图像空间分辨率，并具有较好的跨数据集泛化能力；EndoQM 为胶囊内镜超分辨率质量评估提供了更具领域针对性的量化工具。

Abstract: Wireless Capsule Endoscopy (WCE) captures and streams video while passing through a patient's Gastrointestinal (GI) tract and is used to examine its irregularities. Although advantageous over conventional endoscopy, WCE suffers from limitations related to capsule size and wireless transmission, resulting in images with coarser resolution. This work presents UnCapsTSR, an unsupervised transformer-based Generative Adversarial Network (GAN) framework for improving the spatial resolution of Low-Resolution (LR) WCE images. The proposed method accomplishes SR without explicit degradation estimation of real-world LR data and eliminates the need for true LR-HR pairs. UnCapsTSR employs a Bilateral Total Variation (BTV) loss to ensure spatial continuity in SR images. A newly curated dataset from the Kvasir Capsule dataset is also presented for training WCE SR models. Generalizability is validated on KID and GIANA datasets that are not used during training. A new non-reference metric, Endoscopy Quality Metric (EndoQM), is introduced for quantitative evaluation of domain-specific WCE data. Experiments demonstrate consistent improvement over state-of-the-art unsupervised SR approaches using NIQE, BRISQUE, PIQE, and EndoQM. Statistical evaluation shows 40 to 80 percent improvement in EndoQM from LR to SR across the evaluated datasets.

</details>


### [75] [Blending Concepts: Benchmarking Visual Metaphor Generation in Text-to-Image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02502)
*Chuer Chen,Zichen Wang,Yi He,Zhengxi Yu,Nan Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出首个用于评估文生图模型视觉隐喻生成能力的基准 VMetaphor-Bench，并发现现有模型在组合结构与跨域映射方面仍存在明显不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文生图模型已能较好生成具体对象和属性，但其生成视觉隐喻（通过跨领域元素组合表达抽象概念）的能力尚缺乏系统研究与评估。

Method: 作者构建包含 1,500 个真实创意图像来源视觉隐喻的基准，按三层级和十类别组织，并为每个样本提供两种具体程度不同的提示词；同时设计 MLLM-as-judge 混合评估框架，包括基于 9,594 个多选题的四级隐喻保真度评估，以及沿三个感知维度的打分协议。

Result: 对 11 个代表性文生图模型的广泛评估表明，即使最强的闭源模型也难以稳定完成隐喻表达中的组合结构构建和跨域语义映射。

Conclusion: 视觉隐喻生成是文生图研究中尚未充分解决的重要前沿问题，VMetaphor-Bench 为后续模型能力评估和改进提供了系统基准。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) models have achieved remarkable success at faithfully rendering specified objects and attributes, yet their ability to produce visual metaphors, images that convey abstract ideas by combining elements from two distinct domains, remains largely unexamined. To bridge this gap, we introduce VMetaphor-Bench, the first benchmark for evaluating visual metaphor generation in T2I models. It comprises 1,500 visual metaphors curated from real-world creative imagery, organized into three levels and ten categories, with each sample paired with two prompts of differing specificity. For evaluation, we develop a hybrid framework within an MLLM-as-judge paradigm, combining a multiple-choice question (MCQ) based protocol of 9,594 questions across four levels of metaphorical fidelity with a dimension-based scoring protocol along three perceptual dimensions. Extensive evaluation of 11 representative T2I models reveals that even the strongest proprietary models struggle with compositional structuring and cross-domain mapping, key aspects of metaphorical expression, highlighting visual metaphor generation as an important frontier for future T2I research.

</details>


### [76] [SR-Edit: Region-Aware Image Editing via Self-Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02504)
*Andong Wang,Zehua Chen,Yuxuan Jiang,Jun Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SR-Edit 是一种用于生成式图像编辑的自迭代精修框架，旨在无需外部区域标注的情况下，更准确地区分编辑区与非编辑区，并提升非编辑区域保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像编辑方法虽然能自动推断编辑/非编辑区域并约束非编辑区域一致性，但常存在区域估计不准、启发式修正破坏原始采样过程的问题，反而引入伪影并降低编辑质量。

Method: SR-Edit 在每次迭代中基于模型自身预测结果，通过轻量级后处理逐步提取更精确且自一致的区域分离；随后对非编辑区域施加与原始采样动力学保持一致的校正更新，以实现保真保护。

Result: 大量实验表明，SR-Edit 相比现有图像编辑技术在非编辑区域保持能力和整体图像质量方面取得更优表现。

Conclusion: 该工作说明，通过利用模型自身预测进行迭代式区域自精修，并采用不偏离原始采样动态的保真约束，可以有效缓解无标注图像编辑中的区域误判和伪影问题。

Abstract: With the recent rapid progress in generative models, image editing has made remarkable advances, yet achieving faithful edits that precisely modify only the target regions while strictly preserving all other regions remains challenging. Since externally provided region annotations are often difficult to obtain in practice, a growing body of work seeks to improve preservation by automatically inferring edit and non-edit regions, and then enforcing consistency on the latter. However, these approaches still suffer from inaccurate region estimation and heuristic correction strategies that distort the native inference process, making methods designed for fidelity themselves a new source of artifacts. We propose SR-Edit, an image editing framework that overcomes these issues via iterative self-refinement. Specifically, at each iteration, SR-Edit first (i) extracts progressively precise and self-consistent region separation from the model's own predictions by lightweight post-processing, and then (ii) enforces preservation in non-edit areas through correction updates that remain aligned with the original sampling dynamics. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SR-Edit achieves superior preservation and overall image quality compared to existing editing techniques.

</details>


### [77] [Orthogonal Ensembles and Tested Explanations for Performer-Independent Body-Motion Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02510)
*Naoto Nishida,Yoshio Ishiguro*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究基于骨架运动的12类身体动作情绪识别，在严格的留表演者交叉验证设置下，通过集成具有互补误差模式的11个模型显著提升Macro-F1，并重点贡献一套后验解释分析方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 身体-only情绪识别在LPO评估下高度困难且欠定，单一强基线表现有限；作者希望在不单纯追求新架构的情况下，找到可靠性能提升来源，并验证模型决策是否真正依赖运动相关的身体区域证据。

Method: 复现协议匹配的STGCN++基线；在训练表演者上进行10折LPO交叉验证；构建11模型等权logit均值集成；对强集成成员及最终集成使用部位遮蔽、反事实编辑等后验解释方法，并将区域显著性与规则化Laban Movement Analysis属性及经典运动学指标进行Spearman相关比较。

Result: STGCN++基线Macro-F1为25.73±4.03%；11模型集成达到36.80±4.00%，提升11.07个百分点、相对提升43%。区域显著性与LMA属性相关性显著高于经典运动学：rho=+0.500 vs +0.033；最终11模型集成也保持rho=+0.517。时间窗口内显著性呈弥散分布而非局部集中。

Conclusion: 论文表明，在困难的跨表演者身体情绪识别任务中，性能增益主要来自误差互补模型集成而非新架构；其解释套件支持模型使用运动扎根的身体区域证据，且这些证据更符合LMA描述体系。同时，该方法也能诚实揭示时间显著性不集中的负面发现。

Abstract: We study body-only, 12-class acted-emotion classification from skeleton motion under leave-performer-out (LPO) evaluation, a hard, underdetermined setting: chance is 8.3%, and a protocol-matched reproduced STGCN++ baseline reaches only 25.73 +/- 4.03% Macro-F1. We show that reliable gains come not from a new architecture but from combining eleven models with orthogonal error modes: under 10-fold LPO cross-validation on the labeled training performers, an equal-weight logit-mean ensemble reaches 36.80 +/- 4.00% per-fold Macro-F1, a protocol-matched +11.07 pp (+43% relative) over the same-split reproduced baseline. Our central contribution is a tested explanation suite: for a strong ensemble member, part-masking and counterfactual edits show (rather than assert) that its decisions depend on motion-grounded body-region evidence, and this region saliency aligns with rule-based Laban Movement Analysis (LMA) attributes far more than with classical kinematics: region-level saliency-LMA Spearman rho = +0.500 versus +0.033, roughly 15x, and the alignment holds for the submitted 11-way ensemble itself at rho = +0.517; the audit is post hoc and needs no retraining. The same suite faithfully reports a negative: within-window temporal saliency is diffuse rather than localized.

</details>


### [78] [Beauty is in the AI of the beholder: MLLMs systematically overrate facial attractiveness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02512)
*Santiago Grandas,Juan Sebastian Cely-Acosta,Mohit Mendiratta,Shafee Hassan,Macken Murphy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Method: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Result: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Conclusion: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Abstract: Beauty assessments from Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) are increasingly popular amongst users, companies, and aestheticians. This raises the question of whether these AI models can accurately reflect human judgments of attractiveness. In a pre- registered exploratory study, we compared the attractiveness ratings of 2,513 human participants to four widely used commercial AI models: Claude, Gemini, GPT, and Grok. Results showed that MLLMs systematically rate faces more favourably and within a narrower range than humans and, at the time of study, do not reproduce human ratings in absolute terms. However, MLLMs exhibit strong correlations with human attractiveness judgments, accurately tracking the rank-ordering of faces. MLLMs may judge faces by different cues than humans; only face age was a predictor of facial attractiveness in both humans and MLLMs, with inconsistent patterns across models for ethnicity and gender. AI models strongly agree with one another, except for Grok, which also showed the lowest agreement with humans. Our findings suggest that while they may be able to approximate rank-orderings of human attractiveness, current off-the-shelf commercial MLLMs systematically overrate the beauty of human faces.

</details>


### [79] [Doppio: A Dataset for Contactless Weight Estimation of Falling Particles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02528)
*Simon Kiefhaber,Jan-Martin O. Steitz,Julia Grabinski,Christoph Reich,Paul Wagner,Max Zimmermann,Simone Schaub-Meyer,Stefan Roth*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证用计算机视觉与深度学习对下落粉末颗粒进行非接触式累计质量估计，并发布咖啡研磨场景视频数据集 Doppio。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业中粉末及下落颗粒的质量测量很常见；传统秤适合静态测量，但许多动态场景需要非接触方案，而现有方案往往昂贵、专用且技术复杂。因此需要一种更通用、低成本、易部署的视觉测量替代方法。

Method: 作者以咖啡研磨为现实案例，构建 Doppio 视频数据集：包含下落咖啡粉视频及逐帧精确质量标注。随后评估多类基于深度学习的视觉模型，包括仅利用空间信息的前馈网络和利用时空信息的循环模型，并比较其预测精度与计算开销。

Result: 实验表明，深度学习视觉模型能够较准确地估计下落颗粒的累计重量；不同模型在精度和计算成本之间存在权衡。

Conclusion: 基于深度学习的计算机视觉可作为粉末/颗粒动态质量非接触测量的可行方案；Doppio 数据集和基线模型为未来视觉式工业测量系统提供了基础。

Abstract: Measuring the mass of powder, including falling particles, is a common task in industrial applications. While scales are effective for static measurements, many applications require contactless sensing, where existing solutions are often costly, application-specific, and technically complex. In this work, we investigate computer vision as a practical alternative for contactless mass estimation. As an accessible real-world case study, we focus on coffee grinding and introduce \emph{Doppio}, a novel video dataset capturing videos of falling ground coffee, paired with precise, per-frame ground-truth weight measurements. To demonstrate contactless measuring, we evaluate deep learning-based approaches ranging from purely spatial feed-forward networks to recurrent spatio-temporal models. These models are analyzed with respect to their predictive accuracy and computational trade-offs. We demonstrate that deep learning-based computer vision models accurately estimate the cumulative weight of falling particles, establishing a solid foundation for future vision-based contactless measurement solutions.

</details>


### [80] [Fine-Grained Anomaly Perception in Wild UGC-Enhanced Images: A Comprehensive Dataset and Difference-Fusion Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02529)
*Yan Zhong,Gefei Chen,Qiufang Ma,Zhen Wang,Zhiwei Fan,Lei Shi,Tingting Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出面向UGC图像增强结果的异常感知新任务UEAP，构建首个真实业务场景基准UEAP-4k，并提出DFAP-UGC方法与LADTP训练策略，在局部异常识别、定位与严重程度评估上优于改造后的传统基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短视频与社交媒体平台广泛使用图像增强/修复作为后处理，但这些算法可能在人脸、文本、纹理等局部区域引入视觉异常，损害感知保真度与用户信任；现有IQA方法主要评估整体质量，难以捕捉真实UGC增强场景中的局部、特定异常。

Method: 作者定义UEAP任务，构建包含异常类别、位置和严重程度细粒度标注的UEAP-4k数据集；提出DFAP-UGC，通过问题图与参考图的显式差异融合、密集空间查询、区域验证和质量感知排序进行异常识别；同时提出LADTP训练策略以处理多子任务耦合，实现端到端学习并减少多阶段开销。

Result: 大量实验表明，DFAP-UGC在该新任务上超过由经典方法适配而来的基线，验证了UEAP-4k数据集的价值以及所提方法在复杂UGC增强图像异常感知中的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 论文系统填补了UGC增强图像局部异常感知评测与建模的空白，提供了新任务定义、首个基准数据集和有效端到端方法，对平台级图像增强质量监控具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Image enhancement and restoration have become standard back-end operations on short-video and social media platforms to boost UGC visual experience. Yet these processes inevitably introduce visual anomalies--especially in faces, texts, and textures--that directly undermine perceptual fidelity and viewer trust. While existing IQA methods perform well on classic distortions, they target holistic quality assessment and fail to capture the specific, localized anomalies caused by enhancement algorithms in real-world UGC. To bridge this gap, we formally define a new task-quality Anomaly Perception for UGC image Enhancement (UEAP), and contribute the first UEAP benchmark dataset, named UEAP-4k, curated from the real business scenarios. It provides fine-grained annotations for anomaly categories, localization and severity levels. Furthermore, we propose a Difference-Fusion Anomaly Perception Method (DFAP-UGC) for wild UGC-enhanced images, which leverages explicit problem-reference difference fusion with dense spatial querying, regional verification, and quality-aware ranking, enabling robust anomaly identification in challenging scenarios. To handle the inherent coupling of subtasks in this new task, we propose a Locality-Aware Dynamic Task Prioritization (LADTP) training strategy that enables effective end-to-end learning and eliminates multi-stage overhead. Extensive experiments show that our method outperforms baselines adapted from classical approaches for this task, validating the value of this dataset and the superior of DFAP-UGC for robust UGC-enhanced image anomaly perception. Code and data will be public.

</details>


### [81] [Spatially Aware World Action Model via Geometric Latent Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02531)
*Javier Alejandro Lopetegui Gonzalez,Paul Pacaud,Cordelia Schmid*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SA-WAM 将预训练视频扩散模型改造为同时预测动作、RGB 与深度的 3D 感知世界动作模型，在仿真与真实机器人任务中取得更好表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 World Action Models 主要依赖 RGB 观测，未充分利用深度/三维几何信息，限制了机器人策略学习中对空间结构、物体位置和交互关系的建模能力。

Method: 提出 Spatially Aware World Action Model：在单一扩散骨干中联合预测未来 RGB、深度与动作；使用非线性深度编码将无界深度信号映射到冻结 VAE tokenizer 可接受的有界输入域，从而复用预训练视频模型的视觉与物理先验，无需对 tokenizer 进行 3D 专用微调。

Result: 在 RoboCasa 与 LIBERO-Plus 基准上达到 state-of-the-art，并提升未来状态预测质量；在 UR5 真实机械臂评测中优于强基线，尤其在随机化环境中提升明显。

Conclusion: 引入深度信息能显著增强 WAM 的空间感知与策略泛化能力；世界模型预测质量与 rollout 成功率之间的相关性分析为后续改进 WAM 提供了依据。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) leverage the capabilities of large-scale pretrained video diffusion models to jointly predict future observations and actions, inheriting rich visual and physical priors from internet-scale video. This has made them a promising paradigm for robot policy learning, yet the prevailing models operate exclusively on RGB observations and do not leverage 3D information. To bridge this gap, we introduce a Spatially Aware World Action Model (SA-WAM), which repurposes a pretrained video model for joint action, RGB, and depth prediction, enabling 3D-aware world modeling and action prediction within a single diffusion backbone. We use a nonlinear encoding that maps the unbounded depth signal into the bounded input domain expected by the frozen VAE tokenizer. This allows us to reuse the tokenizer without 3D-specific fine-tuning, incorporating geometric information without sacrificing the pretrained priors. SA-WAM achieves state-of-the-art results on the RoboCasa and LIBERO-Plus benchmarks, while simultaneously improving future-state predictions. Furthermore, SA-WAM outperforms strong baselines in real-world evaluation using a UR5 robotic arm, with strong gains in randomized environments. We analyze the correlation between world model prediction quality and rollout success, providing insights into WAM performance and avenues for its improvement.

</details>


### [82] [RGB-to-IR image translation for infrared vehicle detection in unseen UAV domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02556)
*Thijs A. Eker,Ella P. Fokkinga,Jan Erik van Woerden,Elfi I. S. Hofmeijer,Sebastiaan P. Snel,Klamer Schutte,Friso G. Heslinga*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究利用生成式RGB到热红外（IR）图像翻译来缓解无人机航拍热红外车辆检测中的目标域数据稀缺问题，并证明其可显著提升跨域检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实热红外航拍数据稀缺，而RGB无人机图像相对丰富；但热红外图像包含发动机热量等RGB中不可直接观测的模态特征，使RGB到IR的可迁移翻译较困难。因此需要验证现代生成模型是否能跨越该模态差距，用于提升未见目标域的IR车辆检测。

Method: 在成对RGB-IR源域数据集上训练多类翻译模型，包括监督式GAN、基于ControlNet的扩散模型以及结合LoRA的基础模型编辑方法；随后将这些模型应用于保留目标数据集的RGB训练图像，生成合成IR图像。用生成的IR数据训练RF-DETR车辆检测器，并在五个航拍数据集的未见IR目标测试集上评估，其中Kust4K和VTUAV作为主要目标域。

Result: 合成IR训练数据稳定优于RGB和灰度基线。Stable Diffusion 3.5结合ControlNet表现最佳：相较仅用源域IR数据训练的模型，Kust4K上的mAP从50.8提升到60.1，VTUAV上的mAP从25.6提升到38.4。在VTUAV上，通过多随机种子增加输出多样性可额外提升1.1 mAP，使用提示词变化可额外提升3.3 mAP。

Conclusion: 生成式RGB-to-IR翻译虽仍无法完全达到真实目标域IR数据的效果，但能有效缓解热红外数据稀缺，并提升跨域航拍车辆检测性能；其中扩散模型与ControlNet的组合最具潜力。

Abstract: Synthetic training data is crucial for developing vision AI when real-world data is scarce, as in thermal infrared (IR) aerial vehicle detection. While abundant UAV RGB imagery motivates RGB-to-IR translation for data augmentation, unobservable thermal traits (e.g., engine heat) make learning transferable mappings challenging. This work investigates whether modern generative translators can overcome this cross-modal gap to improve infrared vehicle detection on unseen UAV target domains. Translators are trained on paired RGB-IR source datasets and applied to RGB training images from held-out target datasets to generate synthetic IR data. Evaluated methods include supervised GANs, ControlNet-based diffusion models, and foundation-model editing via LoRA. The resulting synthetic IR imagery is used to train RF-DETR vehicle detectors, which are evaluated on unseen IR target test splits across five aerial datasets, with Kust4K and VTUAV serving as target domains. Synthetic IR consistently outperforms RGB and grayscale baselines. Stable Diffusion 3.5 with ControlNet yields the best results, improving mAP from 50.8 to 60.1 on Kust4K and from 25.6 to 38.4 on VTUAV compared to models trained only on source-domain IR data. Increasing output diversity via multiple seeds (+1.1 mAP) and prompt variations (+3.3 mAP) provides additional gains on VTUAV. Although a performance gap to real target IR data remains, generative RGB-to-IR translation effectively mitigates IR data scarcity and improves cross-domain aerial vehicle detection.

</details>


### [83] [Stereo 4D Radar for 3D Object Detection: Integrating Geometric Alignment and Absolute Velocity Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02560)
*Seung-Hyun Song,Dong-Hee Paek,Woong-Chan Byun,Seung-Hyun Kong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于双目4D雷达的3D目标检测框架，通过左右雷达几何视差估计目标绝对速度，并融合互补特征以提升感知鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始4D雷达数据存在地面、护栏、车辆等杂波，以及多径鬼影和噪声；预处理会导致点云过度稀疏。同时，4D雷达多普勒仅提供径向速度，难以恢复目标完整运动状态，限制了3D目标检测性能。

Method: 利用左右两侧4D雷达形成的双目几何关系，基于几何视差估计目标绝对速度；同时设计几何感知的特征融合机制，将左右雷达的互补信息融合用于3D目标检测。

Result: 在自建双目4D雷达数据集上验证，相比先进的单目4D雷达基线方法，AP 3D提升8.82个百分点，AP BEV提升9.0个百分点。

Conclusion: 绝对速度估计与双目几何感知特征融合能够显著增强4D雷达3D目标检测性能，说明双目4D雷达在复杂环境下具有更强的运动感知与目标检测潜力。

Abstract: Four-dimensional (4D) Radar is a powerful sensing modality capable of detecting surrounding three-dimensional (3D) objects under diverse weather conditions and providing Doppler-based motion information. However, raw 4D Radar signals contain significant clutter from road surfaces, guardrails, and surrounding vehicles, along with multipath-induced ghost reflections and the receiver's inherent noise floor. Consequently, preprocessing algorithms designed to remove such invalid measurements often make the Radar data excessively sparse. Moreover, the Doppler measurements provided by 4D Radar describe only the radial component of an object's velocity, limiting their ability to recover the full motion state. In this paper, we introduce a stereo 4D Radar-based 3D object detection framework that exploits the geometric disparity between left and right Radars to estimate the absolute velocity of objects and achieve more robust perception through the fusion of their complementary features. The effectiveness of the proposed framework is validated on our in-house stereo 4D Radar dataset, demonstrating performance gains of 8.82 points in AP 3D and 9.0 points in AP BEV over state-of-the-art mono 4D Radar baselines. These results demonstrate that absolute velocity estimation combined with stereo geometry-aware feature fusion leads to substantial improvements in 3D object detection.

</details>


### [84] [MARS: What Retrieval Signals Are Hidden in Multimodal Large Language Models for Text-Video Retrieval?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02565)
*Uicheol Jung,Juyoung Hong,Geuntaek Lim,Yukyung Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MARS 提出一种面向文本-视频检索的多层、多槽嵌入框架，用多个自适应表示槽融合解码器不同层隐藏状态，以提升细粒度匹配能力，并在多个基准上取得最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大语言模型作为嵌入模型时，通常用最终层的单个 token 表示整个文本或视频，容易将复杂的视频-文本线索压缩到单一向量中，导致对相似场景、动作和时序模式的区分能力不足。

Method: MARS 从不同解码器层提取并融合隐藏状态，构建多个自适应表示槽；对文本与视频的对应槽进行相似度比较，并聚合槽级相似度用于检索。同时引入 hard-negative-aware slot specialization objective，使不同槽学习捕获更具判别性的匹配线索，尤其针对易混淆负样本。

Result: 在四个文本-视频检索基准上，MARS 在直接相似度检索和重排序设置中均达到 state-of-the-art。消融实验表明，多层融合、多槽表示和难负样本感知槽专门化均带来互补提升。

Conclusion: 该论文表明，相比单向量表示，多层多槽的细粒度表示更适合文本-视频检索；通过槽级匹配和难负样本驱动的专门化训练，模型能更好地区分语义相近的视频候选。

Abstract: Text-video retrieval requires representations that can distinguish videos with similar scenes, actions, and temporal patterns. Recent multimodal large language models have been adapted as embedding models, but they often represent each input using a single token from the final layer. This can compress diverse video-text cues into a single vector and limit fine-grained retrieval. To address this limitation, we propose MARS, a multi-layer and multi-slot embedding framework for text-video retrieval. MARS constructs multiple adaptive representation slots by combining hidden states from different decoder layers, compares corresponding text and video slots, and aggregates their similarities for retrieval. To better handle confusing candidates, we further introduce a hard-negative-aware slot specialization objective that encourages the slots to capture discriminative matching cues. Experiments on four text-video retrieval benchmarks show that MARS achieves state-of-the-art results in both direct similarity-based retrieval and reranking settings. Ablation studies and analyses demonstrate that multi-layer fusion, multiple slots, and hard-negative-aware slot specialization provide complementary gains. Code is available at https://github.com/sejong-rcv/MARS.

</details>


### [85] [Deeply Interleaved Text-Image Contexts for Multimodal LLMs Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02573)
*Zihao Wang,Xi Xiang,Yuwen Sun,Yingyu Li,Yabo Zhang,Yihan Zeng,Fan Li,Wangmeng Zuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 TIC-Bench，用于评估多模态大语言模型在深度交错文本-图像上下文中整合线索并恢复事实的能力；实验显示现有模型与人类专家存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态模型的训练与评测主要集中在多图像任务，文本多作为指令存在，缺乏与视觉内容的深层语义交互；而真实应用需要模型持续整合交错出现的文本与图像信息，因此需要新的评测基准。

Method: 构建 TIC-Bench 基准，覆盖逻辑关联、时间关联、空间关联三大核心领域，并细分为八类任务，共包含 2,280 个问题；使用该基准评估 10 个先进多模态大语言模型。

Result: 实验发现，现有模型在深度交错文本-图像上下文中的证据整合能力仍明显不足，与人类专家表现存在较大性能差距，尤其难以综合分布在文本与图像中的多源线索。

Conclusion: TIC-Bench 为分析和推动多模态模型在深度交错文本-图像场景下的信息融合能力提供了有价值的评测工具，并揭示了当前模型在复杂跨模态上下文理解方面的关键短板。

Abstract: Current evaluations and training of multimodal models predominantly focus on multi-image tasks, largely overlooking interleaved text-image scenarios. In such multi-image tasks, text typically serves merely as task instructions, lacking deep semantic interaction with the visual content. In contrast, realworld applications like text-image co-creation, character tracking, and spatial reconstruction require constant interaction between text and images. Consequently, models must possess a deep understanding of these interleaved contexts. To bridge this gap, we introduce a novel benchmark, TIC-Bench (deeply interleaved Text-Image Contexts), designed to evaluate the capability of models to integrate text-image clues and recover the ground truth facts within deeply interleaved contexts. This benchmark encompasses three core domains: Logical, Temporal, and Spatial Association, which are further categorized into eight specific types, comprising a total of 2,280 questions. We evaluated 10 state-of-the-art MLLMs and observed a substantial performance gap compared to human experts, together with persistent difficulties in integrating evidence distributed across interleaved visual and textual inputs. Ultimately, this benchmark provides a valuable analytical tool for assessing and advancing the ability of multimodal models to effectively integrate text and image information in deeply interleaved contexts. TIC-Bench is publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/pino10010/TIC-Bench

</details>


### [86] [RVSD: Retrieval Vision Sparse Decoding for Mitigating Visual Hallucinations in Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02731)
*Canjie Liu,Jiawen Kang,Jinbo Wen,Zishao Zhong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RVSD，一个免训练、即插即用的单次解码框架，通过结合视觉token稀疏化与语义空间视觉检索来缓解大型视觉语言模型的视觉幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型在视觉语言任务中表现突出，但容易产生视觉幻觉，影响实际应用可靠性；现有方法通常依赖精心构造的数据集、额外训练或多轮解码，计算开销较大。

Method: 提出Retrieval Vision Sparse Decoding（RVSD）：在单次解码中统一token sparsification与Semantic-Space Visual Retrieval。具体包括语义导向的token选择策略，用于去除冗余视觉token并保留关键信息；以及SSVR机制，将视觉补偿建模为共享语义空间中的按需跨模态检索。

Result: 大量实验表明，RVSD在缓解视觉幻觉方面达到当前最优性能，并且在长上下文生成场景下仍能保持稳定的幻觉抑制能力。

Conclusion: RVSD提供了一种高效、免训练且可插拔的解码方案，在降低计算负担的同时增强了视觉语言模型的可靠性，尤其适用于需要长文本生成的场景。

Abstract: Large vision-language models have achieved remarkable success in vision-language tasks. However, they remain prone to Visual Hallucinations (VHs), undermining their reliability in real-world applications. Existing solutions typically require curated datasets, additional training, or multi-round decoding, resulting in considerable computational overhead. In this paper, we propose \textbf{RVSD} (\underline{R}etrieval \underline{V}ision \underline{S}parse \underline{D}ecoding), a training-free and plug-and-play decoding framework that, for the first time, unifies token sparsification and \textbf{Semantic-Space Visual Retrieval} (SSVR) within a single decoding pass. Within RVSD, we introduce a \textbf{semantics-directed token selection} strategy that selectively sparsifies redundant tokens while preserving critical visual information. We further propose the SSVR mechanism, which reformulates visual compensation as an on-demand cross-modal retrieval process within a shared semantic space. Extensive experiments demonstrate that RVSD achieves state-of-the-art performance in mitigating VHs while maintaining robust suppression capabilities under long-context generation settings. Our code is available here.\footnote{https://github.com/canjie-liu/RVSD}

</details>


### [87] [Generalizable Brain Tumor Segmentation with Self-Training and Tumor-Aware Deformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02600)
*Henrique Zan Grande,Jeovane Honorio Alves,Rayson Laroca,Andre Gustavo Hochuli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向 BraTS-GoAT 任务的脑肿瘤跨人群泛化分割方法，在 nnU-Net 大残差编码器基础上结合伪标签半监督学习与肿瘤感知可变形增强，较仅使用标注数据的基线在各肿瘤区域均有提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BraTS-GoAT 关注在异质患者群体中对脑肿瘤亚区进行鲁棒分割，核心挑战是模型需要具备良好的跨肿瘤、跨患者泛化能力；仅依赖有限标注数据可能不足以覆盖复杂病灶形态与数据分布变化。

Method: 方法采用 nnU-Net 框架和 large residual encoder 架构；利用未标注训练数据生成伪标签进行半监督自训练，并设计 tumor-aware deformable augmentation，在局部变形肿瘤病灶的同时尽量保持周围解剖结构稳定；同时比较不同组件及不同置信度伪标签比例的贡献。

Result: 在 BraTS-GoAT 验证集上，提交配置取得 Whole Tumor Dice/NSD 为 0.881/0.473，Tumor Core 为 0.817/0.490，Enhancing Tumor 为 0.775/0.533；相较 labeled-only 基线，所有肿瘤区域均有提升。

Conclusion: 实验表明，伪标签自训练能够有效利用未标注数据，肿瘤感知可变形增强可增强形态泛化能力，二者具有互补性，是提升脑肿瘤分割泛化性能的有效组合。

Abstract: This work presents an approach to the Generalizability Across Tumors (BraTS-GoAT) task of the BraTS 2026 Challenge, which focuses on robust segmentation of brain tumor sub-regions across a heterogeneous patient population. The proposed method employs the nnU-Net framework with a large residual encoder architecture, integrating a semi-supervised learning technique with pseudo-labels generated from the unlabeled training data and a tumor-aware deformable augmentation that locally deforms the lesion while preserving the surrounding anatomy. We evaluate the individual contributions of each component, as well as their combination, using varying proportions of the most confident pseudo-labeled cases. The submitted configuration for the generalization task achieves Dice and NSD scores of 0.881 and 0.473 for Whole Tumor, 0.817 and 0.490 for Tumor Core, and 0.775 and 0.533 for Enhancing Tumor on the BraTS-GoAT validation set, improving over the labeled-only baselines across all tumor regions and confirming that self-training and the proposed augmentation are complementary. Our source code is publicly available at https://github.com/Henrique-zan/brats-goat-2026/.

</details>


### [88] [AffectDelta: Beyond Emotion Labels for Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02616)
*Xingzu Zhan,Lin Gu,Ruogu Fang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 AffectDelta，将情感驱动图像编辑建模为八维情感分布之间的源感知转移，而非单一情感类别编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有场景级情感图像编辑通常使用单一情感类别或操作级文本指令作为目标，难以表达混合情感中不同维度的增减与保持关系，也难以精确控制源图像到目标情感状态的变化幅度与方向。

Method: 方法首先使用冻结的情感分布预测器估计源图像的八维情感分布，再以源分布到目标分布的有符号差值表示编辑方向和强度。AffectDelta 通过内部转移编码器和源感知扩散骨干网络，将该情感变化信号转化为上下文相关的语义与外观修改。作者还构建了 AffectPair-249K 数据集，包含 248,841 个源-目标图像对及其预测的八维情感分布，覆盖跨类别和类内情感转移。

Result: 与六个基线方法相比，实验在定量指标和定性比较中显示 AffectDelta 具有更好的情感对齐能力和内容保持能力；消融实验验证了源感知情感差值建模、转移编码器和扩散骨干等设计的有效性。

Conclusion: AffectDelta 表明，将图像情感编辑从单标签目标扩展为多维情感分布转移，可以实现更细粒度、更可控且更保持内容一致性的情感驱动图像编辑。

Abstract: Emotion-driven image editing aims to evoke a specified target emotion by modifying emotion-relevant visual cues in a source image, while preserving the overall composition and semantic-structural coherence of the original scene. Existing scene-level editors typically specify the target with a single emotion category and often learn visual transformations from operation-level text instructions. A category collapses a mixed affective endpoint into one dominant label, while language cannot precisely quantify how coexisting emotions should increase, decrease, or remain stable. We introduce AffectDelta, a source-aware editor that treats editing as a transition between eight-dimensional emotion distributions. A frozen Emotion Distribution Predictor estimates the source state, and the signed source-to-target difference encodes the direction and magnitude of the requested transition. Within AffectDelta, an internal transition encoder and a source-aware diffusion backbone jointly translate this signal into context-dependent semantic and appearance changes. To train this formulation, we construct AffectPair-249K, comprising 248,841 source-target pairs with predicted eight-dimensional distributions and spanning both cross-category and within-category transitions. Experiments against six baselines, combining quantitative evaluation with qualitative comparisons, demonstrate improved affective alignment and content preservation, while ablations validate our design choices. Code and dataset will be made publicly available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [89] [From Detection to Localization: A Unified Forensics Framework for Fully Synthetic and Tampered Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02640)
*Annalisa Gallina,Marco Fiorucci,Marco Brigo,Federica Battisti,Lamberto Ballan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个统一的多类别图像取证框架，同时区分真实图像、完全生成图像与局部篡改图像，并通过分割分支定位篡改区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式模型快速发展，使伪造图像更加逼真，传统二分类检测方法只能判断真实或生成，难以区分不同操纵类型，也无法有效进行像素级篡改定位，因此需要更细粒度、更统一的多任务取证方法。

Method: 在现有检测器基础上扩展为统一多分类框架，将任务设定为真实、完全生成、篡改三类判别；同时加入分割分支，对篡改图像中的被修改区域进行像素级定位，实现分类与定位的联合建模。

Result: 相较若干近期基准方法，该方法在分类准确率上表现更好，并在篡改区域定位任务中取得更高的 IoU 分数，说明其检测与定位能力均有所提升。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，将图像真实性分类与篡改区域分割整合到统一框架中，可以更有效应对生成式伪造和局部篡改检测问题，具有较好的多媒体取证应用价值。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of generative models has significantly worsened the problem of manipulated image detection, as these methods are capable of producing highly realistic forgeries, reinforcing the importance of multimedia forensics. Conventional approaches typically frame image manipulation detection as a binary classification task (real vs. generated), which limits the capability to distinguish and localize different forms of manipulation. To address these constraints, this work extends an existing detector by introducing a unified multiclass framework (real vs. fully generated vs. tampered). In addition to classifying image authenticity, the framework incorporates a segmentation branch to enable pixel-level localization of tampered regions. The proposed approach outperforms selected recent benchmarks, offering an efficient solution with improved classification accuracy and higher IoU scores for the localization task. Find the code at https://github.com/anngal01/From-Detection-to-Localization-A-Unified-Forensics-Framework-for-Fully-Synthetic-and-Tampered-Images.

</details>


### [90] [Learning to Attract and Repel: Dual Quality Margin Learning for Face Recognition (DQM-Face)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02644)
*El Ouanas Belabbaci,Bhavesh Wani,Philipp Terhörst*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 DQM-Face，通过结合特征幅值质量与语义质量，并引入吸引-排斥双重边界优化，提升非受控环境下人脸识别的判别性与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人脸识别中的 margin-based 方法通常用特征幅值建模样本质量，但仅依赖幅值容易受到与身份无关噪声影响，导致表示可靠性和判别能力下降。因此需要一种能更准确刻画样本质量、同时优化类内紧凑性与类间分离性的学习框架。

Method: 提出 Dual Quality Margin Learning for Face Recognition（DQM-Face）。方法将传统基于特征幅值的质量估计与新引入的语义质量学习结合起来，其中语义质量通过 squeeze-and-excitation 语义注意力机制实现。在此基础上构造质量感知 margin，以增强类内吸引；同时设计 repulsion margin 显式扩大类间间隔，形成统一的吸引-排斥 margin 优化框架。

Result: 在多个具有挑战性的基准数据集上，DQM-Face 持续优于现有先进人脸识别方法。此外，实验表明该框架中学习到的质量信号也能有效用于人脸图像质量评估，说明其与识别目标具有内在一致性。

Conclusion: DQM-Face 通过融合幅值质量和语义质量，并联合优化类内吸引与类间排斥，获得了更结构化、更具判别性的特征空间，是一种面向复杂真实场景人脸识别的有效方法。

Abstract: Face recognition in unconstrained environments remains highly challenging due to diverse and extreme variations encountered in real-world scenarios. To mitigate these effects, existing margin-based approaches model sample quality through feature magnitude. However, magnitude-based modeling alone is susceptible to identity-agnostic noise, which can degrade the reliability and discriminative power of learned representations. In this paper, we propose Dual Quality Margin Learning for Face Recognition (DQM-Face), a novel framework that enables refined attraction and repulsion dynamics during representation learning. Our approach unifies conventional magnitude-based quality estimation with a newly introduced semantic quality learning mechanism, realized via squeeze-and-excitation semantic attention. By jointly leveraging magnitude and semantic cues, we construct enhanced quality-aware margins that adaptively strengthen intra-class compactness through improved attraction during learning. To further enhance inter-class discrimination, we introduce a repulsion margin formulation that explicitly enlarges inter-class separation. The unified integration of semantic quality modeling with dual attraction-repulsion margin optimization results in a more structured and discriminative feature geometry. Extensive experiments on multiple challenging benchmarks demonstrate that DQM-Face consistently outperforms state-of-the-art face recognition methods. Moreover, we show that the quality learned for margin optimization is highly effective for face image quality assessment within the proposed framework, demonstrating that the learned quality signal is intrinsically aligned with the recognition objective. The code is publicly available: https://github.com/RAIB-group/DQM-Face

</details>


### [91] [Physics-Driven Independent Pair Generation for Iterative Self-Supervised Low-Dose CT Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02654)
*Xianlei Han,Shaoyu Wang,Jiancheng Fang,Weiwen Wu,Qiegen Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向低剂量CT去噪的物理驱动自监督框架，通过显式建模混合泊松-高斯噪声并进行跨域迭代，在无需干净标签的情况下提升去噪性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LDCT测量中存在混合泊松-高斯噪声，而多数现有自监督方法主要依赖通用图像统计特性，缺乏对真实CT噪声形成机制的显式建模，因此在真实低剂量噪声抑制方面可能受限。

Method: 方法包括三步：首先利用学习到的正弦图先验和LDCT噪声模型进行光子计数后验推断，分离泊松与高斯噪声成分；其次分别对泊松成分和高斯成分采用二项 thinning 与高斯数据 thinning，并通过残差缩放匹配噪声水平，从单次低剂量测量构造近似独立噪声训练对；最后用该训练对训练图像域网络，并将网络输出前向投影以更新正弦图先验，实现与CT采集物理一致的跨域迭代优化。

Result: 在AAPM、LIDC-IDRI、LoDoPaB-CT模拟数据以及真实LDCT数据上的实验表明，该方法在多个剂量水平下相较所评估的自监督基线取得稳定提升，性能接近所评估的监督式基线。

Conclusion: 显式结合LDCT噪声物理模型与跨域迭代机制能够有效提升自监督CT去噪能力，使模型在无配对干净数据条件下仍能获得接近监督方法的表现。

Abstract: Low-dose computed tomography (LDCT) measurements contain mixed Poisson-Gaussian noise. However, most self-supervised methods rely on generic image statistics and do not explicitly model this noise, which may limit their ability to effectively suppress realistic LDCT noise. To address this issue, we propose a physics-driven framework with cross-domain iteration for self-supervised LDCT denoising. The proposed framework proceeds in three main steps. First, a learned sinogram prior and the LDCT noise model guide posterior inference of photon counts, enabling separation of the Poisson and Gaussian components. Second, the separated Poisson and Gaussian components are respectively processed by binomial thinning and Gaussian data thinning to construct two branches, and residual scaling matches each branch's noise level to that of the observation, yielding a training pair with approximately independent noise realizations from one low-dose measurement. Finally, the pair is used to train an image-domain network whose forward-projected outputs update the prior. Through cross-domain iteration, the prior and the training pair are progressively refined while maintaining consistency with CT acquisition physics. Experiments on simulated data from AAPM, LIDC-IDRI, and LoDoPaB-CT and on real LDCT data show consistent gains over the evaluated self-supervised baselines across dose levels, with performance comparable to the evaluated supervised baseline.

</details>


### [92] [Characterizing Text Branch Sensitivity in Medical Vision-Language Segmentation via Evidence Decoupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02663)
*Ziquan Liu,Zhewei Zhu,Xuyang Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统研究文本信息在多模态医学图像分割中的实际作用，发现不同数据集对文本依赖程度差异显著，文本主要通过全局语义调制影响预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管预训练视觉-语言模型通过引入临床文本提升了医学图像分割表现，但文本信息究竟在多大程度上贡献于像素级预测仍不清楚，需要系统分析图像与文本模态的交互机制。

Method: 作者首先比较多种常用图文融合策略，分析分割性能对融合模块选择的敏感性；随后提出基于证据深度学习与深监督的 Evidence Decoupling Decoder（EDD），用于在解码过程中分解图像证据与文本调制证据，并作为内部表征分析工具。

Result: 实验表明，模型对文本扰动的敏感性在不同数据集间差异明显：BUSI 和 BTMRI 中移除文本会导致性能灾难性下降，说明模型强依赖文本；而在 ISIC 和 Kvasir-SEG 中，文本影响相对有限。融合模块选择对整体分割性能影响不大。

Conclusion: 文本在多模态医学图像分割中并非总是提供独立的空间定位能力，而更多通过全局语义调制影响预测；不同数据集中文本敏感性的驱动语义成分不同。该研究有助于理解多模态交互机制，并为未来模型设计提供实践启示。

Abstract: Pretrained vision-language models (VLMs) have shown promising performance in medical image segmentation by incorporating clinical text. However, it remains unclear how much textual information actually contributes to pixel-level predictions. In this work, we systematically investigate the role of text in multimodal medical image segmentation. We first analyze several commonly used fusion strategies and find that segmentation performance is largely insensitive to the choice of fusion module. To further understand modality interactions, we propose an Evidence Decoupling Decoder (EDD) based on evidential deep learning and deep supervision. EDD serves as an internal representation analysis tool that decomposes image evidence and text-modulated evidence throughout the decoding process while maintaining competitive segmentation performance. Experimental results show that the sensitivity to text perturbation varies substantially across datasets. On BUSI and BTMRI, removing text causes catastrophic performance drops, indicating strong model reliance on textual input. On ISIC and Kvasir-SEG, text exerts relatively marginal influence. We further find that text affects predictions mainly through global semantic modulation rather than independent spatial localization, and that the specific semantic components driving text sensitivity differ across datasets. These findings provide a deeper understanding of modality interaction in multimodal medical image segmentation and offer practical insights for future model design.

</details>


### [93] [Query Rewriting for Complex Object Segmentation in 4D Gaussian Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02664)
*Thanh-Khoi Nguyen,Thien-Phuc Tran,Minh-Triet Tran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究在4D Gaussian动态场景理解中，通过训练无关的查询重写将冗长叙述式查询压缩为关键词导向查询，以提升复杂对象分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有4D Gaussian语言引导动态场景理解方法对冗长、叙事化且包含噪声上下文的查询高度敏感，导致时间定位和空间分割不稳定。

Method: 提出一种无需训练的查询重新解释策略，逐步去除语言噪声，同时保留与对象中心表示相关的语义锚点，将长描述查询改写为简洁的关键词化形式。

Result: 在HyperNeRF和Neu3D上的实验表明，简洁重写查询显著提升时间定位与空间分割表现；平均时间准确率从60.92%提升至92.21%，平均vIoU从20.08%提升至76.94%，且无需额外微调。

Conclusion: 关键词聚焦、长度更短的查询能产生更稳定的视频特征相似度分布，并与对象中心Gaussian表示更好对齐，是提升4D Gaussian语言引导分割鲁棒性的有效训练无关方案。

Abstract: Recent 4D Gaussian representation frameworks have demonstrated strong performance in language-guided dynamic scene understanding. However, these methods remain highly sensitive to verbose and narrative-style queries that contain noisy contextual information. In this paper, we investigate the impact of query rewriting for complex object segmentation in 4D Gaussian representations. Inspired by recent findings in retrieval-augmented language models and keyword-guided query reformulation, we propose a training-free reinterpretation strategy that transforms long descriptive queries into concise keyword-grounded forms. Our approach progressively reduces linguistic noise while preserving semantic anchors relevant to object-centric representations. Experiments on HyperNeRF and Neu3D demonstrate that concise rewritten queries significantly improve both temporal localization and spatial segmentation performance. In particular, our method improves average temporal accuracy from 60.92% to 92.21% and average vIoU from 20.08% to 76.94% without any additional fine-tuning. Extensive ablation studies further reveal that shorter, keyword-focused queries consistently yield stable video-feature similarity distributions and better alignment with object-centric Gaussian representations

</details>


### [94] [Genesis: A Generative Engine for Hierarchical Satellite Image Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02683)
*Subash Khanal,Yangzhi Cui,Daniel Cher,Eric Xing,Brian Wei,Srikumar Sastry,Nathan Jacobs*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出多尺度瓦片补全任务，并用 Genesis 通过垂直超分辨率与水平外扩生成完整一致的卫星影像金字塔。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球观测天然具有多尺度特性，但现有卫星影像生成方法通常只沿单一维度工作：要么提升单个瓦片分辨率，要么在固定尺度上扩展空间范围，难以生成同时满足跨尺度一致性与邻域空间无缝性的完整瓦片金字塔。

Method: 定义 multi-scale tile completion：给定任意缩放层级和位置的稀疏种子瓦片，合成完整、统一的四叉树金字塔。提出 Genesis 生成引擎，在四叉树结构上组合两个专门算子：垂直方向的超分辨率模型用于跨 zoom 细化，水平方向的基于掩码的 outpainting 模型用于同尺度空间扩展。

Result: 两个算子在各自子任务上达到 state-of-the-art；Genesis 能从任意初始稀疏配置传播生成无缝、多分辨率地图。论文还引入 dense500 数据集和金字塔级评测指标，用于任务评估与基准测试。

Conclusion: 该工作将卫星影像生成从单尺度/单方向扩展到跨尺度与跨空间联合一致的金字塔生成，为多尺度地理空间生成建模提供了新任务、方法、数据集和评测框架。

Abstract: Earth observation is fundamentally multi-scale; geospatial tasks span varied resolutions, and satellite imagery is organized into cascading tile pyramids that nest fine detail within wide coverage. Current generative models of satellite imagery, however, operate along a single axis: they either zoom to enhance a single tile's resolution or pan to extend imagery at a fixed scale. As a result, no existing method produces a complete pyramid that stays consistent across both scale and space, where a high-zoom tile must agree with the coarse context it refines and with the neighbors it meets. Motivated by this gap, we introduce a new task, multi-scale tile completion: given a sparse set of seed tiles at arbitrary zoom levels and positions, synthesize a complete, uniform quadtree that is globally consistent across both scale and space. We approach this task with Genesis, a generative engine that brings both axes together by composing two specialized operators over the quadtree: a vertical super-resolution model and a horizontal mask-based outpainting model, producing pyramids that are consistent across zoom levels and seamless across neighboring tiles. Each operator achieves state-of-the-art results on its subtask, and the engine propagates sparse seeds into seamless, multi-resolution maps from any initial configuration. To evaluate the task and benchmark Genesis, we introduce dense500, a fully observed multi-scale pyramid dataset spanning diverse geographic regions, together with a suite of pyramid-level metrics. Code, models, and our dataset are available at https://github.com/mvrl/genesis.

</details>


### [95] [GaLe: memory-efficient Global Approximate and Local Exact features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02689)
*Alberto Ancilotto,Elisabetta Farella*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GaLe 是一种无需重训练的内存高效推理方法，可在资源受限嵌入式设备上部署预训练网络，同时尽量保持精确推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 嵌入式设备通常缺乏 GPU 级计算与内存资源；现有方法中，patch-based 推理计算开销高，而 approximation-based 方法容易造成精度下降，因此需要一种兼顾精度、速度和内存占用的部署方案。

Method: 论文提出将特征图划分为两类表示：局部精确表示 Le，用于保留细粒度局部细节；全局近似表示 Ga，用于保留长距离依赖。该设计区别于普通切块推理，能够支持混合 CNN-Transformer 模型中的全局操作与注意力机制，并且不需要对预训练模型进行重训练。

Result: 在 ImageNet 上，该方法达到接近或匹配精确推理的性能；在 Cortex-M33 上，相比 patch-based inference，最高实现 65% 加速和 90% RAM 降低。此外，该方法还在分类、检测和生成任务中展示了适用性。

Conclusion: GaLe 为资源受限设备上的高效神经网络推理提供了一种实用方案，在保持模型性能的同时显著降低内存与计算成本，并可能作为面向高效架构设计的基础技术。

Abstract: Embedded devices typically lack the resources of GPU-equipped machines, and existing inference methods suffer from either high computational overhead (patch-based) or accuracy loss (approximation-based). We propose GaLe, a memory-efficient technique that enables the deployment of pretrained networks on constrained devices without retraining. GaLe partitions feature maps into two components: a local exact (Le) representation that preserves fine details and a global approximate (Ga) representation that retains long-range dependencies. Unlike standard tiling, GaLe supports global operations and attention mechanisms found in hybrid CNN-transformer models. Validated on ImageNet, our method matches exact-inference performance while achieving up to 65% speedup and 90% RAM reduction on a Cortex-M33 compared to patch-based inference. We further demonstrate GaLe's versatility across classification, detection, and generation tasks, highlighting its potential as a foundation for resource-efficient architecture design.

</details>


### [96] [Generating Medical Image Counterfactuals using Causal Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02697)
*David A. Kelly,Tom Yaacov,Nathan Blake,Sander Beckers,Hana Chockler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需生成模型的医学图像反事实生成框架，直接利用分类器中的因果证据构造反事实图像，以提升可解释性并更透明地审计模型决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习在医学影像诊断中表现优异，但临床部署受限于可解释性不足。现有反事实解释方法多依赖 GAN、扩散模型等辅助生成模型，容易将分类器决策逻辑与生成器自身归纳偏置混淆，降低解释的直接性与可信度。

Method: 作者提出一种不需要额外生成模型的确定性反事实生成方法：从目标分类器中提取与预测相关的因果证据，并基于这些证据直接修改原始图像；同时支持在用户指定的感兴趣区域内进行可控编辑，且不需要额外模型训练。

Result: 在真实医学影像数据集上的实验表明，该方法能够成功改变分类器预测结果，并且相比生成式基线方法，生成的反事实图像与原始图像更接近。

Conclusion: 该方法为医学图像分类器提供了更直接、透明且可控的反事实解释，有助于分析模型决策边界，并减少由外部生成模型带来的解释偏差。

Abstract: Deep learning models have achieved impressive performance in medical image diagnosis, yet their deployment in clinical settings remains constrained by limited explainability. Counterfactual images provide one means of auditing model behavior by showing how an image would need to change for a classifier to produce a different prediction. Existing approaches typically generate such explanations using auxiliary models, including generative adversarial networks and diffusion models. While often capable of producing visually realistic images, these methods explain one black-box model using another, making it difficult to separate the classifier's decision-making process from the inductive biases of the generator.
  We propose a novel counterfactual-generation framework that requires no generative model. Instead, counterfactuals are constructed directly from causal evidence extracted from the classifier. The resulting approach is deterministic, requires no additional model training, and enables controllable edits within user-specified regions of interest. Experiments on real-world medical imaging datasets demonstrate that the proposed method successfully changes classifier predictions while remaining closer to the original image than generative baselines, providing a more direct and transparent view of the classifier's decision boundary.

</details>


### [97] [A Top-Down Framework for Metric-Scale Athlete Localization from Single Broadcast Frames](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02705)
*Thanh-Khoi Nguyen,Hoang-Phuc Nguyen,Linh-Huynh,Minh-Triet Tran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出一种用于单帧转播画面中运动员世界坐标定位的自顶向下框架，通过边界感知自适应切片和两关键点姿态估计，在公开测试集上显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超高分辨率体育转播图像中，运动员尺度差异极大，单帧中进行准确的度量级世界坐标定位很困难；常规切片推理容易产生目标被边界截断的问题，导致召回下降。

Method: 方法包括三部分：提出 Boundary-Aware Adaptive Tiling，根据粗略检测框迭代扩展切片边界以保证目标完整；将 RTMPose-X 改造为仅预测骨盆点与地面投影点的两关键点估计器，并重构 Gated Attention Unit 以适配几何耦合点对；最后利用相机标定参数通过射线投影将二维地面点确定性提升到世界坐标。

Result: 在公开测试集上取得 LocSim 97.44、mAP 0.9128，相比基线提升超过 21%。

Conclusion: 该框架有效缓解高分辨率极端尺度变化下的召回退化，并将残余误差主要归因于透视畸变，为单帧运动员世界坐标定位提供了稳健方案。

Abstract: Accurate world-coordinate localization of athletes from single-frame broadcast footage is inherently challenging due to extreme scale disparities in ultra-high-resolution imagery. In this paper, we propose a top-down framework for metric-scale athlete localization from a single calibrated frame. Our approach centers on three key contributions. First, we propose Boundary-Aware Adaptive Tiling, a semantics-guided extension of standard sliced inference. By iteratively expanding tile boundaries based on coarse bounding-box predictions, it systematically ensures full object containment, effectively mitigating boundary-splitting artifacts through a lightweight pipeline adaptation without architectural modifications. By substantially mitigating recall degradation under extreme scale variance, Boundary-Aware Adaptive Tiling enables us to isolate perspective distortion as the primary source of residual localization error. Second, we adapt the RTMPose-X architecture into a specialized two-keypoint estimator (pelvis and ground projection), employing a reformulated Gated Attention Unit optimized for this geometrically coupled point pair, and then deterministically lift the 2D ground projections into world coordinates via camera-calibrated ray casting. On the public test set, our method achieves a LocSim score of 97.44 and an mAP of 0.9128, outperforming the baseline by over 21 \% and establishing a robust solution for high-resolution scale variance.

</details>


### [98] [MV-dVRK: A Multi-Viewpoint Benchmark for Spatial Surgical Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02717)
*Guido Caccianiga,Sergey Prokudin,Yutong Chen,Bernard Javot,Rachael L'Orsa,Omer Burak Aladağ,Yarden Sharon,Jens Rolinger,Ivan Capobianco,Anton Deguet,Siyu Tang,Katherine J. Kuchenbecker*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 MV-dVRK，这是首个面向真实内窥镜图像的多视角手术 3D 重建基准数据集，并系统评估不同稀疏视角重建方法在外科场景中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大规模训练和优化技术推动了稀疏多视角 3D 重建发展，但这些方法尚未在真实内窥镜图像上严格评估；临床遥操作系统通常仅有单个体内双目相机，导致多视角数据稀缺，限制了手术感知研究。

Method: 构建 MV-dVRK 离体手术数据集，包含多台曝光同步双目内窥镜、准确表面几何和相机位姿；静态子集提供经工业 3D 扫描仪验证的稠密 SfM 参考几何、真值相机位姿和稀疏视角测试集；基于该数据集比较零样本单目、双目、多双目和多视角 3D 重建方法，并分析视角数量增加时的性能变化。

Result: 两台内窥镜设置下，多双目重建获得最高表面覆盖率；增加到第三视角后，基于优化的多视角方法表现最佳，在 1 mm 容差内覆盖 67% 的真值表面点，并恢复高精度相对相机位姿；相比之下，前馈式基础模型在相同设置下仅覆盖 43%。数据集还包含 10 个动态序列，覆盖多种手术任务、运动复杂度和组织形变。

Conclusion: MV-dVRK 填补了真实手术内窥镜多视角重建评测数据的空白，表明优化式多视角方法在增加视角后优于前馈基础模型，并为未来多视角手术感知、动态组织重建和相机位姿估计研究提供了重要基准。

Abstract: Large-scale training and refined optimization techniques have greatly improved sparse multi-view 3D reconstruction. Despite their relevance to surgery, such methods have never before been rigorously evaluated on real endoscopic images. Current clinical telerobots deploy a single stereo camera inside the patient, making multi-viewpoint data extremely rare. This paper presents MV-dVRK, the first ex-vivo surgical dataset to combine multiple exposure-synchronized stereo viewpoints with accurate surface geometry and camera poses. The static subset of the benchmark provides dense SfM reference geometry, validated against an industrial 3D scanner, together with ground-truth camera poses and sparse-view test sets. We use MV-dVRK to systematically compare zero-shot monocular, stereo, multi-stereo, and multi-view 3D reconstruction methods as the number of viewpoints increases. With two endoscopes, multi-stereo reconstruction achieves the highest coverage. With a third viewpoint, optimization-based multi-view methods perform best, covering 67% of ground-truth surface points within a 1 mm tolerance and recovering highly accurate relative camera poses. By contrast, feed-forward foundation models cover only 43% of the ground-truth surface in the same setting. MV-dVRK also includes ten dynamic sequences spanning multiple surgical tasks, with increasing kinematic complexity and tissue deformation, providing a basis for future research in multi-viewpoint surgical perception. The project is available at: https://mv-dvrk.is.mpg.de.

</details>


### [99] [InceptionGS: Generative Bootstrapping for Large-Scale Gaussian Splatting under Unstructured View Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02747)
*Tianheng Lu,Guangyu Wang,Ruqi Huang,Lu Fang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: InceptionGS 通过在高斯泼溅中自适应融合生成式先验，修复大规模场景中因视角稀疏造成的问题区域，同时保持已充分观测区域的重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模场景数字化需要在任意视角下获得一致且美观的渲染结果，但完整采集覆盖所有细节的多视角图像成本高且常不可行，导致视角分布高度非结构化，部分区域观测不足。现有重建方法在视角稀缺区域表现脆弱，而生成式方法又存在泛化性、可控性和 3D 一致性不足的问题。

Method: 提出 InceptionGS，从初始 Gaussian Splatting 出发，对因视角稀缺导致的问题区域进行识别、重新推理与修复；同时以软融合方式引入场景自适应和视角自适应的生成式先验，在重建约束与生成补全之间取得平衡。

Result: 在真实世界大规模场景上的大量实验表明，该方法能更好处理非结构化图像输入，并显著提升高保真 Gaussian Splatting 的渲染质量与适用范围。

Conclusion: InceptionGS 展示了重建式 3D 表示与生成式先验结合的有效性，尤其适用于大规模、视角覆盖不均衡的场景数字化任务。

Abstract: Achieving truly immersive large-scale scene digitization necessitates consistent and visually pleasing rendering across all possible viewing perspectives. However, collecting multi-view images covering every fine detail of a large-scale scene is prohibitive due to scene complexity, capture cost, negligence, or accessibility constraints. As a result, the sampled views tend to be highly unstructured -- the majority of the scene is well covered yet certain regions inevitably lack sufficient observations. Existing reconstruction based methods are vulnerable to view scarcity while generation based approaches suffer from generalization, controllability, and 3D consistency issues. To address this challenge, we propose InceptionGS, which bootstraps Gaussian splatting by subtly balancing reconstruction and generation. Starting from an initial Gaussian splatting, InceptionGS reasonably rethinks and repairs problematic regions caused by view scarcity while preserving the quality elsewhere, by softly incorporating scene- and view-adaptive generative priors. Extensive experiments on real-world large-scale scenes demonstrate the superiority and broad applicability of our approach in handling unstructured imagery and boosting high-fidelity Gaussian splatting. Please refer to the supplementary video for better visual demonstrations.

</details>


### [100] [Balancing Frequencies and Pixels in Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02748)
*Lucas Degeorge,Paul Couairon,Arijit Ghosh,Alexei A. Efros,David Picard,Vicky Kalogeiton*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 Focal Log-Frequency Loss，用频域平衡训练信号，缓解像素空间损失偏向低频的问题，在不改模型结构的情况下加速像素空间 flow models 收敛并提升感知质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然图像能量主要集中在低频，而纹理、边缘等关键感知结构位于稀疏高频；传统像素重建目标对所有空间误差等权处理，导致优化信号被低频主导，高频细节学习滞后。作者认为这是训练像素空间 flow models 的重要低效来源。

Method: 提出 Focal Log-Frequency Loss（f-loss），在频域中平衡不同频率的学习信号，增强原本在像素损失中被低估的高频成分；进一步采用阶段式训练策略：早期强调频域监督以覆盖各频段，后期切换到标准像素空间 v-loss 进行空间细化。该方法无需架构修改，可作为 flow matching 损失的即插即用替代。

Result: 在多个模型规模上，该方法最多可将收敛速度提升 40%，并稳定改善 FID 与感知保真度。

Conclusion: 目标函数层面的频谱不平衡会显著影响像素空间生成模型训练效率；通过频域平衡与像素域细化结合，可以更高效地学习全频段信息，尤其提升高频细节建模能力。

Abstract: Natural images follow a $1/f^2$ spectral distribution: most signal energy lies in the low spatial frequencies, while the perceptually important structures such as textures and edges occupy sparse high-frequency bands. Pixel-space reconstruction objectives, however, treat all spatial errors uniformly, causing low frequencies to dominate the optimization signal and delaying the learning of fine-scale details. In this work, we identify this objective-level spectral imbalance as a key inefficiency in training pixel-space flow models. To address it, we propose a Focal Log-Frequency Loss (f-loss), a spectrally balanced objective that equalizes the learning signal across frequencies, emphasizing high-frequency components that are otherwise underrepresented in pixel-space objectives. Building on this, we introduce a simple training strategy that combines frequency and pixel supervision: we first emphasize frequency-domain learning early to capture all frequencies, and then transition to standard pixel-space v-loss for spatial refinement. This balancing mitigates the low-frequency bias of pixel losses and aligns the training signal with the evolving needs of the model. Our approach is conceptually simple, requires no architectural changes, and acts as a drop-in replacement for flow matching losses. Across multiple model scales, it accelerates convergence by up to 40% while consistently improving FID and perceptual fidelity. We will release code and models.

</details>


### [101] [Multi-Tool Image Editing Attribution in Facial Forgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02751)
*Sheng Liu,Qiang Sheng,Danding Wang,Yu Li,Chenming Zhou,Juan Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究多工具图像编辑归因（MIEA），提出MultiEdit数据集与DPEC方法，用于识别同一人脸图像中涉及的多个编辑工具，并在多步编辑场景下优于九种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI工具使人像编辑变得容易，真实场景中一张图像可能经过多个工具连续编辑；现有归因方法通常假设单一编辑工具，难以处理多工具编辑中伪迹复合、重叠的问题。

Method: 作者构建包含50万以上编辑人脸图像的MultiEdit数据集，覆盖六类支持换脸与面部增强的编辑工具；提出DPEC方法，从空间域与频率域提取具有局部感知能力、可区分的编辑工具痕迹，并引入基于错误的课程学习策略提升多工具归因能力。

Result: 实验表明，DPEC在最多五步编辑的人脸图像上，相比九种现有方法取得更好的多工具归因性能。

Conclusion: 该工作首次系统探索多工具图像编辑归因任务，提供大规模数据集与有效方法，证明结合空间/频率线索和课程学习有助于识别复杂多步编辑痕迹。

Abstract: As generative AI tools become increasingly powerful and easy to use, people can easily edit portrait images with a prompt, necessitating the task of image editing attribution, which predicts the involved editing tools from the given image. Existing attribution methods hold the single-tool assumption and can only attribute a specific editing tool, but struggle to handle the more complex and increasingly common multi-tool editing scenarios, where artifacts left by different editing tools are composite and overlapped. To address this gap, we explore Multi-Tool Image Editing Attribution (MIEA), which aims to identify multiple editing tools involved in a multi-tool edited facial image. To simulate the real-life editing operations on facial images, we then construct a new dataset, MultiEdit, which contains 500k+ edited facial images and covers six types of editing tools that support face swapping (Deepfake) and various facial enhancements. Inspired by the findings from data analysis, we design DPEC, a multi-tool attribution method that can capture distinguishable, locality-aware editing tool traces from both spatial and frequency domains with the support of an error-based curriculum learning strategy. Experiments show \Method\ outperforms nine methods for facial images edited in at most five steps.

</details>


### [102] [Video-Based Palm-Vein Authentication under Challenging Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02776)
*Xiaofeng Yan,Kechen Liu,Abhilash Venkatesh,Cathy Zhang,Xia Zhou,Salvatore Stolfo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出首个公开的视频型掌静脉数据集CUP，用于研究真实部署中的表面污染、光照/运动变化与温度影响，并提出无需训练参数的时空鲁棒匹配方案，在多种条件下显著降低错误率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有掌静脉识别多在受控、干净条件下评估，缺乏覆盖湿、脏、温热等真实干扰条件的视频数据，导致模型在实际部署中的鲁棒性问题研究不足。

Method: 构建CUP数据集：每只手掌在干净、温热、湿润、脏污四种条件下采集视频，并配套生理与人口统计元数据；评测21种静态、视频和多帧聚合识别器；提出时间维度的多帧共识聚合以抑制瞬态噪声，以及空间维度的测试时匹配器，将全局余弦相似度与显著性引导的区域级最优传输匹配融合，绕开受污染区域。

Result: 干净条件下可靠的模型在脏污条件下精度大幅下降，平均EER约增至四倍；所提方法在CUP所有表面条件上取得最佳EER、TAR@FAR=0.01与Rank-1表现，仅需4.3M参数和3.1 GFLOPs；接入四个冻结SOTA骨干后无需重训即可将平均EER降低29%–37%；区域匹配在四个公开单图数据集上也有提升。

Conclusion: CUP填补了真实干扰条件下视频掌静脉数据的空白；结合多帧时间共识与显著性引导区域级匹配，可在低计算成本下显著增强掌静脉认证的鲁棒性，并揭示部分条件下仍存在需进一步研究的性能差异。

Abstract: Palm-vein biometrics are increasingly used for secure, contactless authentication. Yet real-world deployment exposes them to surface noise (sweat, dirt), illumination and motion variation, and temperature-driven changes in vascular visibility, which remain underexplored for lack of data captured under such conditions. To study these effects, we introduce the Columbia University Palm-vein (CUP) dataset, to our knowledge the first public video-based palm-vein dataset. CUP records every palm under four surface conditions (a clean baseline, warm, wet, and dirty) and pairs each subject with physiological and demographic metadata. On it we benchmark twenty-one recognizers spanning static, video, and multi-frame aggregation architectures. Models that verify reliably on clean palms lose most of their accuracy on dirty ones, and the mean equal error rate (EER) roughly quadruples. We recover much of that robustness along both axes of the capture. Temporally, a consensus over the few frames the sensor already returns cancels transient corruption; spatially, a test-time matcher that adds no learned parameters fuses the global cosine with a saliency-steered region-level optimal transport that routes the comparison around corrupted regions. The full design leads on every surface of CUP in EER, TAR@FAR=0.01, and Rank-1, at 4.3M parameters and 3.1 GFLOPs, a fraction of the video models' cost. Attached to four frozen state-of-the-art backbones it cuts their mean EER by 29-37% without retraining, and on four public single-image datasets the regional matching alone still helps. A preliminary audit across ten demographic and physiological traits finds two warm-condition gaps, along body water and gender, that survive multiple-comparison correction. CUP will be released for non-commercial research use at https://github.com/MobileX-CU/CUP_v1 upon publication.

</details>


### [103] [AutoCompass: Accurate Visual Localization on Public Maps by Learning from Weak Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02798)
*Javier Tirado-Garín,Alan Savio Paul,Shuai Chen,Axel Barroso-Laguna,Tommaso Cavallari,Daniyar Turmukhambetov,Victor Adrian Prisacariu,Eric Brachmann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AutoCompass 通过弱化对噪声绝对位姿标签的依赖，使用原始 GPS、位置容差区域以及可选的相对位姿监督来训练神经地图匹配器，并在多个基准上提升了 3-DoF 图像定位性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经地图匹配器需要从地理参考图像学习图像相对于二维地图的 3-DoF 位姿，但大规模数据集中的位置与朝向标签通常存在噪声，直接使用这些绝对位姿标签会损害模型训练效果与定位精度。

Method: 论文提出 AutoCompass 监督策略：首先证明训练时不必依赖朝向标签，模型仅从原始 GPS 位置标签即可自动学习较准确的朝向预测；其次，在原始 GPS 周围定义位置容差区域，以减轻位置标签噪声对监督信号的影响；最后，在条件允许时引入由 SLAM 或 SfM 获得的训练图像间相对位姿，提供比绝对标签更精确的监督。

Result: 在驾驶场景和第一视角场景的多个基准测试中，AutoCompass 相比传统强依赖绝对位姿标签的训练方法表现更好，位置与朝向估计均更稳健。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，对于神经地图匹配任务，精确的绝对朝向和位置标签并非训练的必要条件；通过合理处理 GPS 噪声并利用相对位姿信息，可以获得更可靠、更准确的地图匹配模型。

Abstract: Neural map matchers estimate an image's 3-DoF pose relative to a 2D map. These models are trained on large-scale datasets of geo-referenced images, whose position and heading labels often contain noise that affects the trained models. To address this, we present AutoCompass, a supervision approach for training neural map matchers from inaccurate absolute pose labels. First, we show that heading labels are unnecessary: trained from raw GPS labels, models learn to predict accurate headings, automatically. Second, defining a tolerance region around raw GPS improves positional accuracy. Third, if available, our supervision uses relative poses between training images, obtained via SLAM or SfM, which provide a more accurate training signal. Across driving and egocentric benchmarks, AutoCompass consistently outperforms counterparts trained with the usual strong reliance on absolute pose labels.

</details>


### [104] [GDB-Reward: From Evaluation Metrics to Training Rewards for Graphic Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02813)
*Adrienne Deganutti,Purvanshi Mehta,Simon Hadfield,Andrew Gilbert*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 GDB-Reward，将多种图形设计评估指标统一转化为强化学习奖励，用于优化冻结文本到图像生成器的提示，从而提升设计约束满足度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到图像模型在自然图像生成上表现较好，但在图形设计任务中难以精确满足字体、布局、颜色和视觉传达等约束。微调扩散模型成本高，而提示优化虽有吸引力，却需要在非可微生成过程中获得有效奖励信号。因此，作者关注能否将设计评估指标直接作为强化学习奖励。

Method: 提出 GDB-Reward 框架，系统地整合异构、非可微的图形设计评价指标，并将其转化为统一的标量强化学习奖励，用于对冻结图像生成模型的提示进行优化；强化学习仅依赖候选输出的奖励排序，不要求目标函数可微。

Result: 实验表明，GDB-Reward 能作为有效优化目标，在不更新图像生成器参数的情况下，显著提升生成结果对设计规范的遵循程度，包括感知质量、渲染保真度和空间准确性。

Conclusion: 该工作说明，异构且非可微的评估指标不仅可用于被动评测，也能作为强化学习中的有效优化目标，尤其适用于缺乏可微监督信号的图形设计生成等领域。

Abstract: Text-to-image models excel at natural image synthesis but struggle with graphic design, where success depends on satisfying precise constraints on typography, layout, color, and visual communication. While prompt optimization offers an attractive alternative to expensive diffusion model fine-tuning, learning prompts for frozen image generators requires informative reward functions despite the entirely non-differentiable generation process.
