Our results show that automated approaches can improve the realism of alignment evaluations, and that these improvements use additional compute more effectively than making the audits longer.

</details>


### [211] [SALA: Semantic-Aware Logical Alignment for Complex Reasoning in In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02336)
*Zhao Ji,Wenqing Chen,Zhixuan Chu,Jianxing Yu,Jingping Liu,Shanhe Zhao,Zibin Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SALA通过自动归纳任务特定推理操作，并在语义空间中用动态时间规整对齐推理序列，从而更灵活地为复杂推理型ICL选择示例，实验表现优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂推理中的上下文学习效果高度依赖示例选择。传统基于表层相似度的检索无法捕捉问题背后的解题逻辑；已有逻辑匹配方法依赖预定义推理步骤和精确匹配规则，难以适应灵活、多样的推理过程。因此需要一种既能表达推理逻辑、又支持软匹配和可解释对齐的方法。

Method: 提出Semantic-Aware Logical Alignment（SALA）框架：首先自动学习/归纳任务特定的推理操作，而不是使用固定操作清单；随后将这些推理操作嵌入连续语义空间；最后使用动态时间规整（DTW）对候选示例与目标问题的推理操作序列进行对齐，实现语义感知的柔性逻辑匹配，并保留较强可解释性。

Result: 在四个推理基准和三个大语言模型上的实验显示，SALA优于已有的示例选择方法。进一步分析验证了推理操作归纳和逻辑语义对齐两个核心组件的有效作用。

Conclusion: 该论文表明，面向ICL示例选择时，仅依赖表层相似度或刚性逻辑规则都不充分；通过自动归纳推理操作并进行语义级序列对齐，可以更准确地匹配问题求解逻辑，从而提升复杂推理任务中的模型表现。

Abstract: Effective in-context learning (ICL) for complex reasoning relies on selecting the right demonstrations. Traditional retrieval methods based on surface similarity fail to capture the underlying problem-solving logic. Recent logic-based methods address this by matching predefined reasoning steps, but the rigid rules and exact-match criteria is improper to handle flexible or diverse reasoning processes. To address the problem, we propose SALA, a Semantic-Aware Logical Alignment framework. Instead of relying on a fixed inventory, SALA automatically learns task-specific reasoning operations. It then embeds these operations into a continuous semantic space and uses dynamic time warping (DTW) to align the reasoning sequences. This approach allows for soft, flexible matching of reasoning logic while remaining highly interpretable. Experiments across four reasoning benchmarks and three LLMs demonstrate that SALA outperforms existing demonstration selection methods. Further analysis confirms the roles of the operation induction and the logical semantic alignment.

</details>


### [212] [Diagnosing with Insights: Structured Analysis of Agent Failures via Behavioral Abstractions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02371)
*Jiayi Bi,Yanjie Gao,Yuanmin Xie,Liqun Li,Tianyin Xu,Fan Yang,Mao Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AGENTSCOPE 通过将 LLM Agent 的长轨迹抽象为结构化表示，并结合“神经不变量”和 LLM 引导推理，实现对 Agent 失败步骤与失败类型的自动诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM Agent 的失败通常隐藏在冗长复杂的执行轨迹中，人工排查成本高；传统软件调试方法难以适配 Agent 行为，而完全依赖 LLM 作为评判器又不够可靠。因此需要一种更可靠、可解释的失败诊断方法。

Method: 论文提出 AGENTSCOPE，一种神经符号方法：首先基于 Agent 轨迹抽象其行为，形成结构化表示；随后引入神经不变量来刻画 Agent 行为属性；最后在结构化表示之上，利用 LLM 引导的推理与神经不变量进行对照，从而定位失败发生的具体步骤并判断失败类型。

Result: 在公开的 Agent 失败数据集 Who&When 以及作者构建的更全面数据集 AgentErrata 上，AGENTSCOPE 在故障定位和故障归因准确率方面显著优于当前最先进方法。

Conclusion: 结构化抽象与 LLM 引导推理的结合能够有效提升 LLM Agent 失败诊断的准确性、可靠性和可解释性，为复杂 Agent 系统的调试与可信部署提供了有价值的方法。

Abstract: With the proliferation of LLM agents, the ability to understand and diagnose failures in agents is essential to achieving superior effectiveness and trustworthiness. As agent failures often manifest via long and complex trajectories, manually finding the needles in the haystack is untenable. However, traditional diagnosis techniques for software bugs can hardly address LLM agent failures, while completely relying on LLMs as the judge yields unreliable diagnosis results. To overcome these challenges, this paper presents AGENTSCOPE, a new neuro-symbolic approach for agent failure mode diagnosis. The key principle of AGENTSCOPE is to abstract agent behavior, based on its trajectories, into structured representations. Furthermore, AGENTSCOPE introduces the concept of neural invariants to specify agent behavior properties. AGENTSCOPE leverages LLM-guided reasoning atop the structured representation against neural invariants to pinpoint both the failure step and its type in the trajectory. We show the effectiveness of AGENTSCOPE on publicly available agent failure datasets (Who&When) and a more comprehensive dataset created by us (AgentErrata), where AGENTSCOPE significantly outperforms the current state of the art in fault localization and attribution accuracy. Our work shows that integrating structured abstractions with LLM-guided reasoning enables effective, reliable, and interpretable diagnosis for agent failures.

</details>


### [213] [Contrastive Explanations in Quantitative Bipolar Argumentation Frameworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02399)
*Xiang Yin,Nico Potyka,Antonio Rago,Francesca Toni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出用于定量双极论证框架（QBAFs）的对比解释方法，用于解释两个主题论证之间结果差异，并构建了多类对比归因函数及其性质分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有QBAF解释方法多聚焦于单个主题论证的推理结果，难以回答“为什么一个论证的结果不同于另一个论证”这类对比性问题；而在分类、医疗决策和偏差识别等场景中，对比解释能提供更具诊断性和可理解性的说明。

Method: 作者提出一般形式的对比归因函数（CAFs），定义其应满足的一组通用性质；进一步构造了基于移除、梯度和Shapley值的CAF实例，并系统研究这些方法是否满足所提出的性质。

Result: 论文给出了QBAF中对比解释的形式化框架，展示了不同CAF方法在解释能力和理论性质上的差异，并通过医疗健康和偏差识别场景说明其应用价值。

Conclusion: 对比解释扩展了QBAF解释研究的范围，使解释对象从单一论证结果转向两个论证之间的差异；所提出的CAF框架为可解释AI中的论证式解释提供了更细粒度、可比较和可分析的工具。

Abstract: Argumentation frameworks are useful tools for representing and reasoning with information in a variety of settings, e.g. in supplementing AI models as they perform classification tasks, with a notable benefit of providing additional explainability. In this paper, we introduce contrastive explanations for Quantitative Bipolar Argumentation Frameworks (QBAFs), one such formalism. Unlike most existing explanations for QBAFs, which explain the reasoning outcome of a single argument of interest (i.e. a topic argument), contrastive explanations explain the difference between two topic arguments. We introduce a general form of contrastive attribution functions (CAFs) and establish a set of general properties they should satisfy. We introduce CAFs based on removal, gradients and Shapley-values, and study their properties. Finally, to illustrate contrastive explanations, we demonstrate their usefulness in healthcare and bias identification settings.

</details>


### [214] [UTP-Bench: Uncertainty-aware Travel Planning Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02421)
*Etcharla Revanth Rao,Priyanshu Karmakar,Shubhojit Mallick,Manish Gupta,Shreya Ghosh,Abhik Jana*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 UTP-Bench，用于评估大语言模型在不确定条件下生成旅行行程的鲁棒性，弥补现有旅行规划基准只考虑确定性约束的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实旅行规划存在交通延误、人流波动和随机等待等不确定因素，现有基准如 TravelPlanner 和 TripCraft 主要评估静态约束满足，无法衡量行程在扰动下是否仍可执行。

Method: 构建覆盖504个城市的大规模真实旅行数据集，包含景点、餐厅、住宿和多模式交通网络；引入基于经验数据的延误分布与人流密度模式；提出 BAS、CATS、TDAS 三个指标评估缓冲时间、人流敏感安排和交通延误吸收能力。

Result: 在 GPT-5、Qwen3、Mistral 和 Phi-4 等模型上的实验显示，模型生成计划与人工计划之间仍有显著差距，尤其体现在时间缓冲、延误感知交通安排和人流敏感规划方面。

Conclusion: UTP-Bench 为不确定环境下的旅行规划提供了更贴近现实的评测框架，揭示当前大语言模型在鲁棒行程生成方面仍存在明显不足。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have recently demonstrated strong capabilities in automated travel itinerary generation. However, real- world travel planning is inherently uncertain: transportation delays, crowd fluctuations, and unexpected stochastic delays frequently inval- idate otherwise feasible schedules. Existing benchmarks like TravelPlanner and TripCraft assume deterministic environments, evaluating only static constraint satisfaction and ignoring whether generated plans remain robust when such uncertainties arise. To address this limitation, we introduce UTP-Bench1 , a large-scale benchmark for uncertainty-aware travel planning. The dataset integrates real-world travel data spanning 504 cities of India, including attractions, restau- rants, accommodations, and multi-modal trans- portation networks. To model realistic disrup- tions, UTP-Bench incorporates empirical delay distributions and crowd-density patterns col- lected from major cities, enabling evaluation of travel plans under stochastic conditions. We further propose three evaluation metrics, namely Buffer Adequacy Score (BAS), Crowd- Aware Timing Score (CATS), and Transport Delay Absorption Score (TDAS), which quan- tify the ability of generated itineraries to main- tain robustness against transit delays and crowd variability. Experiments with state-of-the-art LLMs like GPT-5, Qwen3, Mistral and Phi-4 re- veal substantial gaps between model-generated and human-authored plans, particularly in tem- poral buffering, delay-aware transportation scheduling, and crowd-sensitive planning.

</details>


### [215] [CivBench: A Long-Horizon Benchmark for Tool-Mediated Agents in Civilization VI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02459)
*Austin Tudor David Andrews,Liam Wilkinson,Jamie Heagerty,Harry Coppock,Jakob Nicolaus Foerster,Rui Ponte Costa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出开源基准 CivBench，用于评估语言模型智能体在基于 MCP 的长程、工具介导环境中的规划、监控与执行能力；试点结果显示智能体普遍存在战略状态监控不足和计划承诺执行不稳定的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评测难以覆盖长时程、多工具、大动作空间、部分可观测环境中的智能体行为。作者希望构建一个可复现的开放基准，分析语言模型智能体在持续规划、状态查询和工具执行方面的实际表现。

Method: 作者构建 CivBench：单个 episode 超过 300 轮、产生数千次工具调用，环境提供 76 个 MCP 工具，并通过叙事层将视觉游戏状态转为结构化文本。研究在 23 次可采纳运行中比较四个模型家族，但定位为试点分析而非模型排名；同时提出 PMR 和 RAG@10 两个接口级指标。

Result: 实验发现，在共享 playbook 协议下，智能体未能充分主动查询关键战略状态：尽管要求每 20 轮查询胜利进度，实际间隔为 30 到 75 轮；在 20 次可检测失败中有 7 次未在失败前 20 轮窗口内查询。此外，智能体对自身规划反思中近期待办承诺的执行率有限，RAG@10 在不同模型间为 48.2% 至 65.8%。

Conclusion: CivBench 展示了长程工具环境中智能体评测的可行性，并揭示当前模型即使具备工具访问和明确指导，也会在主动监控与计划执行上偏离指令。作者认为这些问题更像是遵循指令和长期执行稳定性的偏差，而非能力完全缺失。

Abstract: We present CivBench, an open-source benchmark for evaluating language model agents in long-horizon, tool-mediated environments through the Model Context Protocol (MCP). A single episode spans 300+ turns and produces thousands of tool calls over a large action space, requiring sustained planning, state monitoring, and execution under partial observability. The environment exposes 76 MCP tools and a narration layer that converts visual game state into structured text.
  We use CivBench to characterise agent behaviour across four model families in 23 admissible runs. The sample is a pilot, not a model ranking: aggregate outcomes do not reliably discriminate models at this scale. Instead, we introduce two interface-level metrics that the environment makes measurable: Proactive Monitoring Rate (PMR), capturing whether agents actively query latent strategic state, and RAG@10, capturing whether commitments stated in structured planning reflections are executed within ten subsequent turns.
  Across runs we observe two consistent patterns under a shared playbook protocol. Agents under-monitor strategically relevant state that is available but requires explicit querying: despite playbook guidance to query victory progress every 20 turns, agents do so only every 30 to 75 turns, and in 7 of 20 detectable defeats they failed to query within the 20 turn warning window before game end. Agents also frequently fail to execute near-term commitments stated in their own planning reflections (RAG@10 between 48.2% and 65.8% across models). Both patterns arise despite tool access and explicit guidance, and we interpret them as deviations under instruction rather than absences of capability.
  We release the environment, scenarios, logs, metrics, and analysis pipeline at https://github.com/lmwilki/civ6-mcp

</details>


### [216] [Collective creativity in hybrid societies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02620)
*Mason Youngblood,Katie Mudd,Manuel Anglada-Tort,Cameron Jones,Elena Miu,Diana Omigie,Margaret Schedel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文认为，生成式AI对创造力的影响不能简单归结为促进或损害，而应区分个体作品的新颖性与群体层面的多样性，并从人机混合集体的组成与连接方式来理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前研究对生成式AI是否丰富或削弱文化创造存在分歧。作者认为分歧源于将创造力中的两个不同维度混淆：单个作品的新颖性与作品群体的多样性，因此需要新的分析框架。

Method: 论文提出概念性分析框架：将生成式AI语境下的创造力视为由人类与算法交互构成的混合集体属性，而非单个个体属性；并从群体组成、比例与连接结构解释创造结果。

Result: AI辅助构思通常提升单个产出的新颖性，但可能降低总体多样性。不过这并非必然，因为人类与模型的搜索方式具有互补性，混合群体可能在表现和多样性上优于纯人类或纯机器群体。

Conclusion: 关键问题不再是AI究竟帮助还是伤害创造力，而是何种人机组合能够在提升个体创造收益的同时，避免削弱集体层面的文化多样性。

Abstract: Generative AI is changing how cultural artifacts are created and circulated, and with it our understanding of creativity itself. Researchers disagree about whether these tools enrich or impoverish culture, and we argue that much of that disagreement comes from conflating two distinct components of creativity: novelty, a property of single artifacts, and diversity, a property of populations. We argue further that creativity in the context of generative AI is best understood as a property of hybrid collectives, or populations of interacting people and algorithms, rather than of individuals. AI-assisted ideation reliably raises the novelty of individual output while narrowing diversity in the aggregate, but this is not an inevitable consequence of putting machines in the loop. Because humans and models search in complementary ways, mixed groups can outperform and out-diversify groups of either kind alone, and machine-discovered solutions can enter human culture and persist there. What decides the outcome is composition: which agents are present, in what proportion, and how they are connected. The question is no longer whether AI helps or harms creativity, but which mixtures let individual gains accumulate without eroding collective diversity.

</details>


### [217] [Loom: Weaving Diagnostic Strands into Free-Text Consensus via Embedding-Space Reweighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02649)
*Ron Begleiter,Katya Egert Berg,Gilad Saban,Gil Shabat*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Loom 是一个面向真实根因分析（RCA）的生成式共识框架，通过聚合多个模块化启发式方法产生的开放式文本假设，在显著降低 LLM 调用次数和延迟的同时保持接近先进自治代理的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业场景中的 NLP/RCA 系统需要从嘈杂、冲突的文本假设中形成可靠结论。单体 LLM 代理表达能力强，但受上下文长度、幻觉累积和推理延迟限制；传统弱监督具备统计严谨性，但通常局限于离散类别，难以处理开放式文本假设。

Method: 论文提出 Loom：先由模块化启发式诊断模板生成包含事件实体、时间和指标的开放式假设；再将这些假设投影到连续嵌入空间；使用迭代的基于质心的重加权算法解决冲突信号并生成共识权重；最后用这些权重约束一次轻量级 LLM 综合生成最终 RCA 结论。

Result: 在 OpenRCA 基准上，Loom 位于准确率—效率帕累托前沿：在 Bank 和 Market-2 数据集上匹配当前先进自治代理，在 Market-1 和 Telecom 上略落后；所有数据集每个事件仅需一次 LLM 调用，约快 26 倍，使用 8B 参数综合器时约快 33 倍。

Conclusion: Loom 展示了在开放式 RCA 中结合弱监督式共识聚合与轻量 LLM 综合的可行性，能够在保持较高准确率的同时显著降低推理成本和延迟；其确定性共识机制也有助于提升领域专家信任，但仍需权衡代理深度与延迟，并面对冗余检测等负面结果。

Abstract: Aggregating noisy, conflicting textual hypotheses into a reliable consensus is a fundamental challenge when deploying NLP systems in real-world industrial settings. While monolithic Large Language Model (LLM) agents offer unbounded expressivity for tasks like Root Cause Analysis (RCA), they suffer from context limits, compounding hallucinations, and prohibitive inference latency. Traditional weak supervision offers statistical rigor but is mathematically restricted to discrete classes. We present Loom, a generative consensus framework deployed for real-world RCA that bridges these paradigms. Loom aggregates open-form hypotheses emitted by modular heuristics (diagnostic templates dynamically populated with episode-specific entities, times, and metrics) by projecting them into a continuous embedding space, and resolves conflicting signals with an iterative centroid-based reweighting algorithm. The resulting consensus weights ground a single lightweight LLM synthesis step. Evaluated on the OpenRCA benchmark, Loom occupies the accuracy--efficiency Pareto frontier: it matches a state-of-the-art autonomous agent on Bank and Market-2 and trails on Market-1 and Telecom, while using a single LLM call per incident on all four datasets ($\sim$26$\times$ faster; $\sim$33$\times$ with an 8B-parameter synthesizer). We discuss our deployment experience, highlighting lessons learned regarding the trade-offs between agentic depth and inference latency, negative results in redundancy detection, and how deterministic consensus fosters trust among Subject Matter Experts~(SMEs).

</details>


### [218] [Door-in-the-Face Requests and Refusal Behaviour in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02707)
*Til Jordan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究“先大后小”（door-in-the-face）说服技巧是否会影响语言模型的拒答/遵从行为。结果显示该效应强烈依赖模型家族：部分模型在先拒绝较大请求后更可能回答较小请求，而另一些模型反而更不愿回答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类社会心理学中，大请求被拒后提出相关小请求，会提高对方同意概率。论文希望检验这种人类影响技巧是否能迁移到生产级语言模型，并进一步理解模型拒答机制是否会受对话上下文与请求重写方式影响。

Method: 作者测试来自三家提供商的九个生产模型：先让模型拒绝一个较大请求，再提出同主题较小请求，并与直接提出较小请求的基线比较遵从率。同时设置不相关主题的大请求作为控制条件，并测试来自公开基准的拒答样本是否具有迁移性；另将被拒请求改写为同主题解释型请求，观察拒答是否消失。

Result: 结果因模型而异：Anthropic 的前沿模型上该技巧有效，例如 Opus 5 在先拒大请求后回答小请求的比例为 65.8%，直接询问为 29.3%；而 OpenAI、Google 的前沿模型以及 Haiku 4.5 上该技巧适得其反，使遵从率下降 15.5–23.0 个百分点。相关大请求比不相关大请求影响更强，说明“让步/收缩请求”本身起作用；但不同模型家族对刚刚拒绝后的状态反应不同。公开基准中的拒答样本未能迁移。将 265 个被拒的可操作指令请求改写为同主题解释请求后，263 个不再被拒。

Conclusion: 论文认为，人类影响技巧并不能统一地迁移到所有语言模型，而是取决于模型家族、请求类型和安全策略触发方式。决定“退让”是否有效的关键并非仅是对话顺序，而是请求具体要求模型做什么。

Abstract: Does the door-in-the-face technique work on language models? In humans, a large request that is refused makes a smaller follow-up request more likely to be granted. We test this on nine production models from three providers: each model refuses a large request, then receives a smaller version of the same request, and we compare its compliance with asking directly. The answer depends on the model. On Anthropic's frontier models the technique works: Opus 5 answers the smaller request 65.8% of the time after refusing the larger one, against 29.3% when asked directly. On the frontier models of OpenAI and Google, and on Haiku 4.5, it backfires, lowering compliance by 15.5 to 23.0 points. A control locates the effect: a refused large request on an unrelated topic does less than the related one on all nine models, so the concession itself matters everywhere, while the reaction to having just refused something differs by model family. The technique does not transfer to refusals drawn from public benchmarks. What decides whether a retreat can work is what the request asks for: rewriting 265 refused requests for usable instructions into requests for explanations of the same topic removed the refusal in 263 cases. Human influence techniques port to language models one model family at a time.

</details>


### [219] [Repo-To-Skill: Distilling GitHub Repositories Into AI4AI Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02749)
*Jianlyu Chen,Yuyang Hu,Hongjin Qian,Jiawei Liu,Wenqing Wei,Xiaolong Chen,Defu Lian,Zhicheng Dou,Chaozhuo Li,Qiwei Ye,Zheng Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 DisCo：一种“技能驱动”的机器学习研究智能体，通过将分散在代码库和论文中的操作性知识蒸馏为紧凑、可验证、可复用的技能库，在固定模型骨干、研究框架和执行预算下显著提升自动化科研任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端 ML 研究智能体通常具备模型骨干、规划、执行、记忆与验证模块，但仍缺少领域实践中的操作性知识，即如何把方法真正调通、做稳、做有效的经验。这些知识虽存在于开源仓库和论文中，但面向人类阅读、规模庞大，难以在具体任务中直接加载和复用，因此需要被压缩成可验证技能。

Method: 作者提出 DisCo，一个能够创建并使用技能的研究智能体。其知识蒸馏分为两类：任务无关蒸馏，从广泛使用的 ML 仓库中提炼通用可复用技能；任务导向蒸馏，针对具体研究任务生成所需技能。任务无关蒸馏形成 AREX-Skill Library，包含从 1000 个常用 ML 仓库中提炼出的 5000 多个已验证技能，并按 20 个领域和 178 个能力族组织。

Result: 在 GPT-5.5 骨干、研究框架和下游执行预算均保持不变的条件下，加入技能后的研究智能体相比无技能版本，在 MLE-bench 上提升 134.3%，在 PaperBench 上提升 34.4%，在 FrontierCS 上提升 9.2%，在 PassNet 上提升 14.0%。

Conclusion: 研究表明，将领域操作性知识蒸馏为紧凑、可验证、可复用的技能，可以作为智能体架构中的关键补充层，在不改变底层模型和执行预算的前提下显著增强自动化机器学习研究能力。

Abstract: Autonomous agents are beginning to carry out machine-learning (ML) research end to end. These agents combine a model backbone with a harness for planning, execution, memory, and verification, but this architecture still leaves domain-specific know-how outside the agent. We call this missing layer operational knowledge, the know-how that separates knowing a method from making it work. That knowledge is not absent from the field. It appears in repositories and papers, but in forms written for human readers and too large to load during a task. Once distilled into compact, verified skills, this knowledge can be reused across tasks rather than rediscovered during each run.
  We present DisCo, a skill-powered research agent that creates skills and uses them during research. Its distillation runs in two complementary forms: task-agnostic, condensing the field's widely used repositories into reusable skills, and task-oriented, producing the skills a concrete task calls for. The former, applied across the open ecosystem, yields the AREX-Skill Library, with 5,000+ verified skills distilled from 1,000 widely used ML repositories and organized into 20 areas and 178 capability families. With the GPT-5.5 backbone, research harness, and downstream execution budget held fixed, the skill-equipped research agent scores 134.3% higher on MLE-bench, 34.4% higher on PaperBench, 9.2% higher on FrontierCS, and 14.0% higher on PassNet than the same agent without skills. These gains come from adding distilled operating context under that fixed setup.

</details>


### [220] [Bilevel Coordinated Reflection: A Game-Theoretic Approach to Multi-Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02750)
*Yihang Chen,Yuxiang Chen,Yuxuan Huang,Meng Fang,Weilin Luo,Jun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文为多智能体 LLM 系统提出统一理论框架，将编排器-工作器交互建模为双层协调博弈，并提出 SRMA 反思记忆更新机制，以环境落地评估门控提升可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体 LLM 系统常依赖编排器分解任务、工作器执行并通过文本反思改进，虽有较好经验效果，但缺乏对协调机制、记忆改进过程以及外部验证作用的统一理论解释。

Method: 作者将编排器-工作器交互形式化为双层协调博弈，证明在有界耦合下工作器局部更新近似势博弈；将反思建模为语义记忆状态上的随机运动；分析仅基于文本转录的门控限制，并提出基于环境落地风险下降准则的 Stochastic Reflective Memory Ascent（SRMA），同时扩展到随机评估置信门控与分段平稳环境重锚定。

Result: 理论上给出自由形式反思的有限时间上界、最坏情况紧性、持久伤害条件下的正下界，以及文本不可区分环境中的信息论不可能性结果；证明 SRMA 在校准和非退化纠错质量条件下可精确收敛，收敛速率可为几何或多项式且阶紧。实验中，在 500 个 SWE-bench 实例上，完整 Kimi 系统解决率为 72.2%，高于公开 mini-SWE-agent 参考的 70.8%。

Conclusion: 论文认为，多智能体 LLM 的有效改进关键不只是文本反思本身，而是需要环境落地验证来筛选记忆更新；SRMA 为反思记忆优化提供了理论可证且实验可验证的机制。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems commonly use an orchestrator to decompose a task for a team of workers and then improve through textual reflection. Despite strong empirical results, these systems lack a unified account of coordination, memory improvement, and the role of external verification. We model orchestrator-worker interaction as a bilevel coordination game: under bounded coupling, the workers' local-update game is an approximate potential game whose equilibrium slack is controlled by decomposition quality. We then analyse reflection as stochastic movement over semantic memory states. For free-form reflection, we derive a finite-time upper bound, prove worst-case tightness, and give a positive lower bound under a falsifiable persistent-harm condition. We further prove an information-theoretic impossibility result: no gate that observes only the generated transcript can improve uniformly over text-indistinguishable environments, whereas an environment-grounded gate can. Motivated by this separation, we introduce Stochastic Reflective Memory Ascent (SRMA), which accepts a candidate memory only after a grounded evaluation risk strictly decreases. Under calibration and non-degenerate corrective mass, SRMA converges exactly, geometrically or polynomially; matching constructions show that both rate regimes are order-tight. We also provide confidence gating for stochastic evaluation and re-anchoring guarantees for piecewise-stationary environments. Experiments instantiate these objects with environment-grounded metrics and test the predicted coordination and drift laws. On 500 SWE-bench instances, the complete Kimi-based system resolves 72.2% versus a 70.8% public mini-SWE-agent reference. Code: https://github.com/YihangChen9/Bilevel-Coordinated-Reflection

</details>


### [221] [Measurement-Driven Sub-Network Selection for On-Premise Retrieval-Augmented Factory Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02760)
*Vasileios Rizeakos,Georgios Paisios,Alexandros Machairas,Michael Birbas,Athanasios Bachoumis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究如何将面向工厂工人的本地化文档问答助手部署到资源受限的车间边缘硬件上。核心发现是：经过结构压缩和检索增强适配后，模型参数规模不再可靠预测问答质量，因此应在适配后按设备约束进行子网络选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能力模型通常难以适配车间现场硬件的内存、功耗和吞吐限制；而工厂场景需要在本地访问机器文档并进行可靠对话问答，因此需要一种兼顾质量、通用能力、速度、内存和功耗的部署方法。

Method: 作者采用结构压缩与检索增强适配，并训练一个权重共享的超网络；通过 sandwich-style in-place distillation 降低多个子网络评估和选择成本。部署时，将问题建模为后适配选择：在可配置的通用能力下限和内存预算下，依据人工/评测判定的检索增强回答质量与设备实测吞吐，为每类设备选择一个子网络。

Result: 实验显示，通用能力几乎随参数量线性下降，但检索增强后的回答质量并不随模型大小单调变化。制造手册案例中，子网络抽取导致评测质量损失为未剪枝模型的 13.7%，检索增强蒸馏将其恢复到距原模型仅 4.6%，约追回三分之二损失；同一助手可运行于三类异构边缘设备，待机功耗约 1.3–5 W。

Conclusion: 在检索增强和任务适配后，单纯按模型大小、速度或质量选择部署模型都不是最优策略；更合理的是进行面向设备约束的后适配子网络选择。权重共享超网络与原位蒸馏可高效支持这种多设备部署。

Abstract: On-premise assistants can give factory workers conversational access to machine documentation, but models capable of the task rarely fit shop-floor hardware. We show that after structural compression and retrieval-grounded adaptation, model size is no longer a reliable predictor of adapted answer quality: general capability falls almost linearly with parameter count, while judged retrieval-augmented answer quality does not. We therefore treat deployment as a post-adaptation selection problem, committing one sub-network per device on judged answer quality and measured on-device throughput under a configurable general-capability floor and memory budget; rules that optimize size, speed, or quality alone each give up capability or throughput. A weight-shared supernetwork trained with sandwich-style in-place distillation keeps this selection inexpensive. In a manufacturing-manual case study, extraction costs 13.7 percent of the unpruned model's judged quality and retrieval-grounded distillation returns it to within 4.6 percent, recovering two thirds of the loss, and the same assistant runs across three heterogeneous edge tiers at 1.3 to 5 watts standby.

</details>


### [222] [SafeEvolve: Harness-Policy Co-Evolution from Agent Experience for Safety Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02786)
*Qinghua Mao,Wanying Qu,Dadi Guo,Leitao Yuan,Qingyu Liu,Yu Li,Guanxu Chen,Yanwei Fu,Xi Lin,Xia Hu,Dongrui Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SafeEvolve 是一个面向 LLM 智能体安全对齐的经验驱动自进化框架，通过运行时 harness 与模型策略的协同演化，将多步交互轨迹中的安全经验转化为可审计的安全组件更新和更安全的策略行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体的安全性不仅取决于基础模型，也取决于与环境交互时使用的 harness。现有方法通常单独依赖外部 harness 更新或策略优化，难以同时实现运行时控制与模型内在安全能力的提升，因此需要一种统一的协同对齐机制。

Method: 提出 SafeEvolve：在 harness 侧，将已完成 on-policy 轨迹中的安全证据转化为有界的组件级更新，包括安全提示与层级技能，形成可审计、可回滚的 harness artifacts；在 policy 侧，采用两阶段 SFT-RL 范式，先通过 harness-use SFT 让策略学会主动使用演化后的 harness，再通过 harness-augmented RL 和 verifier-decomposed rewards 在多步探索中强化自主安全行为。

Result: 在 agentic safety benchmarks 上，SafeEvolve 相比基线取得更好的安全-效用权衡。以 Qwen3.5-4B 为例，在 AgentDojo 上 ASR 降低 3 倍，同时 benign utility 从 59.79% 提升到 61.86%。

Conclusion: SafeEvolve 表明，将轨迹级安全经验同时反馈到运行时 harness 与模型策略中，可以更有效地提升 LLM 智能体在多步执行场景下的安全性，并保持甚至改善正常任务效用。

Abstract: The performance of LLM-based agents is jointly shaped by the base model and the harness used when interacting with the environment. This exposes them to safety risks in both harmful final responses and multi-step execution trajectories. Existing safety alignment mechanisms often rely on either external harness updates or policy optimization, yet applying either paradigm in isolation fails to bridge runtime control with intrinsic safety. We propose SafeEvolve, an experience-driven self-evolving framework for agent safety alignment. SafeEvolve leverages safety experience from completed on-policy trajectories to drive a continual loop of harness-policy co-evolution. On the harness side, SafeEvolve converts trajectory-level safety evidence into bounded, component-level updates across safety prompt and hierarchical skills, yielding auditable and reversible harness artifacts. On the policy side, SafeEvolve follows a two-stage SFT-RL paradigm, where harness-use SFT bootstraps the policy to actively leverage evolved harness artifacts, and harness-augmented RL further shapes autonomous safety behaviors during multi-step exploration via verifier-decomposed rewards. Through harness-policy co-evolution, SafeEvolve converts safety experience into an evolved runtime harness and improved policy behavior. Experiments on agentic safety benchmarks show that SafeEvolve achieves a stronger safety-utility tradeoff than existing baselines. For Qwen3.5-4B, SafeEvolve achieves a $3\times$ ASR reduction on AgentDojo while improving benign utility from 59.79% to 61.86%.

</details>


### [223] [Large Language Models (LLMs) for Telecom Root Cause Analysis (RCA): A Structured Reasoning Framework for Evidence-Grounded Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02805)
*Hao Zhou,Mandar Kulkarni,Hao Chen,Yan Xin,Charlie,Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述电信网络根因分析从规则/机器学习到LLM方法的演进，并提出一种面向5G/6G RCA的结构化推理框架，通过规范化上下文、决策路径约束和证据支撑解释提升诊断可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代5G及未来6G网络存在复杂跨层依赖，传统RCA方法难以处理复杂性能退化；直接使用通用LLM又容易产生幻觉、推理不稳定、与结构化网络证据对齐不足，因此需要更可靠、可验证、面向领域证据的LLM诊断方法。

Method: 论文先回顾电信RCA技术演进与LLM相关范式，包括结构化推理、检索增强、智能体编排和可验证推理；随后提出结构化推理框架：将异构网络遥测数据组织为规范化上下文，在诊断阶段强制执行决策路径推理，并生成基于证据的解释以支持故障识别。

Result: 在两个5G RCA数据集TeleLogs和TelecomTS上的实验显示，该框架相较基线方法在诊断准确率和决策一致性方面均有稳定提升，且具备跨数据集有效性。

Conclusion: 结构化推理设计对于实际LLM驱动的下一代电信网络RCA系统至关重要，可缓解幻觉和不稳定推理问题，并提升诊断可信度与可解释性。

Abstract: Root cause analysis (RCA) is a critical task in telecom network operations, but diagnosing performance degradations in modern 5G and emerging 6G networks remains challenging due to complex cross-layer dependencies. While large language models (LLMs) offer promising capabilities for reasoning and knowledge integration, directly applying vanilla LLMs to telecom RCA often leads to hallucination, unstable reasoning, and poor alignment with structured network evidence. This work first reviews the evolution of telecom RCA from rule-based and machine learning (ML) approaches to emerging LLM-enabled techniques, and provides an overview of recent paradigms, including structured reasoning, retrieval-augmented knowledge grounding, agentic orchestration, and verifiable reasoning. Building upon these insights, we propose a structured reasoning framework for LLM-enabled telecom RCA that aligns diagnostic reasoning with telecom-specific evidence and domain knowledge. The proposed approach first organizes heterogeneous network telemetry into canonical contexts, and then enforces decision-path reasoning during diagnosis, and finally generates evidence-grounded explanations for reliable fault identification. Experimental results on two 5G RCA datasets, TeleLogs and TelecomTS, demonstrate that the proposed framework consistently improves diagnostic accuracy and decision consistency compared with baseline techniques. These cross-dataset results highlight the importance of structured reasoning design for practical LLM-based RCA systems in next-generation telecom networks.

</details>


### [224] [AI Contextual Measurement for Recovering Individual and Group-Level Effects: Validation Against Survey Measures and an Occupational Application](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02821)
*Wenxin Jiang,Xuyang Wang,Yuxiao Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 AICOME（AI Contextual Measurement）框架，用于评估 AI 从既有数据中构造的个体层面测量能否在情境模型中恢复个体内差异与群体间差异效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统调查常缺少社会、组织和职业情境特征变量；研究者开始用 AI 构造这些缺失测量，但现有做法往往只关注响应预测，缺乏对 AI 测量能否支持群体层面与个体层面效应估计的系统评估。

Method: 作者提出将 AI 派生的 respondent-level measure 分解为群体聚合值与个体偏离值，以分别估计 between-group 与 within-group 关联。使用 2022 年 CFPS 数据，以职业作为分组结构，并以计算机使用、外语使用、每周工作时长、管理职责等调查变量作为验证基准，开展响应层面、模型层面、情境模型和边界条件验证。

Result: 在具备丰富受访者与工作特征信息时，AI 情境测量能够恢复观测调查变量中相当一部分情境模型信息。其中每周工作时长表现最好，AI 测量能够复现 CFPS 中工作时长与满意度之间显著的职业间和职业内负向关联。

Conclusion: AICOME 适合从信息丰富的既有数据中恢复少量、理论上重要的缺失构念；但当输入信息仅限于职业和基础人口学变量，或多个相关概念同时缺失时，测量恢复能力明显下降。

Abstract: Researchers increasingly use artificial intelligence to construct measures of social, organizational, and occupational characteristics that are absent from conventional surveys. We propose AICOME, AI COntextual MEasurement, a framework for evaluating whether AI-derived respondent-level measures can recover individual and group-level effects in contextual models. The key idea is that an AI measure constructed at the respondent level can be used to derive its group-level aggregate and its individual deviation, allowing researchers to estimate both between-group and within-group associations rather than treating AI measurement as response prediction alone.
  We validate the framework using the 2022 China Family Panel Studies (CFPS), where occupations provide the empirical grouping structure and several job-related survey variables provide validation benchmarks. For computer use, foreign-language use, weekly hours, and management responsibilities, we compare survey measures with AI-derived measures in response-level, model-level, contextual, and boundary-condition validations. The results show that AI contextual measurement can recover much of the contextual-model information contained in observed survey variables when rich respondent and job characteristics are available. Weekly hours provides the strongest validation case, with AI-derived measures reproducing the large negative between- and within-occupation associations with satisfaction observed in CFPS. The framework also identifies clear boundary conditions: performance deteriorates when information is restricted to occupation and basic demographics, and recovery is weaker when several related concepts are treated as simultaneously unobserved. The findings suggest that AICOME is most useful for recovering a limited number of theoretically important constructs from rich existing datasets.

</details>


### [225] [Discriminative World Models for Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02885)
*Kelvin Li,Dhruv Pendharkar,Anish Pahilajani,Chuyi Shang,Leon Oks,Leonid Karlinsky,Rogerio Feris,Trevor Darrell,Roei Herzig*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 predicted-state matching 训练目标，使网页智能体的世界模型生成更能区分候选动作结果的预测状态，从而提升测试时动作选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网页世界模型多用监督式下一状态预测生成 HTML/AXTree 等固定表示，但该目标与下游排序器或过程奖励模型的需求不一致；排序器需要预测状态在不同候选动作之间具有判别性。

Method: 作者构建来自 WebArena Go-Browse 轨迹的 branching 数据集，在每个决策点包含多个替代动作及其结果状态；提出 predicted-state matching，让模型预测表示能够从替代动作到达的状态中辨认真实结果状态。

Result: 在 held-out predicted-state matching benchmark 上优于监督式下一状态预测训练的世界模型；在 WebPRMBench 上提升 PRM 风格动作排序，优于 action-only PRM 和结合监督下一状态世界模型的 PRM；在 WebArena-Lite 上提升端到端任务成功率。

Conclusion: 面向下游排序目标对齐的判别式世界模型训练，比单纯追求下一状态复原更适合网页智能体的测试时动作选择，并能带来实际任务成功率提升。

Abstract: Recent web agents use world models for test-time action selection by sampling candidate actions, predicting the resulting web states, and ranking them with a ranker model or a Process Reward Model (PRM). These world models are typically trained via supervised next-state prediction to generate fixed representations like HTML or AXTree snapshots. However, this objective is misaligned with the downstream ranker, which relies on predicted states being discriminative across candidates to accurately score them. To address this, we introduce predicted-state matching, a training objective where the predicted representation must distinguish the true resulting state from those reached by alternative actions. We train these models using a branching web-agent dataset derived from WebArena Go-Browse trajectories, where every decision point contains multiple alternative actions and their resulting states. Experiments on our held-out predicted-state matching benchmark show that our approach outperforms world models trained with supervised next-state prediction. We further show that our approach improves PRM-style action ranking on WebPRMBench compared with action-only PRMs and PRMs augmented with supervised-next-state world models. Finally, on WebArena-Lite, using our world model for test-time action selection improves end-to-end task success. Our project page is available at: https://dhruvpendharkar.github.io/dwm/.

</details>


### [226] [The Memory Trust Gap: Capability-Dependent Failures in Persistent-Memory Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01852)
*Jundong Hu,Shekar Ramachandran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究持久化记忆中的过时事实何时会压倒当前权威证据，并发现这种“记忆信任缺口”随模型能力/规模呈现明显的能力门控与交互效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化智能体依赖长期记忆，但存储事实可能过时；当模型面对过时记忆与当前权威工具/证据冲突时，可能无警告地信任错误记忆。作者希望刻画这种伤害随模型能力变化从何时开始、由哪些特征触发，以及哪些缓解策略有效。

Method: 构建冻结的闭集动作评分基准，包含 Benefit 与 Safety 两个套件，分别对应“无记忆”在任务中不可解与权威工具始终给出正确值两种设定；在 Qwen3 0.6/1.7/4/8B 同家族规模序列上评估，并用 2×2×2×2 因子实验分析标签、时间新近性、来源权威、位置等因素；通过跨规模对比检验尺度交互，并在 Llama-Instruct 系列及 RGB、MisBench 外部数据集上验证；同时测试元数据暴露和预先解决冲突等缓解方法。

Result: Benefit 套件中各规模模型几乎总是使用存储值，过时值回答率为 0.92–1.00。Safety 套件中，在陷阱条件下低于无记忆基线的伤害 Δmem 呈能力门控：较大模型在过时注记被伪装成当前信息时崩溃最严重。去除标签在所有规模上增强过度信任；新近性特征对大模型欺骗更强；来源权威效应弱且与规模无关；位置效应随规模由正转负。元数据暴露只显著帮助能力较强模型，而预先解决冲突才能恢复两个较小模型的准确率。外部模型与数据集呈现相同模式。

Conclusion: 持久化记忆风险并非简单的模型混淆，而是对记忆的过度信任；其触发机制和缓解方式依赖模型能力。随着模型变强，模型更会利用某些表面特征如时间新近性，从而在过时记忆被包装得更可信时产生更大安全退化。因此，记忆系统需要显式冲突解析、可靠元数据和按能力分层的防护机制。

Abstract: Persistent memory supports personalized agents, but a stale stored fact can override current authoritative evidence without warning. We study when this harm begins as model capability changes. We evaluate a frozen, closed-set, action-scored benchmark with 2 suites that represent 2 different meanings of "no memory" (a Benefit suite, unsolvable without the stored fact, and a Safety suite, in which an authoritative tool always holds the correct value), on a same-family model-size series (Qwen3 0.6/1.7/4/8B). The Memory Trust Gap reflects over-trust rather than confusion. In the Benefit suite, models answer with the stale value 0.92-1.00 of the time at every scale. In the Safety suite, harm below the no-memory baseline under the trap conditions ($Δ_{\mathrm{mem}}$) is capability-gated, with the larger models collapsing most once a stale note is made to look current. In a $2\times2\times2\times2$ factorial, which feature triggers over-trust depends on both the feature and model scale. Removing a label amplifies over-trust at every size, and a recency feature (stale dated newer) fools the larger models harder. Source authority is weak and scale-flat, and position changes from positive to negative across the Qwen3 model-size series. We confirm these scale interactions with direct cross-size contrast tests rather than overlapping per-model intervals. Mitigation is likewise capability-dependent: exposing metadata improves accuracy for the capable models, but only pre-resolving the conflict restores accuracy for the 2 smaller checkpoints. The same pattern appears on the capable models in an independent Llama-Instruct model-size series and on 2 external datasets (RGB, MisBench). A framing control finds no consistent advantage for the memory label: at the 3 smaller scales, models trust a stale document more than a stale memory; at 8B, the difference is not significant.

</details>


### [227] [EmoStance: Response-Side Affective-Orientation Control for Empathetic Response Generation via Emoji Weak Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02133)
*Ziyuan Jin,Yuxuan Ge,Zheng Tian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 EmoStance，将共情回复生成建模为“回复侧情感取向控制”问题，利用多标注者 emoji 分布作为弱监督信号学习潜在控制空间，并通过连续前缀嵌入引导冻结的指令微调大模型生成更具情境性和回应性的回复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共情对话生成不仅要决定回复内容，还要决定如何回应前一说话者的情绪处境。现有方法往往将情绪标签作为显式输出或金标准标签，难以细粒度刻画倾听者立场与态度，因此需要一种能表示回复侧情感/态度取向的可控生成机制。

Method: 作者构建 EmojiDialogue 数据集，在 EmpatheticDialogues 基础上增加话语级 emoji 投票与置信分数；提出 EmoStance：建模源侧情感表达，从对话上下文和说话者角色预测软性的回复侧情感取向，并将该取向映射为连续 prefix embeddings，用于控制冻结的 instruction-tuned LLM。

Result: 在包含 20 名标注者、800 次判断的盲式成对评测中，EmoStance 获得 62.2% 的决定性胜率，尤其在上下文具体性和感知回应性方面提升明显，并且可与外部知识增强方法互补。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，多标注者 emoji 分布可作为有效的弱情感—态度证据，用于学习潜在的倾听者立场控制空间；连续前缀控制能够在不微调大模型主体的情况下提升共情回复质量。

Abstract: Empathetic response generation requires models to decide not only what to say, but also how to respond to the previous speaker's affective situation. We formulate this as response-side affective-orientation control and use multi-annotator emoji distributions as weak affective--attitudinal evidence, rather than as output symbols or gold labels, to induce a latent control space that operationally approximates listener stance. We construct EmojiDialogue, an utterance-level extension of EmpatheticDialogues with emoji votes and confidence scores, and propose EmoStance, which models source-side affective expression, predicts a soft response-side orientation from dialogue context and speaker roles, and steers a frozen instruction-tuned LLM through continuous prefix embeddings. In blind pairwise evaluation with 20 annotators and 800 judgments, EmoStance achieves a 62.2% decisive win rate, with the clearest gains in contextual specificity and perceived responsiveness, while remaining complementary to external-knowledge methods. Code, annotation metadata, and reconstruction scripts are available in our GitHub repository: https://github.com/18277390221/EmoStance.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [228] [Solomonoff Induction and Singular Integrals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01666)
*Will Troiani,Daniel Murfet*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文证明 Solomonoff 先验分布中可重组出近似任意可计算贝叶斯模型证据的黎曼和，从而把算法概率与奇异学习理论中的几何不变量联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Solomonoff 分布通常被视为离散的程序复杂度概念，而贝叶斯证据是连续参数空间上的积分；论文旨在说明二者并非割裂，离散程序和的结构可以包含连续统计模型的证据积分。

Method: 作者对 Solomonoff 分布的程序求和进行适当重组，构造单个单调图灵机，使其诱导的半测度在统一乘法常数范围内等价于任意可计算贝叶斯模型的证据 Z_n；并结合 Watanabe 的自由能渐近理论分析代码长度上界。

Result: 若模型满足 Watanabe 自由能渐近条件，则对来自真实分布的 i.i.d. 样本 X^n，有 -log M(X^n) ≤ nL_n(w_0)+λ log n-(m-1)log log n+O_P(1)，其中 λ 为学习系数，m 为其重数。

Conclusion: 论文表明奇异学习理论中的学习系数这一代数几何意义上的模型简单性度量，会作为 log n 项系数出现在 Solomonoff 分布对应代码长度的上界中，建立了算法信息论与贝叶斯奇异模型复杂度之间的桥梁。

Abstract: The Solomonoff distribution $M$ assigns an a priori probability to a finite binary string $z$ by summing over all programs whose output begins with $z$, weighting a program of length $\ell$ by $2^{-\ell}$. Thus, likely strings are those with many short explanations. This appears to be a purely discrete notion of complexity. However, Riemann sums are also countable. We show that the sum defining $M$, suitably reorganised, contains Riemann sums approximating the Bayesian evidence of any computable statistical model. These evidence integrals are singular integrals whose asymptotics are governed by Singular Learning Theory and, through it, by invariants of algebraic geometry. Concretely, for every computable Bayesian model, we construct a single monotone Turing machine whose induced semimeasure agrees with the evidence $Z_n$ up to a uniform multiplicative constant. If the model also satisfies the hypotheses of Watanabe's free-energy asymptotics, then a sample $X^n=X_1\cdots X_n$ drawn i.i.d. from the true distribution satisfies $-\log M(X^n) \le nL_n(w_0)+λ\log n-(m-1)\log\log n+O_{\mathbb{P}}(1)$, where $L_n$ is the empirical loss, $w_0$ is an optimal parameter, $λ$ is the learning coefficient, and $m$ is its multiplicity. Thus the learning coefficient, a geometric measure of model simplicity, appears within the Solomonoff distribution as the coefficient of $\log n$ in an upper bound on code length.

</details>


### [229] [CRB-Guided Sensing and Resource Allocation for Human Pose Prediction in Integrated Sensing, Communication, and Computation Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01908)
*Zhonghao Liu,Yahao Ding,Jiaxiang Wang,Zhaohui Yang,Abdol Hamid Aghvami,Mohammad Shikh-Bahaei*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出面向室内以人为中心应用的ISCC资源协同优化框架，通过CRB引导的感知不确定性建模和自适应深度姿态预测，在资源受限条件下降低短期人体姿态预测误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在集成感知、通信与计算（ISCC）系统中，短期人体姿态预测可支持连续跟踪和主动资源分配；但实际系统受感知信噪比、计算资源和通信/波束资源限制影响，预测误差与资源配置之间存在耦合关系，需要联合优化。

Method: 首先设计ET-Mamba姿态预测模型，用于预测人体关节点位置；随后在不同推理层附加轻量级预测头，实现自适应推理深度。接着提出CRB引导的扰动策略，将不同感知SNR下的感知不确定性转化为点云扰动，并建立预测误差、感知SNR与推理深度之间的经验关系。最后构建联合优化波束成形矩阵、模型推理深度和计算频率的资源分配问题，并采用基于交替优化的算法，结合闭式更新与半定规划求解混合整数非凸问题。

Result: 仿真结果表明，在资源受限条件下，所提方法可将姿态预测性能最高提升约35%，优于未进行联合感知、通信和计算设计的方案。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了在ISCC系统中联合考虑感知质量、通信波束、模型推理深度和计算频率的重要性；CRB引导的不确定性建模与自适应深度推理能够有效提升资源受限场景下的人体姿态预测精度。

Abstract: Integrated sensing, communication, and computation (ISCC) provides a promising framework for indoor human-centric applications. In these applications, short-term human pose prediction facilitates continuous human pose tracking and proactive resource allocation. This paper proposes a Cramer-Rao bound (CRB)-guided sensing framework and investigates a problem of minimizing prediction error in resource-constrained ISCC systems. Specifically, a pose prediction model (ET-Mamba) is first developed to predict human joint positions for continuous tracking. To account for computation-resource limitations, lightweight prediction heads are attached to different inference layers, enabling adaptive-depth pose prediction. A CRB-guided perturbation strategy is then introduced to translate sensing uncertainty at different sensing SNR levels into point-cloud perturbations. Based on that, an empirical relationship among pose prediction error, sensing SNR, and model inference depth is established. Furthermore, to improve prediction accuracy under limited resources, this paper formulates a resource allocation optimization problem that minimizes the pose prediction error by jointly optimizing the beamforming matrix, model inference depth, and computation frequency. To solve this mixed-integer non-convex optimization problem, we propose an alternating optimization (AO)-based algorithm, where closed-form updates and semidefinite programming (SDP) are integrated into the iterative solution process. Simulation results show that the proposed method effectively improves pose prediction performance by up to 35 percent under resource constraints, verifying the effectiveness of conducting joint sensing, communication, and computation design in ISCC systems.

</details>


### [230] [New binary optimal LCD codes using heuristic embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02096)
*Haeun Lim,Junmin An,Jon-Lark Kim*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出基于贪心启发式搜索的短 LCD 嵌入构造方法，并发现了 14 个新的二元最优 LCD 码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二元 LCD 码在编码理论中具有重要意义，但构造更短长度、更优参数的 LCD 码仍具有挑战。作者希望通过短 LCD 嵌入扩大可搜索的构造空间，以获得新的最优 LCD 码。

Method: 设计基于贪心算法的启发式框架。利用“可逆矩阵与任意矩阵组合可产生 LCD 嵌入”的性质，将可逆块上的初等行变换和任意块上的单元素翻转作为局部搜索移动，在 LCD 嵌入空间中迭代搜索。

Result: 该方法在长度 55 到 201、维数 7 和 8 的范围内找到了 14 个新的二元最优 LCD 码。

Conclusion: 短 LCD 嵌入结合贪心局部搜索是一种有效的二元最优 LCD 码构造策略，能够发现此前未知的优参数码。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the construction of binary optimal LCD codes through short LCD embeddings. For this purpose, we design heuristic frameworks based on a greedy algorithm. We explore the search spaces of LCD embeddings using the fact that an invertible matrix together with an arbitrary matrix yields an LCD embedding. We therefore use elementary row operations on the invertible block and single entry-flips on the arbitrary block as local moves in a greedy algorithm. Using this method, we have found $14$ optimal new LCD codes with dimensions 7 and 8 for lengths from 55 to 201.

</details>


### [231] [Instance Optimal Sparse Recovery from Nonlinear Observations: A Unified Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02120)
*Junren Chen,Arian Maleki*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出一个用于非线性观测下稀疏恢复的统一分析框架，通过信号依赖的受限近似可逆性条件（RAIC）证明迭代硬阈值类算法的实例最优性，并应用于稀疏相位恢复、one-bit压缩感知和稀疏ReLU回归。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性观测模型中的稀疏恢复问题广泛存在，但不同问题通常需要分别设计分析工具；同时，已有实例最优性结果往往依赖计算不可行方法，或对高效算法的理论保证不足。因此，论文希望建立统一框架，为高效迭代算法提供实例最优恢复保证。

Method: 论文引入某类梯度的信号依赖受限近似可逆性条件（RAIC），并证明该条件可推出迭代硬阈值算法的实例最优性。在高斯设计下，将该框架分别用于无相位测量、one-bit测量和ReLU测量；技术上依赖高维集中不等式、受限特征值界以及新的实例依赖超平面镶嵌结果。

Result: 在稀疏相位恢复中，提出阈值化幅度流的变体，并在约 O(s^3) 次测量（忽略对数因子）下证明其实例最优性；在one-bit压缩感知中，证明归一化二值迭代硬阈值的实例最优性并强化近期结果；在稀疏ReLU回归中，证明Soltanolkotabi (2017)算法的轻微变体具有实例最优性。此外，还获得了这些问题的非一致 $(\ell_2,\ell_2)$ 实例最优保证。

Conclusion: 该工作为多类非线性稀疏恢复问题提供了统一且可计算的实例最优性理论，尤其首次给出了稀疏相位恢复中高效算法的实例最优保证，并通过新的高维概率工具扩展了非线性观测模型的理论基础。

Abstract: This paper develops a unified framework for instance optimal sparse recovery from nonlinear observations. The main ingredient is a signal-dependent restricted approximate invertibility condition (RAIC) of some gradient, which leads to the instance optimality of iterative hard thresholding. Under Gaussian designs, we apply the proposed framework to phaseless, one-bit, and ReLU measurements, which correspond to the problems of sparse phase retrieval, one-bit compressed sensing, and sparse ReLU regression, respectively. For sparse phase retrieval, we propose a variant of thresholded amplitude flow and show its instance optimality under $O(s^3)$ measurements (up to logarithmic factors), where $s$ is the sparsity level. To our best knowledge, this is the first instance optimal efficient algorithm for sparse phase retrieval and complements Gao, Wang and Xu (2016) that achieved this via a computationally intractable program. In one-bit compressed sensing, we establish the instance optimality of normalized binary iterative hard thresholding and strengthen the recent result of Matsumoto and Mazumdar (2024). In sparse ReLU regression, it is shown that a slight variant of the algorithm in Soltanolkotabi (2017) is instance optimal. Moreover, $(\ell_2,\ell_2)$ non-uniform instance optimal guarantees are obtained for these problems. The analysis is built upon a number of high-dimensional concentration bounds, including bounds on restricted eigenvalues and a novel instance-dependent hyperplane tessellation result.

</details>


### [232] [Constraint-Preserving Genetic Algorithms for Embedding Linear Codes into Self-Orthogonal Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02135)
*Haeun Lim,Junmin An,Jon-Lark Kim*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于遗传算法的启发式框架，用于通过最短自正交嵌入构造二元最优自正交码，并获得大量新的最优或当前最佳码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二元自正交码的构造需要在满足严格正交约束的同时优化最小距离等性能指标；传统搜索方法在最短自正交嵌入空间中效率有限，因此需要更有效的启发式搜索策略。

Method: 作者设计了遗传算法框架，以最小距离和最小重量码字数量为适应度指标；构造保持约束的交叉与变异算子，确保每个染色体对应有效自正交嵌入，并使优良的正交生成元子序列等结构特征在代际间传播；同时分析时间和存储复杂度，并通过消融实验与随机搜索对比验证方法。

Result: 该方法构造出66个达到上界的新的二元最优自正交码，并得到135个达到目前已知最佳最小距离的自正交码。

Conclusion: 基于遗传算法的约束保持最短自正交嵌入搜索能够有效探索自正交码构造空间，相比随机搜索更具针对性和效率，为发现新的最优或近最优二元自正交码提供了实用方法。

Abstract: In this paper, we aim to construct binary optimal self-orthogonal codes using shortest self-orthogonal embedding methods. For this purpose, we design a heuristic framework based on a genetic algorithm. We explore the search space of shortest self-orthogonal embeddings using a fitness function based on the minimum distance and the number of minimum-weight codewords. We construct \emph{constraint-preserving} crossover and mutation operations so that every chromosome yields a valid self-orthogonal embedding, while high-fitness structural features, such as favorable subsequences of orthogonal generators, are propagated across generations. We also analyze the time and storage complexity of the algorithm, and validate our design through an ablation study on guided crossover and a comparison with random search under an equal time budget. Using this method, we obtain $66$ new binary optimal self-orthogonal codes that meet the upper bound, together with $135$ further self-orthogonal codes attaining the best minimum distance found so far.

</details>


### [233] [Agentic UE-CoMIMO for 6G Terminals: From Virtual Antenna Augmentation to AI-Native Virtualization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02290)
*Chao-Kai Wen,Yen-Cheng Chan,Lung-Sheng Tsai,Pei-Kai Liao,Geoffrey Ye Li*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出 Agentic UE-CoMIMO：通过多层智能体控制附近终端协作，动态选择通信、感知、计算与语义信息交换机制，以提升任务连续性与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单个用户设备受天线、算力、链路与能耗限制，传统 UE-CoMIMO 主要关注通信增强，难以在多设备、多任务、动态环境中理解用户意图并持续重规划协作策略。

Method: 构建由设备微智能体、智能手机或 CPE 枢纽智能体、边缘/网络智能体组成的控制层，协调设备参与、 relay 模式、流量拆分与复制、计算卸载位置、语义 token 交换及拓扑重构；并在创作者直播与可穿戴协同盲区感知两个系统级场景中与能力匹配的自适应基线比较。

Result: 实验/场景研究表明，所提智能体控制可通过提前预测变化并准备协作与回退动作，使高质量直播维持更长时间，并在设备失效时保持盲区告警能力。

Conclusion: Agentic UE-CoMIMO 将 UE 协作从单一通信增强扩展为面向任务的跨通信、感知、计算与语义交换协同框架，但仍需解决标准化、互操作性、信任与验证等挑战。

Abstract: End-user-centric collaborative MIMO (UE-CoMIMO) lets nearby devices form a virtual multi-antenna terminal to overcome the antenna limitations of individual user equipment. Extending such cooperation to communication, sensing, computing, and task-relevant information exchange requires a control layer that can interpret user intent, select cooperation mechanisms, and replan as conditions change. This article introduces Agentic UE-CoMIMO, in which device micro-agents, a smartphone or CPE hub agent, and edge/network agents coordinate device participation, relay modes, traffic splitting and duplication, compute placement, semantic-token exchange, and topology reconfiguration. Two system-level scenario studies on creator-centric live streaming and wearable-collaborative blind-spot sensing compare the proposed controller with capability-matched adaptive baselines. The results show that, by anticipating changes and preparing cooperation and fallback actions in advance, agentic control sustains high-quality streaming for longer and maintains blind-spot warnings through device outages. We also discuss the associated standardization, interoperability, trust, and validation challenges.
